Abstract: This paper investigates the dynamic channel estimation algorithm in mmWave movable antenna (MA) multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems. To achieve highly accurate channel estimation, we propose a tensor decomposition-based channel estimation algorithm. First, by leveraging the path response model and utilizing the intrinsic sparsity of mmWave channels, the channel corresponding to MA pairs at the base station and mobile station is transformed into a superposition of channels from sparse paths. Next, the received signal is constructed as a fourth-order tensor to fully capture the high-dimensional structural information of the MA MIMO channel. Then, two tensor decomposition schemes are adopted to extract the factor matrices, and our analysis reveals that the uniqueness of the decomposition can be guaranteed in our model. Subsequently, the propagation loss, frequency offset, angle of arrival/departure, and time delay are obtained based on these factor matrices and the channel matrix can be rebuilt. Additionally, Cramér-Rao bound (CRB) is also derived as a performance evaluation standard, proving that the proposed algorithm achieves a higher estimation accuracy and nearly approaches this minimum bound. Moreover, normalized mean square error (NMSE) is selected as the evaluation metrics for estimation accuracy. Finally, simulation results reveal a notable reduction in the estimation error of the proposed algorithm when compared to the baseline algorithms, confirming its estimation advantage.

</details>


### [100] [Waveform Design for OTFS Assisted Simultaneous Acoustic Information and Power Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17631)
*Jinheng Kang,Yizhe Zhao,Kun Yang,Jun Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于OTFS的水下声学信息与功率同时传输(SAIPT)波形设计，用于动态水声信道中的自供能物联网。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是水下声学通信与能量传输(SAIPT)，涉及OFDM/OTFS波形设计、水声信道、水下物联网(IoUT)，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 基于OTFS(正交时频空间)的波形设计，联合设计AIT(声学信息传输)和APT(声学功率传输)符号。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于水下声学通信与能量传输领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化研究方向无任何交集。

Abstract: Simultaneous acoustic information and power transfer (SAIPT) is a promising technique for supporting self-sustainable Internet of Underwater Things (IoUT) networks through concurrent data transmission and energy supplement. However, existing OFDM-based SAIPT studies are vulnerable to severe multipath propagation and Doppler effects in dynamic underwater acoustic channels. To address this issue, this paper proposes an orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS)-based SAIPT waveform design for dynamic underwater acoustic channels. The acoustic information transfer (AIT) and acoustic power transfer (APT) symbols are jointly designed, while the transducer conversion efficiencies and nonlinear rectifier characteristics are incorporated into the system model. Based on the derived achievable data rate and DC output expressions, a waveform optimization problem is formulated to maximize the harvested DC output under transmit power and minimum data-rate constraints. To solve the resulting non-convex problem, a successive convex approximation (SCA)-based algorithm is developed. Simulation results show that the proposed OTFS-based design outperforms the OFDM-based scheme in terms of DC output in the dynamic transmission scenarios. The effects of key system parameters are also analyzed, confirming the effectiveness of the proposed design in improving acoustic energy transfer efficiency.

</details>


### [101] [DMSNet: Cross-Band Learning for Multi-Target Sensing in Multi-Band ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17655)
*Haotian Liu,Zhiqing Wei,Quanjiang Zhao,Lin Wang,Yunxin Geng,Xingwang Li,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种双频多目标感知神经网络（DMSNet），用于ISAC场景下的联合目标数量与参数估计，在仿真数据上提升了精度并降低了计算开销。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于双频集成感知与通信（ISAC）的多目标感知，属于通信/雷达感知领域，不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心场景，也不涉及目标检测中的旋转框、小目标、舰船/飞机/车辆等遥感典型任务，更未涉及星上部署或轻量化模型压缩方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多目标数量估计与参数（距离、速度、角度）估计；应用场景：双频ISAC感知；传感器类型：通信/雷达双频段信号；目标类别：未说明具体目标类别，为通用多目标。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: DMSNet（Dual-Band Multi-Target Sensing Neural Network），摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明使用了剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。仅提到相比现有方法减少了68.7%的运行时间，但这属于算法效率提升而非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署关键指标。仅报告了runtime减少68.7%，但未说明具体硬件平台和延迟指标，无法判断星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：代表性仿真条件（simulation conditions），未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标：目标数量估计的准确率（count accuracy 89.01%→91.74%）、Macro-F1（90.80%→93.07%）；参数估计的中位数绝对误差（range/velocity/angle的MAE分别降低82.2%/56.9%/73.2%）；运行时间减少68.7%。

Experiments: 在仿真条件下，DMSNet在目标数量估计和参数估计上均优于最佳基线方法，并显著降低了运行时间。摘要未说明对比方法的具体名称和消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 仅在仿真条件下验证，缺乏真实遥感/卫星数据实验；2. 未涉及遥感目标检测任务（如旋转框检测、小目标检测）；3. 未提供模型参数量、FLOPs等轻量化关键指标；4. 未说明是否可在边缘/星上硬件部署；5. 与遥感在轨智能场景无直接关联。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可参考的点是：DMSNet通过双频信息融合提升了感知精度并降低了计算开销，这种多模态/多频段信息融合的思路或许可迁移到遥感多光谱/高光谱与SAR融合检测中，但论文本身未涉及轻量化模型设计方法。

Abstract: Multi-band integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) offers complementary high- and low-frequency echo information for multi-target sensing. However, existing dual-band ISAC sensing methods have a limited ability to exploit deep complementary information across heterogeneous bands and often incur high computational costs. To address these limitations, we propose a Dual-Band Multi-Target Sensing Neural Network (DMSNet) for joint target number and parameter estimation. Under representative simulation conditions, DMSNet outperforms the best baseline in target number estimation, increasing count accuracy from 89.01 % to 91.74 % and Macro-F1 from 90.80 % to 93.07 %. For parameter estimation, compared with the best baselines, DMSNet reduces the median absolute errors of range, velocity, and angle by 82.2%, 56.9%, and 73.2%, respectively. Moreover, DMSNet reduces runtime by 68.7 % relative to the fastest existing dual-band ISAC sensing method.

</details>


### [102] [Ambiguity Function Analysis of Pilot-Embedded Random OFDM Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17663)
*Jialin Wu,Fan Liu,Ying Zhang,Yifeng Xiong,Jie Yang,Shi Jin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究OFDM波形在通感一体化(ISAC)中的统计模糊函数，推导了导频嵌入信号的DP-AF和FST-AF解析表达式，并分析了导频模式对模糊函数的影响。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是通信领域的通感一体化(ISAC)波形设计与模糊函数分析，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星在轨处理、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Abstract: This paper investigates the statistical ambiguity functions (AFs) of orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) waveforms that incorporate deterministic unit-modulus pilot symbols and random data payloads for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). We derive analytical expressions for the mean squared discrete periodic ambiguity function (DP-AF) and fast-slow-time ambiguity function (FST-AF) of such pilot-embedded OFDM signals. Our analysis demonstrates that, under a fixed signal length and constellation scheme, the mean squared DP-AF depends jointly on the pilot patterns, pilot symbols and number of pilots, while the mean squared FST-AF relies only on the number of pilots. Numerical simulations closely match the theoretical expressions. Furthermore, in numerical results, we show that different pilot patterns correspond to DP-AF with distinct characteristics, offering relevant considerations for pilot design in communication-centric ISAC systems.

</details>


### [103] [On the Detection Optimality and Exact BER Analysis of NOMA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17755)
*Tasneem Assaf,Hamad Yahya,Arafat Al-Dweik*

TL;DR: 该论文研究NOMA通信系统中的多用户检测与误码率优化，提出最优逐用户最大后验检测器，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化无关。

Abstract: Non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) detection using successive interference cancellation (SIC) and joint multiuser maximum likelihood (JML) has been extensively studied, although their presumed optimality for individual-user bit error rate (BER) minimization is unestablished. Moreover, existing BER analyses overlook the SIC-induced statistical changes. This letter derives optimal per-user maximum a posteriori (MAP) detectors, identifies when the near-user detector reduces to conventional SIC, and proves the equivalence of conventional SIC and JML. An exact average BER analysis accounting for SIC-induced statistical changes is presented. The results show that the proposed detector achieves modest but consistent BER gains at low-to-moderate signal-to-noise ratio (SNR).

</details>


### [104] [Deep Recurrent Q-Learning Based Beam Steering Strategy for Throughput Maximization in WPCNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17838)
*Samannaya Adhikari,Navchetan Awasthi,Siddhartha Sarma*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于深度强化学习（ADRQN）的波束控制策略，用于优化无线供能通信网络中能量采集设备的随机接入协议，不涉及遥感或目标检测。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是无线供能通信网络（wireless powered communication networks）中的MAC协议和波束控制策略，属于无线通信领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：无线供能通信网络的介质访问控制（MAC）协议优化；场景：能量采集设备（energy harvesting devices）的harvest-then-transmit策略；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于action-specific deep recurrent Q-Network (ADRQN)的深度强化学习框架，用于学习波束控制策略。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注的是通信协议层面的能效优化，而非模型在边缘/星上的部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：未使用遥感或视觉数据集，基于仿真实验；评价指标：吞吐量（throughput），与oracle policy对比的性能百分比。

Experiments: 数值实验表明：相比非学习方案吞吐量提升最高68%，达到oracle policy性能的75-80%，且无需信道估计、充电水平报告或设备状态跟踪。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。论文未涉及任何视觉检测任务、遥感图像处理或模型压缩技术。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接联系是：深度强化学习（DRL）在资源受限场景下的决策优化思路，但与本课题的遥感目标检测模型轻量化方向差异过大。

Abstract: In wireless powered communication networks, medium access control protocols for devices using the harvest-then-transmit strategy must be distributed, low-overhead, and capable of handling irregular and infrequent data transmissions to ensure efficient energy utilisation. However, most existing protocols fail to meet one or more of those requirements, leading to wasted scarce harvested energy. We address this by identifying beam steering as a potential mechanism to regulate the charging rate of energy harvesting devices and thus control their access to the shared wireless medium. After formulating a joint problem of energy beam steering and slotted ALOHA-based random access, we leverage a deep learning framework based on an action-specific deep recurrent Q-Network (ADRQN) to learn a beam-steering policy only from the macro-level ternary slot outcomes, namely, idle, success and collision. Additionally, we design an oracle policy with global knowledge of the network to benchmark our proposed blind adaptive beam-steering approach. The numerical results demonstrate that our approach achieves up to 68\% increase in throughput compared to non-learning schemes, while also reaching 75-80\% of the oracle policy's performance, all without requiring channel estimation, charge-level reporting, or device-state tracking.

</details>


### [105] [Massive MIMO-OFDM ISAC for Sparse ISAR Imaging: Joint Power and Subcarrier Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17862)
*Hamid Reza Hashempour,Yanjiao Li,Jie Zhang,Hyundong Shin,Hien Quoc Ngo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究mMIMO-OFDM框架下的通感一体化ISAR成像，提出自适应重加权ADMM算法用于稀疏孔径ISAR重建，以及基于SAC的资源分配方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。虽然涉及ISAR成像（可视为遥感的一种形式），但核心是通信与感知一体化的信号处理与资源分配问题，不涉及目标检测（object detection），也不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（pruning, quantization, knowledge distillation, NAS等均未出现）。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：ISAR成像重建与通感一体化资源分配；场景：mMIMO-OFDM通信感知一体化系统；传感器类型：ISAR（逆合成孔径雷达）；目标类别：未明确指定。不涉及目标检测（object detection）、小目标检测、旋转框检测等。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的深度学习模型架构。提出的是信号处理算法：自适应重加权二维ADMM算法（用于ISAR图像重建），以及SAC（soft actor-critic）强化学习方法（用于资源分配）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未提及剪枝（pruning）、量化（quantization）、知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上部署（onboard deployment）、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：ISAR重建精度（定性比较）、频谱效率（sum spectral efficiency）。

Experiments: 数值实验表明：提出的自适应ADMM算法在稀疏ISAR重建上优于传统方法；基于SAC的设计在满足通信和感知约束的同时，在全频带基准上获得了频谱效率的显著提升，揭示了ISAR重建精度与通信频谱效率之间的权衡。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及目标检测任务，仅涉及ISAR成像重建；2. 不涉及任何深度学习模型轻量化技术；3. 不涉及星上/边缘部署可行性分析；4. 缺乏在标准遥感目标检测数据集上的实验验证。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能的交叉点是：如果未来在轨遥感目标检测中涉及ISAR成像与通信一体化场景，其稀疏观测下的重建方法（ADMM）可能作为预处理步骤，但与轻量化目标检测模型本身无关。

Abstract: This paper investigates a massive multiple-input multiple-output (mMIMO) orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) framework for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) with inverse synthetic aperture radar (ISAR) imaging, supporting applications such as the Internet of Things (IoT). A dual-function architecture combines communication precoding and dedicated sensing beamforming to enable simultaneous downlink communication and ISAR imaging. Due to intermittent pilot transmission and sparse sensing-subcarrier activation, the received echoes provide incomplete measurements, resulting in a sparse-aperture ISAR reconstruction problem. To address this issue, an adaptive reweighted two-dimensional alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) algorithm is developed for high-resolution image recovery from sparse observations. A joint resource-allocation framework is also proposed to optimize communication-subcarrier assignment, sensing-subcarrier selection, and transmit power allocation subject to communication quality-of-service and sensing constraints. Exploiting channel hardening, analytical full-band sensing benchmarks based solely on statistical channel state information (CSI) are derived for maximum-ratio (MR) and zero-forcing (ZF) precoding, while a soft actor-critic (SAC)-based method is developed for sparse-sensing resource allocation. Numerical results show that the proposed adaptive ADMM algorithm improves sparse ISAR reconstruction over conventional methods. The SAC-based design also achieves substantial gains in sum spectral efficiency over the full-band benchmarks while satisfying communication and sensing constraints, thereby revealing the tradeoff between ISAR reconstruction accuracy and communication spectral efficiency.

</details>


### [106] [Radio Map Updating from Streaming Spectrum Measurements via Memory-Based Online Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17954)
*Yuanyuan Deng,Bo Zhou,Tian Chen,Shijian Gao,Jia Yan,Lantu Guo,Qiuming Zhu,Qihui Wu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于记忆的在线稀疏变分高斯过程（M-OSVGP）方法，用于从流式频谱测量数据中高效更新无线电地图，并引入网格辅助在线诱导点选择（GOIPS）算法以提升后验近似质量。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于无线电地图（radio map）重建，属于无线通信/频谱管理领域，不涉及遥感图像、卫星观测、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然"在线更新"和"计算效率"概念有泛化借鉴意义，但与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，阅读优先级极低。除非对在线高斯过程方法有特殊兴趣，否则不建议投入时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：无线电地图在线重建（radio map reconstruction）；场景：频谱管理、网络规划；传感器类型：频谱测量设备；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。与遥感、卫星、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等均无关。

Model architecture: 基于稀疏变分高斯过程（Sparse Variational Gaussian Process），引入记忆机制（memory-based）进行在线更新。核心模块：M-OSVGP（记忆在线稀疏变分高斯过程）、GOIPS（网格辅助在线诱导点选择）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化方法。但方法本身通过稀疏变分推断（sparse variational inference）和诱导点选择（inducing point selection）降低计算复杂度，具有计算效率优化的思想。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及"computational efficiency"和"maintaining computational efficiency"，但未给出具体数值或硬件部署方案（FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等均未提及）。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：重建精度（reconstruction accuracy）、计算效率（computational efficiency）、不确定性量化（uncertainty quantification）。实验方式：仿真实验（simulations），对比基线包括batch方法和online方法。

Experiments: 通过大量仿真验证了M-OSVGP和GOIPS在重建精度、计算效率和不确定性量化方面优于现有批处理和在线基线方法。摘要未给出具体数值结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 摘要未说明实际硬件部署可行性；3. 仅基于仿真实验，缺乏真实场景验证；4. 未讨论与深度学习目标检测模型的结合可能性。

Ideas for my research: 1. "在线更新+记忆机制缓解灾难性遗忘"的思想可借鉴到星上模型持续学习/增量学习场景，但需结合遥感目标检测任务重新设计；2. "稀疏变分推断降低计算复杂度"的思路与轻量化有概念上的相似性，但方法本身（高斯过程）与主流CNN/Transformer检测器差异较大，直接迁移难度高；3. 整体借鉴价值有限，不建议作为重点参考论文。

Abstract: Radio maps, which estimate spatial radio-frequency characteristics from spectrum measurements, are essential for applications such as spectrum management and network planning. With the continuous arrival of spectrum measurements, conventional batch processing methods for radio map reconstruction become computationally prohibitive, as they require reprocessing all accumulated measurements for each radio map update. To address this, we propose a memory-based online sparse variational Gaussian process (M-OSVGP) method that efficiently updates radio maps from streaming spectrum measurements. Our method employs sparse variational inference and updates the posterior online by minimizing a hybrid objective that integrates newly received measurements and a memory subset of previous ones to mitigate catastrophic forgetting. To further improve posterior approximation as measurements accumulate over spatially diverse regions, we extend M-OSVGP with a grid-assisted online inducing point selection (GOIPS) algorithm. GOIPS dynamically adapts the number and locations of inducing points based on measurement density and spatial correlation, providing a more informative inducing set while maintaining computational efficiency. Extensive simulations demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed methods in reconstruction accuracy, computational efficiency, and uncertainty quantification, compared to existing batch and online baselines across various scenarios.

</details>


### [107] [System-Level Evaluation of LEO Satellite Communications Under Service-Driven Traffic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17993)
*Miyeon Lee,Dong-Hyun Jung,Sucheol Kim,Hwanjin Kim,Junil Choi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文评估了低轨卫星通信在非全缓冲流量模型下的系统级性能，包括吞吐量、资源块分配和时延分析，不涉及遥感目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分。论文主题是LEO卫星通信的系统级性能评估，与遥感、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。虽然涉及卫星（LEO satellite），但属于通信领域，不是遥感成像或在轨智能处理方向。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LEO卫星通信系统性能评估；应用场景：卫星通信网络；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未涉及任何遥感图像或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文使用系统级仿真器（system-level simulator），遵循3GPP TR 38.811和38.821标准，不涉及深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是通信系统性能评估，不涉及星上部署、边缘推理、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：无（使用3GPP标准信道模型和流量模型进行仿真）；评价指标：UE throughput（用户设备吞吐量）、RB allocation ratio（资源块分配比例）、packet delay（数据包时延）。

Experiments: 在3GPP LEO卫星研究案例下，对不同流量模型进行系统级仿真，分析了吞吐量分布、资源消耗模式（随UE密度变化）和时延特性。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何相关借鉴。论文本身局限在于仅关注通信性能，未涉及星上计算或遥感数据处理。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于卫星通信领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: As low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite communications take shape on a global scale, system-level evaluation has become essential for a rigorous understanding of their performance characteristics. Most existing studies have relied on a full-buffer assumption, which obscures the heterogeneity and intermittency that characterize actual user equipment (UE) traffic. This article evaluates the performance of LEO satellite communications under realistic service conditions through the incorporation of non-full-buffer traffic models. To this end, we develop a system-level simulator that complies with the channel modeling and evaluation methodologies specified in 3rd Generation Partnership Project (3GPP) technical reports 38.811 and 38.821. Three key system-level performance metrics are considered: UE throughput, resource block (RB) allocation ratio, and packet delay, which are evaluated across 3GPP LEO satellite study cases under diverse traffic models. The throughput results characterize the distribution of achievable data rates, which delineates the practical operating boundaries of the system. The RB allocation analysis reveals patterns of resource consumption as UE density varies, which provides a quantitative basis for assessing system capability. Furthermore, the delay analysis characterizes latency behavior, which is of particular importance in satellite environments where substantial propagation delays are inherent and must be examined to ensure service feasibility. These evaluations provide a realistic performance outlook for non-full-buffer LEO satellite communications and provide insight into the user experience under practical operating conditions.

</details>


### [108] [Self-Interference-Aware AS-Assisted Tri-Hybrid Beamforming for Full-Duplex Massive MIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18036)
*Yuanzhe Gong,Cheng-Jie Zhao,Tho Le-Ngoc*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于天线选择的三混合波束赋形方案，用于全双工大规模MIMO系统，通过交替优化联合优化天线子集、模拟/数字波束赋形器和功率分配。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是全双工大规模MIMO通信系统中的波束赋形与天线选择问题，属于无线通信领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：全双工大规模MIMO系统中的混合波束赋形与天线选择；应用场景：无线通信基站；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 三混合波束赋形（tri-HBF）架构，基于天线选择的子阵重构，子连接HBF架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及模型轻量化方法。论文中的"轻量化"体现在天线选择（antenna selection）以减少射频链路数量，但这属于通信系统的硬件资源优化，与深度学习模型压缩/加速无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 基于8×8 Tx-8×8 Rx全双工阵列原型的实验自干扰信道数据；评价指标：平均和速率（average sum-rate）、DL/UL速率增益、波束隔离度（beam-level isolation）。

Experiments: 与全阵列激活相比，选择性激活实现21.3%更高的平均和速率；与多种选择基线相比，平均和速率提升45.1%，DL和UL速率增益分别为36.9%和82.9%；波束隔离度优于63 dB。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文属于通信物理层优化问题，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交集。

Abstract: This paper proposes a tri-hybrid beamforming (tri-HBF) scheme with antenna-selection (AS)-based reconfigurable sub-arrays for full-duplex (FD) massive multiple-input multiple-output (mMIMO) systems. A sub-connected HBF architecture is adopted, where AS is performed in a group-wise manner to avoid excessive switch-network and routing complexity. An alternating optimization (AO) algorithm is developed to jointly optimize the i) active antenna subsets considering a self-interference (SI)-aware utility, ii) analog beamformers through projected gradient ascent (PGA), iii) digital precoders/combiners via SI-aware regularized zero-forcing (RZF) and minimum mean-square error (MMSE) updates, and iv) DL/UL power allocation by successive convex approximation (SCA). To capture realistic electromagnetic coupling in FD mMIMO operation, experimental SI channels based on an 8x8 Tx-8x8 Rx FD array prototype are incorporated into the study. The proposed AS-aided tri-HBF optimization scheme exhibits robust convergence across various base station configurations and effectively balances desired-signal enhancement, SI mitigation, and multi-user interference suppression in FD mMIMO operation. Illustrative results show that selective activation can outperform full-array activation, achieving a 21.3% higher average sum-rate and a more consistent performance across user realizations, with power-efficiency benefits by reducing the active paths. A comprehensive study is conducted to characterize how the number of activated antennas affects the achievable rate, user-channel coherence, and SI suppression gain. Compared with various selection baselines, it achieves a 45.1% improvement in average sum-rate, with average DL and UL rate gains of 36.9% and 82.9%, respectively. In addition, beam-level isolation better than 63 dB is achieved, further confirming the effectiveness of the proposed SI-aware design.

</details>

### [109] [Lipschitz Continuity in Deep Learning: A Systematic Review of Theoretical Foundations, Estimation Methods, Regularization Approaches, and Certifiable Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16329)
*Róisín Luo,James McDermott,Colm O'Riordan*

Main category: stat.ML

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。Lipschitz连续性理论可用于理解网络对输入扰动的敏感性，在遥感目标检测中可能间接相关于：(1) 量化后模型的鲁棒性分析——量化引入的扰动是否影响检测精度，可用Lipschitz常数估计；(2) 知识蒸馏中学生模型对教师模型输出的平滑性约束。但这些均为间接关联，且摘要中未提供任何具体方法或实验支撑。

Abstract: Lipschitz continuity is a fundamental property of neural networks that characterizes their sensitivity to input perturbations. It plays a pivotal role in deep learning, governing \textbf{robustness}, \textbf{generalization} and \textbf{optimization dynamics}. Despite its importance, research on Lipschitz continuity is scattered across various domains, lacking a unified perspective. This paper addresses this gap by providing a systematic review of Lipschitz continuity in deep learning. We explore its \textbf{theoretical foundations}, \textbf{estimation methods}, \textbf{regularization approaches}, and \textbf{certifiable robustness}. By reviewing existing research through the lens of Lipschitz continuity, this survey serves as a comprehensive reference for researchers and practitioners seeking a deeper understanding of Lipschitz continuity and its implications in deep learning.

</details>


### [110] [MTSSL: Meta-Thresholding Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16363)
*Shuyang Liu,Ziang Zeng,Ruiqiu Zheng,Jiazheng Wang,Zechen Liu,Wenxi Li,Zhou Yu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种元阈值半监督学习方法(MTSSL)，将SSL中的伪标签阈值τ视为可学习参数并通过微分优化，理论解释了不同τ值可达到相似性能的原因。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是纯半监督学习(SSL)的理论方法研究，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化或边缘部署中的任何一个核心要素。虽然半监督学习可应用于遥感目标检测，但论文本身是通用SSL理论，未提及任何遥感场景或检测任务。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文未指定任何应用场景、传感器类型或目标类别。任务为通用半监督学习。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型结构。核心贡献是将阈值τ作为可学习参数进行优化，不依赖特定网络架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅提及"accuracy curves"作为性能衡量。

Experiments: 摘要称大量实验证明了MTSSL的优越性能，并观察到不同τ值下准确率曲线可完全重叠，但未给出具体数值结果或对比方法。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 未讨论该方法在计算资源受限场景下的适用性；3. 未说明τ作为可学习参数带来的额外计算开销；4. 未在遥感目标检测数据集上验证。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1) 如果未来在遥感半监督目标检测中使用伪标签，可考虑将阈值作为可学习参数而非固定超参；2) 该理论框架说明不同阈值可达到相似性能，意味着在星上部署时对阈值选择可以更宽松，减少调参成本。但整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接帮助很小。

Abstract: A large body of Semi-supervised Learning~(SSL) algorithms encounter the threshold $τ$ to select pseudo-labels. The value of $τ$ across different SSL algorithms can vary depending on the learning perspective, yet they may achieve similar performance. It motivates us to establish a unified theoretical framework to explain the role of $τ$ in SSL. We statistically explained that the unsupervised loss is affected independently by correct and incorrect pseudo-labels, while $τ$ adjusts their numbers to balance the corresponding error term. This inherent trade-off indicates that SSL can reach the same loss with varying $τ$, precise optimal values of $τ$ during training may be unnecessary. With this, we treat $τ$ as an updatable parameter and optimize it via differentiation; the new policy is named \textbf{Meta-Thresholding Semi-Supervised Learning (MTSSL)}. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance of MTSSL. We observe that the accuracy curves of SSL algorithms can overlap completely even when the values of $τ$ differ significantly, which supports our theoretical framework and indicates that the selection of $τ$ can be relaxed in the future design of SSL algorithms.

</details>


### [111] [Backpropagation-Free Trunk Training via the Split Forward Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16612)
*Tian Qin,Wei-Min Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Split Forward Gradient (Split-FG)方法，通过在前向传播中分割网络、精确计算头部梯度并仅用雅可比-向量积估计主干梯度，以减少反向传播的显存占用，但该方法主要面向通用语言模型和图像分类任务。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨部署、目标检测、轻量化均无直接关系。它研究的是通用深度学习中的前向梯度训练方法，旨在减少反向传播的显存开销，应用场景是NLP（WikiText-103）和通用图像分类（CIFAR-10/100），不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或星上部署。虽然其减少显存的目标与轻量化有间接关联，但方法本身（前向梯度估计）并非模型压缩或加速的主流技术，且实验场景与遥感完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言建模（WikiText-103）和通用图像分类（CIFAR-10/100）。应用场景：通用深度学习训练优化。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: GPT-2风格模型（16M参数）用于语言建模；heavy-head设计用于CIFAR分类。网络被分割为trunk（主干）和head（头部），trunk使用前向梯度估计，head使用精确梯度。

Lightweight method: 不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统轻量化方法。该方法通过前向梯度（forward-mode gradient）替代反向传播来减少训练时的显存占用，属于训练优化而非模型压缩或推理加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明推理部署相关信息。仅提及训练时峰值显存相比反向传播减少最多35%（peak memory reduction up to 35%），但未给出参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等部署关键指标。该方法面向训练阶段，而非推理部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：WikiText-103（语言建模）、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100（图像分类）。评价指标：验证集困惑度（validation perplexity）、分类准确率（accuracy）。

Experiments: 在WikiText-103上，16M GPT-2模型达到验证困惑度387（冻结主干基线668，纯前向梯度基线2885，反向传播150）。CIFAR-10上达到60.5%，CIFAR-100上达到35.2%。峰值显存相比反向传播减少最多35%。但性能差距随前向模式主干增长而扩大。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测完全无关；2. 仅针对训练阶段优化，不涉及推理部署；3. 性能与反向传播差距显著（困惑度387 vs 150，CIFAR-10准确率60.5% vs 通常>90%）；4. 性能差距随模型规模增大而扩大，可扩展性存疑；5. 未在目标检测、遥感等复杂视觉任务上验证。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值极低。唯一可参考的点是：该方法通过分割网络减少显存的思想，在星上训练（on-orbit training）场景中可能有启发意义——如果未来需要在卫星上对模型进行微调或持续学习，减少训练显存的方法可能有用。但当前方法性能损失过大，且未在遥感数据上验证，短期内不具备实用价值。

Abstract: Backpropagation makes training deep networks memory intensive because it must store intermediate activations. Forward-mode methods avoid this cost, but their gradient estimates become increasingly noisy as the number of trained parameters grows. We introduce Split Forward Gradient (Split-FG), which splits a network at an intermediate representation: it computes the output head gradient exactly and estimates only the trunk gradient with a Jacobian--vector product. This reduces estimator variance and requires no backward pass through the trunk, while retaining an Adam-style convergence guarantee. Our experiments reveal an important practical failure mode. On WikiText-103, naive forward-gradient training of the trunk performs worse than leaving a randomly initialized trunk frozen, likely because Adam updates every noisy, under-determined trunk coordinate too aggressively. Simply using a much smaller learning rate for the trunk reverses this result: a $16$M-parameter GPT-2-style model reaches validation perplexity $387$, compared with $668$ for the frozen-trunk control and $2{,}885$ for a matched pure forward-gradient baseline (backpropagation reaches $150$). Split-FG also produces the strongest backprop-free results on our tabular benchmarks and reaches $60.5\%$ on CIFAR-10 and $35.2\%$ on CIFAR-100 with a heavy-head design. It reduces peak memory by up to $35\%$ relative to matched backpropagation, although the performance gap widens as the forward-mode trunk grows.

</details>


### [112] [Isotonic Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16675)
*Daniel Bensimon,Sean Xiang Yu,Eric D. Kolaczyk,Archer Y. Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出等渗保形预测（ICP）框架，通过单次等渗重校准映射和分层预测区间构建，在保证自校准和预测条件有效性的同时，大幅降低计算成本。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于统计学习/不确定性量化领域的纯方法论研究，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：不确定性量化（uncertainty quantification）、保形预测（conformal prediction）；应用场景：通用回归问题、医疗健康数据；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。核心方法为等渗回归（isotonic regression）用于重校准映射，以及分层（stratification）策略构建预测区间。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文关注的是计算效率（computational cost）的降低，但这是针对保形预测中校准步骤的优化，而非模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及计算成本（computational cost）相比SC-CP显著降低，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成异方差回归数据（synthetic heteroscedastic regression problems）和一个真实医疗健康利用数据集（healthcare-utilization dataset）；评价指标：覆盖率（coverage）、自校准性（self-calibration）、预测条件有效性（prediction-conditional validity）。

Experiments: 在合成数据和真实医疗数据上，SICP和TICP两种方法均匹配了SC-CP的覆盖率性能，且计算成本显著降低。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关。论文本身的方法论局限包括：SICP仅渐近达到自校准（asymptotically），TICP虽精确但需每个测试点内循环（per-test-point inner loop）。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于不确定性量化领域，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化无交集。如果硬要寻找间接联系，保形预测方法可用于检测模型的不确定性估计，但与本课题核心方向无关。

Abstract: A point prediction that is well calibrated on average can still be systematically biased conditional on its own value, undermining its use in downstream decision-making. We consider two objectives for reliable uncertainty quantification: self-calibration, requiring a point prediction to be unbiased conditional on its own value, and prediction-conditional validity, requiring a prediction interval to attain nominal coverage conditional on the prediction. Self-Calibrating Conformal Prediction (SC-CP) attains both objectives exactly in finite samples, but requires refitting its calibrator for every candidate outcome, which is computationally prohibitive for continuous outcomes. We propose Isotonic Conformal Prediction (ICP), a framework that decouples calibration from prediction-set construction by fitting a single isotonic recalibration map and constructing prediction intervals within strata of similar recalibrated predictions. Within this framework we develop two procedures. Split Isotonic Conformal Prediction (SICP) attains prediction-conditional validity in finite samples and self-calibration asymptotically, at the computational cost of split conformal prediction. Transductive Isotonic Conformal Prediction (TICP) attains both objectives exactly in finite samples through a per-test-point inner loop that avoids refitting the isotonic calibrator. On synthetic heteroscedastic regression problems and a real-world healthcare-utilization dataset, both procedures match the coverage of SC-CP at substantially lower computational cost.

</details>

Abstract: Conditional diffusion models have become a powerful and flexible framework for learning complex conditional distributions from labeled data. In practice, however, acquiring high-quality labels is costly and time-consuming, leaving large volumes of unlabeled data unused. To address this, we introduce label-augmented conditional diffusion (LACD), a simple and effective approach that incorporates unlabeled examples by assigning them a designated trivial label and performing joint denoising score matching over the augmented dataset. We provide sufficient conditions guaranteeing population-level identifiability of the target conditional distribution under this scheme. Moreover, we establish rigorous statistical guarantees: when sufficiently many unlabeled samples are available, the sampling distribution produced by LACD converges strictly faster than the purely supervised estimator in total variation distance, and at least as fast in Wasserstein-1 distance. Extensive experiments on synthetic, image, and tabular benchmarks corroborate our theory and show substantial gains in sample efficiency and generative performance compared with the purely supervised estimator.

</details>

Abstract: This paper proposes a causal independence principle for value -- the value Causal Markov Condition (v-CMC) -- and develops the conceptual and mathematical foundations of a "causal value theory" linking causality and utility. After motivating a local formulation of the v-CMC, we introduce a probability-value duality that translates standard causal-inference results into the value setting. In particular, we formulate local, global, and decomposition versions of the v-CMC and prove their equivalence. We also define v-separation and show that it is sound and complete for conditional value independence. Furthermore, we derive a Bellman-type recursion as a special case of the v-CMC, thereby generalizing standard Bellman recursion from linear chains to causal DAGs. Finally, we show how the v-CMC supports modular transfer and updating of utility information across causal contexts and develop algorithms for causally structured utility elicitation and canonical influence-diagram construction.

</details>


### [115] [Semi-Supervised Conditional Generative Learning through Stochastic Interpolation and Sufficient Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16725)
*Changyu Liu,Yuling Jiao,Jian Huang*

TL;DR: 提出一种半监督条件生成框架RepG，通过将条件依赖学习限制在低维潜空间来缓解标注数据稀缺问题，并给出了理论收敛性分析。

Abstract: Conditional generative modeling remains a challenging problem in semi-supervised settings where labeled data is scarce but unlabeled samples are abundant. To effectively leverage structural information embedded within the unlabeled dataset and compensate for sparse conditioning signals, we propose a semi-supervised framework combining conditional stochastic interpolation with low-dimensional latent representations. RepG decomposes generation into two stages: label-dependent latent sampling and high-dimensional reconstruction. This isolates the supervised learning of conditional dependencies to a low-dimensional space, requiring few labels while utilizing the abundant unlabeled data purely for reconstruction. Theoretically, we establish an error decomposition showing that the Kullback-Leibler divergence of RepG comprises stage-wise estimation errors and a structural bias quantified by conditional mutual information. For deep neural network estimators, we derive non-asymptotic convergence rates proving that RepG significantly improves sample complexity. By confining the supervised estimation burden to the low intrinsic dimension of the latent representation, RepG achieves a strictly faster convergence rate. Complemented by a minimax lower bound, our theoretical results demonstrate that this method effectively mitigates the curse of dimensionality inherent in direct ambient-space generative modeling.

</details>

*Javier Maass,Lénaïc Chizat*

TL;DR: 理论分析论文，证明Dropout与随机梯度掩码(RaM)在深度残差网络的大宽度/深度渐近极限下收敛到相同的动力学行为。

Abstract: Dropout and Random Gradient Masking (RaM) are two training techniques used to improve performance in deep learning. Both techniques inject randomness into the training dynamics, but in significantly different ways: dropout applies random masks to the activations in the forward pass, whereas RaM leaves the forward pass unchanged and instead masks the gradients. In particular, the noise induced by RaM in the parameter updates is unbiased, so standard explanations for the effectiveness of dropout, such as the penalization effect or the prevention of co-adaptation between neurons, do not apply to RaM. In this work, we show that the difference between the two methods disappears for ResNets in the large depth and width asymptotics: in the complete feature learning regime, they both converge to the same large-scale limiting dynamics. This asymptotic equivalence holds for several variants of dropout and RaM, including layerwise dropout as used in stochastic-depth ResNets, albeit at slower quantitative rates. In fact, we also show that several of these variants collapse to the same limit asymptotically.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce Deep Adaptive Bayesian Screening (DABS), a method for performing adaptive factorial screening in high-dimensional discrete design spaces. DABS learns a policy network offline to sequentially select informative experiments, amortizing Bayesian Optimal Experimental Design. It handles binary designs, incorporates sparsity and interactions via a spike-and-slab prior with strong heredity. The model is trained using a contrastive lower bound on information about factor activity with nuisance effect sizes and noise variance analytically integrated out. Unlike prior amortized Bayesian design approaches, DABS also integrates Gibbs posterior inference at deployment, yielding posterior probabilities of factor activity and credible intervals on effect sizes. We demonstrate DABS on screening problems calibrated to real-world benchmarks and show it achieves superior accuracy and scalability over classical and Bayesian baselines under tight experimental budgets.

</details>


### [118] [Twisted Schrödinger Bridge Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16987)
*Maxence Noble,Marie Scheid,Yazid Janati,Eric Moulines,Alain Durmus*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Twisted Schrödinger Bridge Matching (TSBM)，一种基于扩散的广义薛定谔桥匹配方法，通过引入扭曲布朗运动（Feynman-Kac变换）和轨迹级方差缩减技术，改进生成建模中的最优传输动力学估计。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于生成式建模中的广义薛定谔桥问题，属于扩散模型/最优传输的理论方法研究，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。应用场景为人群导航和单细胞数据轨迹推断，与遥感在轨目标检测无任何交集。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。实验在高维设置下进行，包括人群导航和单细胞数据。

Experiments: 在人群导航和单细胞数据的高维轨迹推断任务上展示了TSBM的改进效果，相比DSBM（零势情况）有性能提升。引入的轨迹级方差缩减技术有助于稳定优化。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。论文是纯生成模型/最优传输理论工作，不涉及检测任务、遥感场景或任何部署优化。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。虽然扩散模型在遥感领域有应用（如超分、去噪、生成），但本文聚焦于薛定谔桥的理论扩展，不涉及检测或轻量化，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无参考价值。

Abstract: Over the past few years, diffusion-based Schrödinger bridge models have been proposed to approximate optimal transport dynamics between two prescribed boundary distributions, with successful applications to generative modeling. More precisely, these methods aim to estimate a path measure whose initial and terminal marginals match the two boundary distributions, while minimizing the Kullback-Leibler divergence with respect to a reference Markov process. In this work, we consider the generalized Schrödinger bridge problem, in which the reference process is a twisted Brownian motion, that is, a Feynman-Kac transform of a Brownian motion induced by a time-dependent differentiable potential. Building on the Iterative Markovian Fitting (IMF) paradigm, and in particular on its special case Diffusion Schrödinger Bridge Matching (DSBM), which corresponds to the zero potential case, we introduce Twisted Schrödinger Bridge Matching (TSBM), a diffusion-based method designed to handle both continuous- and discrete-time potentials. Unlike previous approaches, TSBM provides a rigorous extension of the IMF scheme to the generalized Schrödinger bridge problem. This derivation leads to a new bridge-matching loss that depends explicitly on the gradient of the potential and recovers the DSBM objective when the potential vanishes, yielding improved performance. We further introduce trajectory-based variance-reduction techniques that substantially stabilize optimization and may be useful beyond the present setting. Finally, we empirically demonstrate the benefits of TSBM for trajectory inference across increasingly high-dimensional settings, including crowd navigation and single-cell data. Code available at https://github.com/maxencenoble/twisted-sb-matching.

</details>


### [119] [Non-Asymptotic Best Policy Identification Guarantees in Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17201)
*Joseph Lazzaro,Alessio Russo,Aldo Pacchiano*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究在线表格强化学习中的最优策略识别（BPI）问题，为Navigate and Stop算法提供了首个非渐近样本复杂度保证。

Abstract: In this work we study the Best Policy Identification (BPI) problem in online, tabular Reinforcement Learning. This is an active sequential hypothesis testing problem in which the learner's objective is to identify an optimal policy in a Markov Decision Process (MDP) with high confidence, while minimizing the expected sample complexity to do so. We consider an online setting with deterministic rewards, where the agent must strategically navigate through the MDP in order to effectively explore. Previous works in the literature have provided asymptotically optimal methods for BPI, such as the Navigate and Stop (NaS) algorithm and its variants, however existing analysis remains asymptotic. In this work, we fill that gap by providing the first non-asymptotic sample complexity guarantees for NaS, showing that its sample complexity depends not only on the characteristic time, but also on the connectivity of the underlying MDP, the curvature of the optimal characteristic time, and other instance-dependent quantities. We identify these additional attributes and make explicit their contributions to the overall sample complexity.

</details>


### [120] [Kernel Regression with Tensor Trains and Hadamard Overparameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17390)
*Duc Thien Nguyen,Konstantinos Slavakis,Eleftherios Kofidis,Dimitris Pados*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于张量列（TT）和Hadamard过参数化的无训练数据、可解释的非参数核回归框架KReTTaH，用于多维数据插补，在fMRI和动态图数据上验证了有效性。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署等核心主题，属于纯机器学习/张量方法在多维数据插补（imputation）方向的工作。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多维数据插补（multi-way data imputation）；应用场景：fMRI数据插补、动态图边流恢复；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）的核回归框架，回归系数约束在固定秩的张量列（TT）流形上，并通过Hadamard过参数化（Hadamard overparameterization）促进稀疏性和高表示效率。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然涉及张量列（TT）分解这一模型压缩相关技术，但论文目标并非模型轻量化，而是数据插补的精度提升。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。该工作不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：高维fMRI数据、动态图边流数据；评价指标：建模精度（modeling accuracy），具体指标名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 与基于张量（tensor-based）、贝叶斯（Bayesian-based）和神经网络（neural-network-based）的基线方法对比，KReTTaH在建模精度上持续优于这些方法。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景，与在轨目标检测方向无直接关联；2. 未讨论计算效率、推理速度或部署可行性；3. 仅在fMRI和动态图两类非遥感数据上验证，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 1. 张量列（TT）分解作为一种模型压缩手段，理论上可用于轻量化遥感目标检测网络中的全连接层或卷积核，但本文并未朝此方向探索；2. Hadamard过参数化促进稀疏性的思路可能对剪枝方法有启发，但需要大量额外工作才能迁移到遥感检测任务；3. 整体借鉴价值极低，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Kernel regression with tensor trains and Hadamard overparameterization (KReTTaH) is introduced as a training-data-free, interpretable, and nonparametric framework for multi-way data imputation. The imputation problem is reformulated as regression in reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHS), where the tensor regression coefficients are explicitly constrained to lie on fixed-rank tensor-train (TT) manifolds and structured via Hadamard overparameterization to promote sparsity and high representational efficiency. Rather than relying on costly cross-validation, KReTTaH jointly optimizes the TT coefficient tensors and the kernel covariance matrices within a Riemannian product-manifold framework -- the former on fixed-rank TT manifolds, the latter on the manifold of positive-definite matrices -- thereby enabling automated kernel-hyperparameter selection. Numerical tests on two challenging applications -- imputation of high-dimensional functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data and recovery of missing edge flows in dynamic graphs -- demonstrate that KReTTaH consistently outperforms state-of-the-art tensor-, Bayesian-, and neural-network-based baselines in terms of modeling accuracy.

</details>


### [121] [Efficient Sequential Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17409)
*Chia-Yu Hsu,Shubhanshu Shekhar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究利用历史LLM性能数据对新LLM在固定问题集上的能力构建置信序列，并设计主动查询规则以加速置信区间收缩，属于大语言模型评估领域，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM能力评估与置信序列构建；应用场景：大语言模型评测；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的LLM架构。核心方法涉及测试超鞅（test supermartingales）、反向信息投影（RIPr）、testing-by-betting等统计推断方法。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何部署相关指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等）。

Datasets and metrics: 使用合成测试数据集（synthetic testing datasets），具体数据集名称摘要未说明。评价指标为置信序列宽度（CS width）及收缩速率。

Experiments: 在多个合成测试数据集上比较了不同查询规则（包括growth-oriented querying、uniform exploration等）在RIPr-based和testing-by-betting-based两种置信序列构建方法下的表现。有趣发现：最简单的均匀采样（uniform sampling）有时优于更复杂的自适应查询规则。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。论文中的置信序列构建和主动查询策略属于统计推断和顺序实验设计领域，不涉及目标检测、遥感图像处理或模型压缩加速技术。

Abstract: We study the problem of sequentially evaluating a new large language model (LLM) on a fixed question set using historical performance data from prior LLMs. Our goal is to construct a confidence sequence (CS) for the model's capability on this question set and to design active querying rules that shrink the CS width as quickly as possible. For CS construction, we invert a family of test supermartingales and focus on two representative approaches: a reverse information projection (RIPr)-based approach and a testing-by-betting-based approach. We first study these approaches under an oracle setting, and demonstrate the oracle optimality of the RIPr-based construction. We then propose a growth-oriented querying rule that aims to maximize the worst-case one-step expected log-increment over the endpoints of the current CS. In practice, we build these test supermartingales and the querying rule on predictions of question-level correctness learned from historical data. We then analyze the shrinkage behavior of the resulting CSs and identify two key factors that slow the shrinkage rate of CSs: accumulated prediction mismatch and the spikiness of the querying distribution. Finally, motivated by this analysis, we propose several mixture querying rules that combine growth-oriented querying, prediction refinement, and uniform exploration, trying to mitigate the effects that slow the shrinkage rate. We provide experiments comparing different querying rules for the RIPr-based and testing-by-betting-based CSs across several synthetic testing datasets. Interestingly, we observe that the simplest querying rule, uniform sampling, can sometimes outperform more adaptive querying rules for both methods.

</details>


### [122] [An efficient adaptive dimension selection algorithm for multidimensional probit graded response models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17654)
*Yu Zhou,Yincai Tang,Bin Lv,Meng Gao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于累积有序spike-and-slab先验的自适应贝叶斯维度选择框架，用于心理/教育评估中的多维等级响应模型（MGRM），核心是自动确定潜在维度数量。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦心理测量学和教育评估中的统计建模问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：潜在维度选择（dimensionality selection）；应用场景：心理与教育评估中的序数问卷数据分析；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: probit MGRM（多维等级响应模型），基于累积收缩过程（cumulative shrinkage process）和COSS先验（cumulative ordered spike-and-slab prior）的贝叶斯框架。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文中的"shrinkage"是统计意义上的维度收缩，不是模型轻量化中的剪枝/量化/蒸馏等概念。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 模拟数据（simulation studies）和真实心理评估数据（real psychological assessment data）；评价指标：维度恢复（dimension recovery）、参数估计精度（parameter estimation accuracy）、计算效率（computational efficiency）。

Experiments: 与传统的固定维度估计和模型选择方法（如AIC、BIC、交叉验证）对比，所提方法能准确恢复潜在结构，避免重复拟合多个候选维度模型。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于心理测量学/贝叶斯统计领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向无任何交集。

Abstract: Multidimensional graded response models (MGRMs) are widely used for analyzing ordinal questionnaire data in psychological and educational assessments. A central challenge in applying these models is determining the number of latent dimensions. Conventional approaches usually fit multiple fixed-dimensional models and select among them using post-hoc criteria such as AIC, BIC, or cross-validation, which can be computationally demanding and ignore uncertainty in dimensionality during estimation. We develop an adaptive Bayesian dimension selection framework for probit MGRMs. Building on the cumulative shrinkage process, we assign a cumulative ordered spike-and-slab (COSS) prior to the column-specific variances of the item loading matrix. This prior induces increasing shrinkage across latent dimensions, allowing redundant dimensions to be shrunk toward zero while preserving flexibility for active dimensions. Albert--Chib latent response augmentation is used to handle the ordinal probit likelihood, yielding conditionally Gaussian updates for item loadings and latent traits. These updates are combined with Gibbs updates for threshold and shrinkage parameters in an efficient adaptive sampler.
  Simulation studies evaluate the proposed
  method in terms of dimension recovery, parameter
  estimation accuracy, and computational efficiency, with comparisons to conventional fixed-dimensional estimation and model selection procedures. The results show that the proposed approach accurately recovers the latent structure while avoiding repeated model fitting over multiple candidate dimensions. We further illustrate the method using real psychological assessment data, demonstrating its practical utility for uncovering interpretable latent structures in ordinal item responses.

</details>


### [123] [An Adjoint-Sensitivity Framework for Lost-in-the-Middle Phenomena in Causal Residual Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17696)
*Cheng Huan,Hongwei Yuan*

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于伴随灵敏度的因果残差Transformer位置影响分析框架，从理论上推导了token位置对模型输出的影响机制，并给出了若干诊断与正则化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 因果残差Transformer（causal residual Transformer），分析了残差连接、因果掩码、位置编码等机制对token位置敏感性的影响。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未使用任何标准数据集，仅进行了controlled simulations（受控仿真）。

Experiments: 受控仿真实验，验证了所提出的位置影响诊断和正则化干预方法的效果，但未与任何现有方法进行对比。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。如果课题涉及Transformer-based遥感目标检测模型中的位置编码或长序列处理，本文的positional influence分析框架可能提供理论视角，但距离工程落地和轻量化部署较远。不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: We develop an adjoint-sensitivity framework for positional influence in causal residual Transformers and separate unconditional analytic results from conditional boundary-shape conclusions. The principal unconditional theorem is the residual-to-depth-flow estimate for layer controls converging in $L^1$, complemented by a finite-token-to-Volterra attention estimate that explicitly controls the first cells near the causal endpoint. We define a normalized adjoint-energy influence density and derive its exact evolution along full-batch gradient flow. The adjoint admits an exact generator-term decomposition into residual transmission, nonlocal Volterra, and local channels, including all covariance cross terms. Causal masking can amplify early-position sensitivity and residual identity paths can transmit a right-localized terminal bias, but neither mechanism alone forces a U-shaped profile. We therefore state boundary advantages under independently checkable energy, correlation, and local-channel bounds; these conditions are sufficient rather than necessary. Finite-token influence balancing, positional reweighting, and task-aligned observability are presented as diagnostics or regularizers with explicit differentiation requirements, computational costs, and limitations. Controlled simulations illustrate that each intervention controls its designated surrogate, while observability balance or outer-loop reweighting need not monotonically reduce the influence-based Lost-in-the-Middle diagnostic.

</details>


### [124] [COVAriance-Induced Fairness Gap Penalty for Subgroup-Fair Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18119)
*Kyungseon Lee,Hankyo Jeong,Kunwoong Kim,Kwanho Lee,Yongdai Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种基于协方差代理的公平聚类算法COVA-FC，解决多敏感属性联合定义子群时的公平聚类问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是公平聚类（fair clustering）算法，属于机器学习公平性领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提出COVA-FC算法，基于协方差代理的连续松弛优化。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Experiments: COVA-FC在子群公平性和高阶边际公平性设置下，相比现有基线方法取得了有竞争力的cost-fairness trade-off，并提升了计算效率。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何相关借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器学习公平性方向，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Fair clustering aims to make cluster assignments independent of sensitive attributes, but this goal becomes challenging when multiple sensitive attributes jointly define many subgroups. In such settings, directly extending existing fair clustering algorithms is computationally expensive or numerically unstable, especially when the number of subgroups grows exponentially and some subgroups contain only a few instances. To address these challenges, we define a subgroup-fairness gap for clustering and derive a covariance-based surrogate that exactly matches this gap. We then introduce a continuous relaxation of the surrogate, enabling efficient gradient-based optimization and yielding our proposed algorithm, COVA-FC. We also show that subgroup fairness alone does not imply marginal fairness, and extend our framework to capture a subgroup-marginal-fairness gap. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that COVA-FC achieves competitive cost-fairness trade-offs and improves computational efficiency over existing baselines in both subgroup and higher-order marginal settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [125] [Rater State Bias in RLHF Preference Data: An Audit Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16195)
*Elena Kopteva,Vitaliy Hlynianyi-Zhuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RLHF中标注者状态漂移（rater state shift）作为偏好数据中结构化偏差的来源，并给出审计框架，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。论文讨论的是RLHF（基于人类反馈的强化学习）中偏好标注数据的结构化偏差问题，属于大语言模型对齐领域。与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等方向完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 不适用。论文不涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务或任何计算机视觉任务。

Model architecture: 不适用。论文未涉及任何遥感或目标检测模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不适用。论文未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。论文未涉及星上/边缘部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 不适用。论文未涉及遥感或目标检测数据集及评价指标。

Experiments: 不适用。论文仅提出假设和审计协议，未进行实验验证。

Ideas for my research: 无。该论文属于LLM对齐领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无任何交叉点。

Abstract: We identify a structured confound in Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF). Pairwise preference labels are intended to reflect the compared outputs, but they may also reflect the rater's state during annotation. Under sustained stressful or distressing conditions, raters' preferences may shift over time. As a result, preference data can encode rater state alongside judgments about response quality. These shifts differ from ordinary disagreement or random label noise. They are state dependent, can be shared across annotators working under similar conditions, and can propagate through reward modeling and policy optimization. We therefore propose rater state shift as a plausible and testable source of structured bias in RLHF preference data. This paper develops a hypothesis and an audit framework for studying this source of bias. We define rater state shift, rater state confound, and correlated rater state bias. We also define survival level emotional authenticity as a measurable response pattern using lexical, pragmatic, discourse, and safety related features. We analyze how correlated rater state bias can survive aggregation and enter learned reward signals. We derive five falsifiable predictions and effect size thresholds for an initial audit. Finally, we present an audit protocol and pilot study plan that can be applied to publicly available instruction tuned models. We do not infer the training history of any specific deployed model. Our goal is to isolate a plausible and testable source of structured bias in RLHF preference data.

</details>


### [126] [Design and Validation of a Lightweight 1D CNN for Affective Touch Classification in Soft Plush Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16196)
*Aleksandrs Vališevskis,Aleksandrs Okss,Inese Tīģere,Aleksejs Kataševs,Dina Bethere,Anete Hofmane,Airisa Šteinberga,Undīne Gavriļenko,Santa Meļķe,Lucie Matoušková*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向软体交互伴侣的情感触觉识别框架，通过468个CNN架构搜索找到13.2k参数的紧凑1D CNN模型，结合启发式阈值+CNN的混合推理管线，可在微控制器上实现20Hz实时运行。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是软体机器人触觉情感识别（affective touch recognition），属于社交辅助机器人/人机交互领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感核心内容，也不涉及目标检测任务。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：情感触觉识别（affective touch recognition）；场景：软体交互伴侣（soft interactive companions），社交辅助机器人；传感器：软体触觉传感器（soft sensorized companions）；目标类别：触觉手势情感分类。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 1D CNN（一维卷积神经网络），具体为dilated 1D CNN（膨胀一维卷积）；通过468个CNN架构搜索确定最优结构；最终模型参数量13.2k。

Lightweight method: 模型压缩：通过架构搜索找到紧凑模型（13.2k参数）；量化部署（quantized deployment）；混合推理策略：启发式阈值过滤（heuristic filtering）+ CNN精细分类。摘要未提及剪枝、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 部署平台：微控制器（microcontroller）；计算量：3.2 MMAC per window（量化部署后）；实时性：20 Hz实时运行；推理管线：先启发式阈值快速过滤，再CNN精细分类，适合嵌入式部署。参数量13.2k，极低。摘要未说明FLOPs、显存、能耗等具体指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自建FAIR-compliant数据集，1326个标注手势序列，25名参与者（儿童、青少年、成人）；评价指标：测试准确率75%，留一法交叉验证准确率85%。

Experiments: 468个CNN模型架构与超参数搜索；13.2k参数模型达到75%测试准确率、85%留一法交叉验证准确率；与之前启发式系统对比，CNN能检测到更细微的社交触觉；混合推理管线被提出作为嵌入式部署策略。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；1D CNN架构不适用于遥感图像目标检测（通常需要2D CNN处理空间特征）；硬件集成部分在后续研究中，本文未涉及；数据集为触觉序列数据，非遥感图像数据。

Ideas for my research: 尽管领域完全不同，但以下思路可间接借鉴：(1) 通过大规模架构搜索（468个模型）找到紧凑模型的方法论，可类比用于遥感轻量检测网络的NAS搜索；(2) 混合推理策略（简单规则快速过滤+精细模型分类）可用于遥感在轨场景，例如先通过阈值或简单检测器过滤背景区域，再对候选区域用轻量检测模型精细识别；(3) 量化部署到微控制器的思路与星上FPGA/NPU部署有相似性，但遥感场景通常需要更强的算力；(4) 13.2k参数达到可接受精度的思路表明极轻量模型在特定任务上的可行性，但遥感目标检测的复杂度远高于触觉分类。

Abstract: Soft, sensorized companions offer a physically safe and emotionally intuitive interface for socially assistive technologies, yet their deformability and multichannel tactile sensing complicate the robust interpretation of human affect. This study presents a complete open-source MATLAB-based framework for the development and validation of compact deep learning models for affective touch recognition in soft interactive companions. As a primary contribution, a diverse FAIR-compliant dataset of 1326 labelled gesture sequences collected from 25 participants spanning children, teenagers, and adults is made publicly available, providing a reusable resource for future research in affective touch recognition. Through systematic architecture and hyperparameter exploration across 468 CNN models, the study identifies compact dilated one-dimensional convolutional neural networks (1D CNNs) as the most effective solution, with a 13.2k-parameter model achieving 75% test accuracy and 85% mean leave-one-subject-out cross-validation accuracy. Theoretical inference-time analysis shows that quantized deployment requires 3.2 MMAC per window, compatible with 20 Hz real-time operation on the target microcontroller. PC-based real-time simulation with the physical toy streaming sensor data demonstrates that the CNN resolves subtle social touches that the previous heuristic system failed to detect, whereas high-force negative interactions are captured more reliably by trivial threshold-based logic. The resulting hybrid inference pipeline - instantaneous heuristic filtering followed by CNN-based nuanced gesture classification - is proposed as the embedded deployment strategy. The study demonstrates that emotionally meaningful, privacy-preserving touch interpretation is computationally feasible for direct embedding within soft therapeutic companions, with hardware integration addressed in a forthcoming study.

</details>


### [127] [Some Large Language Models Exhibit Consistent Risk Attitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16197)
*Bowen Sun,Rui Min,Yuxi Wang,Brian Odegaard,Qi Wang,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究大语言模型(LLM)在不确定性下的风险态度一致性，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化/部署等研究方向**完全无关**。论文研究的是LLM在空间导航、临床分诊、金融分配等任务中的风险决策行为，属于AI对齐与行为决策领域。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 摘要未说明目标检测或遥感模型架构。涉及6个代表性LLM，但未给出具体模型名称。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明遥感数据集。涉及100名人类参与者和6个LLM，任务包括空间导航、临床分诊、金融分配。使用回归模型提取信念-决策映射，量化风险敏感度和风险态度偏差。

Experiments: 主要发现：LLM在任务内具有稳健一致性，跨任务保持排序稳定性，风险态度分布比人类基线更受限。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于LLM行为心理学/对齐研究，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence systems are deployed in open-ended, high-stakes settings, a critical dimension remains unmeasured: how perceived risk is translated into action. We test whether large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic and consistent risk attitudes under uncertainty. We introduce a cross-domain framework that decouples contextual risk belief from categorical decision, and apply it to six representative LLMs and 100 human participants across spatial navigation, clinical triage, and financial allocation tasks. Using regression models, we extract each agents belief-to-decision mapping and quantify risk sensitivity and risk attitude bias. We find that most tested LLMs exhibit (i) robust intra-task consistency, indicating stable mappings from contextual belief to risk decision within a fixed task domain; (ii) cross-domain rank-order stability, preserving relative risk posture across tasks; and (iii) a convergence toward a restricted risk-attitude distribution relative to the broader human baseline. These results reveal risk attitude as a stable and previously uncharacterized dimension of LLM behavior, establishing a foundation for evaluating and aligning AI systems in open-ended decision-making and motivating further investigation into the origins of these intrinsic behavioral dispositions.

</details>


### [128] [A Survey on GNN-based Link Prediction: Techniques, Applications, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16198)
*Chengcheng Sun,Yajie Song,Cheng Zhai,Jiayun Tian,Jia Yang,Xiaobin Rui,Jian Zhang,Zhixiao Wang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一篇关于图神经网络（GNN）在链接预测任务上的综述论文，从技术和应用两个维度对现有方法进行分类和评述。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是GNN-based link prediction，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署等任何相关要素。

<details>
