Abstract: AI agents increasingly act within the same clinical, political, scientific, and social systems that behavioral scientists study. Evaluating these systems requires source-level diagnosis: the same behavioral pattern may arise from an agent representational substrate or from the roles, objectives, interaction structures, and governance rules that shape its expression. This Perspective proposes a diagnostic framework for AI agent behavior: layer attribution. The foundational computational layer defines what behaviors are possible through architecture, memory, perception, attention, and representation. The behavioral modulation layer shapes how those capacities are expressed through identity, resources, objectives, social interaction, institutional constraints, and governance. The framework clarifies three consequences: surrogate validity is a model-task-layer relation, human-AI divergence provides diagnostic evidence, and governance requires source attribution before intervention. Treating AI agents as behavioral actors therefore requires evaluation methods that determine where behavior originates before deciding how to explain, validate, or govern it.

</details>


### [180] [Is Your Model Thinking or Just Stagnating? PUMA: Diagnosing Reasoning Pathology via Phase-Momentum Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17188)
*Cheng Yan,Guangyang Ye,Wuyang Zhang,Fan Xu,Zhijun Fan,Xiang Xia,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PUMA框架，通过相位-动量对齐假设和认知-能量模型，在测试阶段动态截断大推理模型的CoT推理过程，以缓解"过度思考"问题，实现精度与效率的更好权衡。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于大语言推理模型(LRM)的测试时推理效率优化，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的剪枝/量化/蒸馏等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。仅当对"测试时自适应推理策略"这一方向感兴趣时，可作为泛化参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大推理模型(LRM)的测试时推理效率优化；应用场景：通用自然语言推理；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: PUMA框架采用分层诊断架构，包含轻量级相位监控(lightweight phase monitoring)和事件触发的几何分析(event-triggered geometric analysis)。基于Cognitive-Energy Model，通过两个正交维度（Geometric Cognitive Effort和Entropic Cognitive Uncertainty）刻画推理动态。

Lightweight method: 不是传统意义上的模型轻量化方法。PUMA是训练无关(training-free)的测试时推理截断框架，通过动态判断何时停止CoT推理来减少计算开销，而非压缩模型本身。涉及轻量级相位监控模块，但未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。实验在1.5B到32B参数的LRM上进行，但未给出具体的计算资源消耗数据。不涉及星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标涉及accuracy和accuracy-efficiency trade-off，但未给出具体指标名称。在多个基准(diverse benchmarks)上进行了跨领域泛化(cross-domain generalization)实验。

Experiments: 在1.5B到32B参数规模的LRM上进行了实验，PUMA一致优于state-of-the-art基线方法。但摘要未给出具体数值结果、对比方法名称或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景，与本研究方向无直接关联；2. 方法针对的是大语言模型的CoT推理过程，无法直接迁移到遥感目标检测模型的轻量化；3. 未提供计算资源消耗的具体量化指标；4. 未讨论在边缘设备或星上平台的部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 虽然论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化无直接关系，但以下思路可能有间接启发：1. "测试时动态截断"的思想——在遥感目标检测推理时，可根据输入图像的复杂度动态调整模型计算路径，简单场景提前退出，复杂场景全量计算；2. "轻量级监控+事件触发"的分层架构设计思路，可借鉴到星上资源受限场景下的自适应推理策略设计；3. 对"过度思考"问题的认识——在星上部署时，模型也可能因冗余计算浪费宝贵的计算资源和能耗，需要设计高效的推理终止机制。

Abstract: Test-time scaling empowers Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) to tackle complex tasks via extensive Chain-of-Thought (CoT). However, this often induces the "overthinking" paradox, where redundant reasoning increases computational overhead without guaranteeing accuracy. Existing test-time efficiency optimization methods primarily fall into two categories: information-theoretic approaches, which are prone to "deceptive convergence" where low uncertainty masks hallucinations, and latent representation analyses, which are often post-hoc, lacking the real-time sensitivity for dynamic reasoning. To bridge this gap, we first posit the Phase-Momentum Alignment Hypothesis, asserting that reasoning correctness hinges on the temporal synchronization between geometric momentum and uncertainty resolution. We then theoretically formulate the Cognitive-Energy Model to characterize these dynamics through two orthogonal dimensions: Geometric Cognitive Effort, quantified by latent velocity and tortuosity, and Entropic Cognitive Uncertainty. To operationalize this, we introduce PUMA (Phase-Uncertainty Momentum Alignment), a training-free framework employing a tiered diagnostic architecture. By coupling lightweight phase monitoring with event-triggered geometric analysis, PUMA effectively distinguishes active exploration from passive stagnation, enabling precise interventions through adaptive truncation or corrective measures. Extensive experiments on LRMs spanning 1.5B to 32B demonstrate that PUMA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse benchmarks, achieving a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off and robust cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [181] [Toward Anthropomorphic Dialogue: A Closed-Loop Framework for Human-Like Chat Generation, Evaluation, and Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17191)
*Wentao Liu,Siyu Song,Xi Chen,Youjia Li,Xiaokun Wang,Min Ji,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AnthroDial框架，用于拟人化对话系统的架构、评估与对齐，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于拟人化对话系统（anthropomorphic dialogue），涉及大语言模型（LLM）、角色扮演、记忆管理、对话评估等NLP领域内容，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：拟人化对话生成（anthropomorphic dialogue）；应用场景：类人私密聊天系统；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: AnthroDial闭环框架，包含：(1) 角色条件调度的对话运行时（persona/scenario cards, long-term memory, virtual time）；(2) 可执行基准测试（L0有效性门控、5个per-turn维度、5个dialogue-level维度）；(3) 后训练pipeline（SFT + GRPO + ZPD感知奖励）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 自建基准：55个persona、50个scenario、50个persona-scenario绑定、每个模型100个角色条件case。评价指标：strict ACC（严格准确率）、overall score（综合评分）。

Experiments: 评估16个系统（含前沿baseline、开源模型、thinking/no-think变体、SFT/RL消融）。最强非训练baseline strict ACC 32.00%；Qwen3.6-27B-SFT+RL达到39.00% strict ACC和98.5 overall score。9B no-think设置下，SFT和RL将strict ACC从0.00%提升至13.00%和18.37%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于NLP/对话系统领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Human-like private chat requires more than fluent response generation: a system must preserve persona, relationship, memory, bounded knowledge, medium-specific timing, and a coherent multi-turn arc. We present AnthroDial, a closed-loop framework that formulates anthropomorphic dialogue as a joint problem of system architecture, executable evaluation, and diagnostic alignment. It combines (1) a role-conditioned scheduled dialogue runtime with persona and scenario cards, long-term memory, virtual time, and single-draft message decisions; (2) an executable benchmark with an L0 validity gate, five per-turn dimensions, and five dialogue-level dimensions; and (3) a post-training pipeline that filters 16,436 scheduled-decision examples for SFT and applies GRPO with a cognitive-diagnostic, ZPD-aware reward. The reward maintains Kalman-filtered capability estimates for each behavioral dimension, upweights dimensions with larger capability deficits, and uses rollout scores as task-level ZPD matches to focus optimization on learnable weak skills. On a benchmark with 55 personas, 50 scenarios, 50 persona-scenario bindings, and 100 role-conditioned cases per model, we evaluate 16 systems spanning frontier baselines, open models, thinking/no-think variants, and SFT/RL ablations. The strongest non-trained baseline reaches 32.00% strict ACC, while Qwen3.6-27B-SFT+RL reaches 39.00% strict ACC and a 98.5 overall score. In the 9B no-think setting, SFT and RL improve strict ACC from 0.00% to 13.00% and 18.37%. These results show that anthropomorphic dialogue benefits when generation, evaluation, and reward shaping share the same behavioral dimensions.

</details>


### [182] [A Systematic Evaluation of Trajectory Data Curation for LoRA Fine-Tuning of Code Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17205)
*Yunze Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究开源大语言模型在代码智能体轨迹数据上的LoRA微调中，轨迹数据质量与数量对模型性能的影响，提出Efficiency-Style两轴质量评分框架。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。研究的是LLM代码智能体的SFT数据过滤策略，属于自然语言处理/代码生成领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或边缘部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：代码智能体监督微调（SFT）数据过滤策略研究。应用场景：代码生成与软件工程（SWE-bench）。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct，LoRA微调。与遥感目标检测模型架构无关。

Lightweight method: 涉及LoRA（低秩适配）微调，这是一种参数高效微调方法，但并非模型轻量化（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS等）。不涉及遥感模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。7B参数规模的模型显然不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: SWE-trajectory数据集（67,074条轨迹，其中32,161条已解决）。评价指标：交叉熵损失（CE loss）、ROUGE-L、Spearman秩相关、Mann-Whitney检验。不涉及遥感数据集或检测指标。

Experiments: 16组控制实验，涵盖策略、规模、消融分析。发现数据质量-数量权衡具有规模依赖性：小规模（500→1000）时数据量翻倍带来~12.7% CE损失降低，而TopQ-Random差距<1%；2000条轨迹时差距扩大至3.6%。消融实验发现error-retry rate是主导子维度。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化内容。7B模型规模过大，不适用于星上场景。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。论文研究的是LLM代码智能体的数据过滤策略，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交集。如果强行关联，其"数据质量评分"的思想或许可以类比到遥感检测训练数据（如难例挖掘）的筛选，但非常间接且牵强。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of open-weight LLMs on expert agent trajectories has emerged as a prominent approach to building capable code agents without reliance on proprietary models. A central yet underexplored question is how trajectory quality and quantity jointly shape model performance. We present a systematic empirical study of trajectory data filtering for LoRA fine-tuning of Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct on the SWE-trajectory dataset (67,074 trajectories, of which 32,161 are resolved). We propose a two-axis quality scoring framework -- Efficiency and Style -- and evaluate it through 16 controlled experiments spanning strategy, scale, and ablation analyses. Since 7B-scale models attain near-zero SWE-bench resolve rates, we adopt cross-entropy (CE) loss on held-out trajectories as the primary metric, validated via first-action generation: CE loss and ROUGE-L are perfectly rank-correlated (Spearman $ρ$ = -1.00), with limited-sample evidence supporting but not conclusively establishing this proxy. Our results reveal a scale-dependent quality-quantity trade-off: at small scales, doubling the dataset (500 to 1,000) yields ~12.7% CE-loss reduction whereas the TopQ-Random gap stays <1% (Mann-Whitney p > 0.10); at 2,000 trajectories this same gap widens to 3.6% (p = 0.016). Ablation further identifies error-retry rate as the dominant sub-dimension, performing comparably to the full composite ($Δ$ < 0.2%). Together, these findings establish trajectory-level quality scoring as a viable but scale-sensitive lever for code-agent SFT and offer a proxy-validated evaluation protocol for the regime where end-to-end resolve rate is statistically infeasible.

</details>


### [183] [Constrained Path Reasoning: Measuring When Committed Stages Earn Their Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17240)
*Honglin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究LLM推理管线中的约束路径推理（CPR）方法，探讨中间阶段的承诺机制与成本效益分析，与遥感、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等方向完全无关。论文研究的是大语言模型（LLM）推理管线中的约束路径推理（Constrained Path Reasoning）问题，属于自然语言处理/形式化推理领域，与计算机视觉、遥感图像分析没有任何交集。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM推理管线中的路径推理与承诺机制。应用场景：形式化推理、QCQP求解、多项式实例验证。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: CPR（Constrained Path Reasoning）框架，包含source-aware path hypothesis、stage-level accounting、residual triage、rollback rule等组件。不涉及任何视觉模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是推理过程中的计算成本与收益权衡，但并非模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：1,180个生成的QCQP实例和40个工程化的退化多项式实例（2,140个端点）。评价指标：usable yield、effective branching、endpoint concentration、cost per usable output、collision entropy等。均为形式化推理领域的指标，与目标检测的mAP、F1等无关。

Experiments: 实验结果显示：residual triage以17.7%的尝试次数恢复了repair-all方法63.0%的额外可行产出；fixed-LLM accounting的usable yield从41.1%（直接）到90.0%（形式化+确定性执行后）；two-action rollback rule达到90% usable yield vs 36.7%。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，不存在可借鉴的局限性分析。摘要未讨论遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化的任何问题。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究LLM推理中的承诺机制与成本效益分析，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: When does a committed intermediate stage in an LLM reasoning pipeline earn its cost? Constrained Path Reasoning (CPR) pairs a source-aware path hypothesis with stage-level accounting. Search generates provisional states; trusted or validated invariants can constrain hard, while other proposals remain soft and revisable. CPR predicts that task-compatible commitments can factor transitions, concentrate candidate mass, induce regularity, and expose feedback when their gains exceed propagated error and execution cost. The formalism covers discrete commitments and continuous flows and measures effective branching, endpoint concentration, and cost per usable output. Across 1,180 generated QCQPs and 40 engineered degenerate polynomial instances (2,140 endpoints), residual triage recovers 63.0% of repair-all's additional feasible yield with 17.7% of its attempts. Fixed-LLM accounting (270 unique calls shared across nested arms) finds usable yield of 41.1% direct, 90.0% after formalization and deterministic execution, 20.0% after one-shot convexification, and 21.1% for the full path. In 120 paired-condition calls, a two-action rollback rule reaches 90% usable yield versus 36.7% for the feedback-conditioned selector. Two endpoint probes separate source from validation: a 72-output cross-trajectory transplant reduces entropy and acceptable mass; a 24-output same-call self-proposal pilot gives unchanged two-repeat collision entropy, 25.0% versus 8.3% usable yield, and 1/8 deterministically confirmed endpoint checks. Model-generated states supply hypotheses; trusted execution earns constraint strength.

</details>


### [184] [LenGuard-GPC: Length Guarding with Guided-Prompt Consistency for Spatial Reasoning Reinforce Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17243)
*Xingjian Tao,Yiwei Wang,Yujun Cai,Jing Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LenGuard-GPC密集奖励框架，通过token级KL散度作为奖励信号并引入分段长度奖励，在六项多视图空间推理基准上提升准确率并缩短响应长度。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于多视图空间推理中的视觉-语言模型推理优化（RL+密集奖励），不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署。虽然"多视图空间推理"在遥感领域也有应用（如多视角遥感图像匹配），但论文方法本质上是LLM/VLM的推理策略改进，与遥感目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多视图空间推理（multi-view spatial reasoning），视觉-语言模型（VLM）的推理优化。应用场景：通用视觉推理。传感器类型：摘要未说明。目标类别：摘要未说明。

Model architecture: 基于GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）的强化学习框架，引入LenGuard-GPC密集奖励机制。核心组件：token-wise KL散度计算（标准prompt vs 引导prompt的预测分布对比）、分段长度奖励（staged length bonus）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是推理质量与推理长度的平衡，而非模型压缩或加速。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等）。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：6个多视图空间推理基准（具体名称摘要未说明）。评价指标：准确率（accuracy）、平均响应长度（average response length）。

Experiments: 在6个多视图空间推理基准上，LenGuard-GPC相比vanilla GRPO提升了准确率，同时降低了平均响应长度。具体数值摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 方法依赖VLM的token级预测分布，计算开销可能较大；3. 仅在通用视觉推理基准上验证，未在遥感场景下测试。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1）"分段长度奖励"控制推理长度的思路，在星上资源受限场景下控制模型推理步长可能有参考价值；2）密集奖励信号设计思想可用于轻量化模型训练时的多目标优化（精度+速度+能耗）。但整体上与本课题方向差异较大。

Abstract: Multi-view spatial reasoning requires vision-language models to compare visual evidence across images, align object correspondences, and infer spatial relations over long visual contexts, a setting where chain-of-thought reasoning tends to grow verbose without becoming more accurate. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards is a natural fit for this task, but standard GRPO reward relies on sparse outcome-level feedback and gives no signal about where a reasoning trajectory goes wrong, nor any control over its length. We propose LenGuard-GPC, a dense reward framework that addresses both problems together. For each sampled trajectory, it compares the token-wise predictive distributions under a standard prompt and a guided prompt, and uses the resulting token-sum KL divergence as a dense reward signal. Since this KL penalty accumulates over tokens and would otherwise reward shorter responses regardless of their quality, we introduce a staged length bonus that keeps reasoning length within a controlled range without simply encouraging brevity. On six multi-view spatial reasoning benchmarks, LenGuard-GPC improves accuracy over vanilla GRPO while reducing average response length.

</details>


### [185] [Coordinated Disentanglement with Iterative Mode Discovery Under Hidden Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17264)
*Rong Hu,Ling Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于解耦表示学习的协调式迭代模式发现框架(CoDID)，用于鲁棒的属性预测，通过动态架构和元优化缓解误差放大问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于解耦表示学习和属性预测，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：属性预测（attribute prediction）；应用场景：通用机器学习/计算机视觉；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：摘要未说明。

Model architecture: CoDID框架，包含动态架构（dynamic architecture，可适应模式数量的动态变化）和协调机制（coordination mechanism，通过元优化meta-optimization缓解误差放大）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标，仅提到"diverse tasks"上达到SOTA。

Experiments: 摘要仅称在多种任务上达到state-of-the-art性能，未提供具体对比方法和消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景；2. 未提供任何轻量化或部署相关信息；3. 未说明计算开销和模型复杂度；4. 动态架构可能引入额外计算负担，不利于边缘/星上部署。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。解耦表示学习的思想在理论上可尝试用于遥感目标检测中的特征解耦（如分离目标类别特征与背景特征），但该论文未涉及检测任务和轻量化，直接借鉴意义不大。

Abstract: Disentangled representation learning is a powerful paradigm for robust attribute prediction. While recent methods address attribute correlations, hidden correlations remain underexplored, where data under the value of a certain attribute exhibit underlying modes correlated with other attributes. To preserve mode information and achieve disentanglement, we jointly discover modes and enforce mode-based conditional independence. Yet, the interdependency between these two modules may lead to error amplification under naive iterations. We propose Coordinated Disentanglement with Iterative mode Discovery (CoDID), an end-to-end framework featuring a dynamic architecture that adapts to evolving number of modes, and a coordination mechanism that mitigates error amplification via meta-optimization. Empirical results demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance on diverse tasks.

</details>


### [186] [An Explicit World Model Based on Data-First Ontology: DaoQL Multimodal Storage Validation and Counterfactual Reasoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17269)
*Zhanbo Li,Shifeng Wu,Xiangjin Meng,Wenjie Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出数据优先本体论（DaoQL），将LLM作为推理引擎，确定性知识迁移到显式多模态数据库中，以解决幻觉、知识冻结、可解释性差和可修改性差四个结构风险。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的结构风险与显式知识库（DaoQL）设计，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然涉及嵌入式部署和延迟测量，但场景和问题域与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无任何交集。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM知识库增强与反事实推理；应用场景：医学、金融等高精度领域；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: DaoQL系统：集成图、列、向量和全文引擎的多模态数据库；LLM（GPT-4o）作为推理和语言引擎；KVCache图节点、专家热更新、DaoQL-Agent运行时为未来工作。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。仅提及嵌入式同机部署（embedded same-machine setup），但这不是模型轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 嵌入式同机部署，报告了图BFS 1.20 ms、HNSW 83.1 μs、Fluent混合查询105.8 μs的延迟；LDBC SNB SF1上34/34查询覆盖，但整体仅1.8 QPS；ANN-Benchmarks Recall@10 >= 99%时达千级QPS。但这些指标针对数据库查询而非目标检测模型，与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: LDBC SNB SF1（图数据库基准）、ANN-Benchmarks（近似最近邻搜索基准）；五领域反事实实验（n=1250）；评价指标：查询覆盖率、QPS、Recall@10、可组合反事实可分解性（composable counterfactual decomposability）。

Experiments: DaoQL+GPT-4o在五领域反事实实验中达到94%可组合反事实可分解性，比单独GPT-4o高49个百分点；数据库查询延迟在亚毫秒到毫秒级。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文本身也明确区分了可证明结构、初步实证证据和架构路线图声明，KVCache图节点等核心组件为未来工作。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于LLM+知识库方向，与遥感目标检测及轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: Large language models encode world models implicitly in neural weights, which exposes four structural risks in high-precision domains such as medicine and finance: hallucination, frozen knowledge, poor explainability, and poor modifiability. This paper proposes data-first ontology: LLMs are treated as reasoning and language engines, while deterministic knowledge is moved into an explicit multimodal database, DaoQL. We formalize an explicit world model and show that, under rule independence, deterministic evaluation, and fixed conflict resolution, explicit models provide a sufficient condition for composable counterfactual decomposability; implicit models lack atomic read/delta semantics and therefore provide no comparable architectural guarantee. The implemented system focuses on DaoQL's verified storage layer and explicit Eval path, integrating graph, column, vector, and full-text engines within one process. KVCache graph nodes, expert hot updates, and the DaoQL-Agent runtime remain future work. On an embedded same-machine setup, DaoQL reports graph BFS at 1.20 ms, HNSW at 83.1 us, and a Fluent hybrid query at 105.8 us; these results indicate engineering potential but must be interpreted with deployment-shape differences from client-server systems. Exploratory measurements on LDBC SNB SF1 and ANN-Benchmarks further show 34/34 query coverage with interactive-class queries mostly in the sub-millisecond to millisecond range, but only 1.8 QPS overall due to long-tail BI/IC queries; ANN-Benchmarks reaches Recall@10 >= 99% at thousand-level QPS after a bridge-edge protection fix. In a five-domain counterfactual experiment (n = 1250), DaoQL+GPT-4o achieves 94% composable counterfactual decomposability, 49 percentage points above GPT-4o alone. The paper explicitly separates provable structure, preliminary empirical evidence, and architectural roadmap claims.

</details>


### [187] [Lossless but Not Free: An Empirical Anatomy of Speculative Decoding on Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17283)
*Param Chordiya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实现并评估了推测解码（Speculative Decoding）在消费级设备上的加速效果，核心是让小模型草稿、大模型并行验证来突破自回归解码的内存带宽瓶颈。

Research relevance: 1分。本文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化均无直接关系。它研究的是大语言模型（LLM）推理加速中的推测解码方法，属于NLP/通用ML领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或星上部署。虽然推测解码作为一种推理加速策略在理论上可迁移，但摘要本身未涉及任何遥感或检测内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非后续需要研究LLM-based遥感检测模型的推理加速，否则参考价值有限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型自回归解码加速；应用场景：消费级设备（Apple Silicon笔记本）上的LLM推理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 采用draft-target双模型架构：小模型（draft model）自回归生成K个候选token，大模型（target model）以batch方式并行验证所有候选token，通过rejection sampling保证输出分布与target模型一致。

Lightweight method: 推测解码（Speculative Decoding）——一种推理阶段的加速策略，不是模型压缩方法。核心思想是用小模型草稿+大模型并行验证来减少大模型的自回归步数。摘要还提到了量化（quantized Metal backend）但仅作为对比配置出现。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅报告了wall-clock speedup（实测1.61×加速比，K=6时最优）和acceptance profile（从K=1的69.7%下降到最优点的37.8%）。5个配置中有3个出现减速，原因是draft模型不够快或Metal后端的"并行"验证实际是串行执行。运行平台为Apple Silicon笔记本（MPS后端）。

Datasets and metrics: 验证方式：三层分布等价性检验，包括两样本检验（约9200个真实模型token，χ²=162.5，dof=200，p=0.976）和精确贪心序列一致性。未使用标准遥感/视觉数据集。

Experiments: 5种draft/target后端配置对比，最优配置在K=6时达到1.61×实测加速比；3/5配置出现减速；分析了减速原因：draft模型速度优势不足、Metal后端串行执行验证。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 仅在消费级笔记本上测试，未涉及边缘/星上硬件（FPGA/NPU等）；3. 推测解码的加速效果高度依赖draft-target速度差和batch并行能力，在资源受限的星上场景中可能难以复现；4. 未讨论目标检测模型（CNN/Transformer-based detector）的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 虽然本文与遥感在轨检测无关，但推测解码的思想有潜在借鉴价值：1. 在星上资源受限场景中，可探索"轻量检测器草稿+高精度检测器验证"的两阶段推理策略，用小模型快速生成候选框，大模型仅验证关键候选；2. 需注意星上硬件（如FPGA/NPU）是否支持真正的batch并行验证，否则可能像本文Metal后端一样出现串行化导致的减速；3. 本文对"失败案例"的分析方法值得学习——在轨部署中同样需要明确什么条件下加速方法会失效。

Abstract: Single-stream autoregressive decoding of large language models is bound by memory bandwidth: each generated token requires one full forward pass through the target model, and successive passes cannot be parallelized. Speculative decoding restructures this computation: a small draft model proposes $K$ tokens autoregressively, the target model scores all of them in one batched pass, and a rejection-sampling rule provably preserves the target model's output distribution. We present a from-scratch, device-agnostic (CUDA/MPS/CPU) implementation and an empirical study across five draft/target backend configurations on a consumer Apple-silicon laptop. Distribution equivalence is verified at three levels, culminating in a two-sample test over roughly 9,200 real-model tokens per method ($χ^2 = 162.5$, dof $= 200$, $p = 0.976$) and exact greedy-sequence agreement. The best configuration reaches a measured $1.61\times$ wall-clock speedup at $K=6$, on an acceptance profile declining from 69.7% at $K=1$ to 37.8% at the optimum, while three of five configurations decelerate, either because the draft fails to out-speed a small target or because the quantized Metal backend executes "parallel" verification serially, an effect we isolate and quantify. The failures are as instructive as the successes: speculative decoding pays off only when verification is genuinely batch-parallel and the draft/target latency gap is real.

</details>


### [188] [Learning-Driven Adaptive Audit Scheduling: A Sequential Decision Approach to Off-Chain Data Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17305)
*Changting Lin,Fan Li,Weihang Yu,Keyang He,Mingyuan Yan,Yourong Chen,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DRQN-CMDP模型，将链下数据加密审计建模为部分可观测约束MDP，使用深度循环Q网络与拉格朗日对偶方法优化审计频率与安全约束的平衡。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究区块链/链下数据加密审计问题，涉及POMDP、深度强化学习、同态MAC等，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：链下数据加密审计（cryptographic auditing of off-chain data）；场景：区块链/分布式存储安全；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: DRQN-CMDP：Deep Recurrent Q-Network，使用GRU层维护对隐节点类型的信念（belief），结合拉格朗日对偶上升法自适应调整miss-rate惩罚系数λ。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。仅提到同态MAC原语实现O(1)链上验证开销，属于密码学层面的计算优化，非模型压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。仅报告gas消耗（比固定高频审计低83%）、miss rate（7.5%）和检测延迟，但这些是区块链审计场景指标，与星上部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 对比方法：4种DQN变体、PPO、A2C、PPO-Lagrangian、贝叶斯启发式、3种固定规则基线、oracle启发式，共13种方法。评价指标：gas消耗、miss rate、检测延迟。数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集。

Experiments: DRQN-CMDP在三个目标（gas消耗、miss rate、检测延迟）上同时取得最优平衡：gas比固定高频审计低83%，miss rate仅7.5%，检测延迟适中。无其他方法能同时在三项指标上匹配该表现。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何视觉/图像处理任务；3. 未提供模型参数量、计算量等轻量化关键指标；4. 场景为区块链安全，无法直接迁移到遥感领域。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的技术交叉点是：DRQN中的GRU循环结构可用于遥感视频流中的时序目标检测，但本文未涉及视觉特征提取，且强化学习框架与监督式目标检测差异较大。不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: We model cryptographic auditing of off-chain data as a Constrained MDP (CMDP) under partial observability: the storage node's hidden type and corruption state make the problem a POMDP, while a miss-rate ceiling rho imposes an explicit security constraint. We propose DRQN-CMDP, a Deep Recurrent Q-Network whose GRU layer maintains a belief over the latent node type, paired with Lagrangian dual ascent that adapts the miss-rate penalty lambda automatically. A pairing-free homomorphic-MAC primitive supplies O(1) on-chain verification cost. Across 13 methods--four DQN variants, PPO, A2C, PPO-Lagrangian, a stateful Bayesian heuristic, three fixed-rule baselines, and an oracle-informed heuristic--DRQN-CMDP achieves a favourable balance: 83% lower gas than fixed high-frequency auditing, single-digit miss rate (7.5%), and moderate detection latency--a combination no other method matches across all three objectives simultaneously.

</details>


### [189] [Agentic ERP: Multi-Agent Large Language Model Architecture for Autonomous Enterprise Resource Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17331)
*Zhihao Liu,Tianyu Wang,Xi Vincent Wang,Lihui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agentic ERP架构，利用角色对齐的LLM智能体与人工监督结合，实现企业资源规划系统的自主运营决策。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是企业资源规划(ERP)系统中的LLM智能体自动化决策，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：企业ERP系统自主运营决策；应用场景：企业资源规划、跨职能业务流程自动化；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于LLM的多智能体架构，包含角色对齐的LLM智能体、风险分层的Human-in-the-loop机制、基于图的编排器(Planner-Executor-Reflector-Responder)。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 评估分为三个层次：基于场景的任务套件、六种编排范式在跨职能危机任务上的对比、365天agent-in-the-loop模拟。指标包括stockouts（缺货次数）等业务指标。

Experiments: 多智能体方法显著优于基线；在模拟一年运营中实现零缺货，而基于规则的基线在相同需求下累积数百次缺货。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于企业管理系统与LLM智能体领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Enterprise Resource Planning (ERP) systems record transactions reliably but still delegate almost all operational decision-making to human specialists, because classical rule-based automation cannot reason about exceptions and monolithic AI assistants degrade when asked to coordinate across functional boundaries. This paper presents Agentic ERP, an expert-system architecture that combines role-aligned large-language-model (LLM) agents with a risk-tiered human-in-the-loop harness and a graph-based orchestrator to execute end-to-end business workflows on a production ERP backend. First, autonomous ERP operation is formulated as a constrained sequential-decision problem over a structured enterprise state, with a decomposition argument linking role-aligned agents to a measurable reduction in per-step tool-selection complexity. Second, a graph-based Planner--Executor--Reflector--Responder orchestration decouples generation from evaluation through externalised grading criteria and sprint contracts, packaging recent harness-engineering principles as inspectable expert-system artefacts. Third, the system is evaluated at three levels: a scenario-based task suite, a comprehensive comparison of six orchestration paradigms on cross-functional crisis tasks, and a 365-day agent-in-the-loop simulation against rule-based RPA and no-intervention baselines. Across these levels the proposed multi-agent method is significantly better than the baseline, and the system sustains a simulated year of operation with zero stockouts while the rule-based baseline accumulates hundreds under the same demand stream. The work shows that role-aligned LLM agents under human oversight can move an ERP system from passively recording transactions to actively executing operational decisions, and it provides a reference architecture and an evaluation protocol for autonomous enterprise resource planning.

</details>


### [190] [DeeperRadar: End-to-End MIMO Radar Design and Multi-Modal Fusion for Autonomous Vehicle Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17351)
*Eli Goldenshluger,Barak Pinkovich,Chaim Baskin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种雷达-传感器融合驱动的端到端稀疏MIMO雷达设计框架，通过可学习的天线激活模式降低雷达硬件成本，同时保持多模态3D检测性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于自动驾驶场景下的雷达-多模态3D检测，不涉及遥感卫星、遥感图像、在轨部署或目标检测轻量化。虽然涉及传感器稀疏采样（类似剪枝思想），但应用场景、传感器类型（雷达ADC vs. 光学/SAR遥感图像）和任务（3D检测 vs. 遥感目标检测）均与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对雷达传感器稀疏采样与感知任务联合优化的跨领域方法感兴趣，可作为泛读参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多模态3D目标检测；场景：自动驾驶（autonomous mobility）；传感器：雷达（raw ADC data）、相机图像、LiDAR点云；目标类别：摘要未说明具体类别。

Model architecture: DeeperRadar框架包含一个可学习的MIMO设计模块（learnable MIMO design module），该模块与融合网络（fusion network）端到端联合训练。训练时设计模块受其他传感器监督；部署时设计模块被移除，替换为学习到的稀疏子采样掩码（sparse subsampling mask），下游模型架构不变。

Lightweight method: 涉及稀疏采样（sparse acquisition pattern）和天线剪枝（learn which receiver antennas to activate and the effective number of them），本质上是一种硬件层面的稀疏化/剪枝方法。但这不是针对模型本身的轻量化（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏），而是针对雷达传感器的硬件配置优化。不涉及模型参数量、FLOPs、推理加速等模型轻量化核心指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明模型参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署信息。仅提及减少接收天线数量可降低雷达成本和集成复杂度（reduce radar cost and integration complexity），但这是硬件层面的，非模型在轨部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：RADIal dataset（自动驾驶雷达数据集）；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标（如mAP等）。

Experiments: 在RADIal数据集上，DeeperRadar发现的稀疏雷达配置在性能上匹配或超过全阵列基线（full-array baselines），同时使用更少的接收器。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对自动驾驶场景，与遥感卫星无关；2. 稀疏化的是雷达硬件天线，而非神经网络模型；3. 未提供模型计算量、推理速度等轻量化关键指标；4. 未讨论在轨部署或边缘部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 虽然论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化弱相关，但其"端到端学习稀疏采样模式"的思想有一定借鉴意义：1. 可类比思考遥感卫星是否可以通过学习的方式确定哪些波段/通道是冗余的，实现多光谱/高光谱数据的稀疏采样；2. 训练时利用多传感器监督、部署时移除设计模块的"训练-部署解耦"思路，与知识蒸馏中教师-学生框架有相似之处；3. 但整体而言，该论文对"遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点有限。

Abstract: DeeperRadar is a radar-centric, sensor-stack-conditioned framework that co-designs radar sensing and multi-modal 3D detection for autonomous mobility by learning a sparse acquisition pattern end-to-end with the fusion model. A learnable MIMO design module is trained end-to-end within a fusion network that operates directly on raw radar ADC data together with camera images and LiDAR point clouds. During training, the design module is supervised by the other sensors, enabling the system to learn both which receiver antennas to activate and the effective number of them. At deployment, the design module is removed and replaced by the learned sparse subsampling mask, leaving the downstream model architecture unchanged. Evaluated on the RADIal dataset, DeeperRadar discovers sparse, task-aware radar configurations that match or exceed full-array baselines while using fewer receivers, potentially reducing radar cost and integration complexity. These results show that learned optimal MIMO radar design depends on the fusion stack and the downstream perception task.

</details>


### [191] [Self-Modifying Lean Proof Agents with Verifier-Grounded Benchmark Coevolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17352)
*Yuqing Li,Zeguan Wu,Yu Gan,Junyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种自进化的Lean形式化数学证明智能体，通过协同进化智能体与基准测试集来提升定理证明能力，在miniF2F上达到45.1%的解决率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是形式化数学证明（Lean proof agent），属于AI for Math领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化或边缘部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：形式化数学定理证明（Lean proof agent）；应用场景：数学推理、自动定理证明；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 自进化智能体架构：一个小的固定可信运行时（trusted runtime）包裹一个完全可变的workspace（包含proof workflow、prompts和tools）。采用mastery-throttled curriculum update和single-anchor recalibration机制。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：miniF2F test split（held-out）；评价指标：held-out solve rate（解决率）。

Experiments: 运行15代进化，与固定基准基线对比。最佳协同进化智能体达到45.1% held-out solve rate，种子模型为12.7%，最佳固定基准智能体为32.0%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于形式化数学证明领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Designing effective Lean proof agents is a central challenge in formal mathematical reasoning. Beyond building stronger provers, recent work emphasizes the workflow around Lean: how an agent decomposes proof obligations, uses tools and compiler feedback, diagnoses failures, repairs proofs, and maintains structured proof context. Motivated by code-level self-evolving agents, we study whether such workflows can be evolved rather than hand-designed. We present a self-evolving Lean proof agent in which a small fixed, trusted runtime wraps a fully mutable workspace: the proof workflow, prompts, and tools. Unlike most self-evolving systems, which optimize against a fixed external benchmark, our system coevolves the agent and its benchmark. Between generations, the highest-scoring agent (the champion) revises the active task distribution through a mastery-throttled curriculum update that introduces harder proof obligations only after the current level is mastered, and a single-anchor recalibration re-runs the champion on the updated benchmark to keep scores comparable as difficulty rises. All evolution stays inside a Lean-grounded verification loop: however the agent rewrites itself, a success counts only when its behavior yields Lean-verified proofs under a trusted snapshot, and each attempt must emit a machine-readable, Lean-grounded proof context whose representation may evolve but whose groundedness is enforced. We run the coevolving trajectory and a fixed-benchmark baseline for 15 active generations and compare them on a held-out miniF2F test split. The best coevolving agent reaches a 45.1% held-out solve rate, versus 12.7% for the seed and 32.0% for the best fixed-benchmark agent, showing that verifier-grounded self-evolution can improve Lean proof workflows under a coevolving benchmark.

</details>


### [192] [Quantifying Diversity of Thought: A Predictive Law of Weighted LLM Ensemble Lift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17384)
*Junade Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算大语言模型集成中思维多样性带来的性能提升的形式化定律，推导了集成提升的分解公式，并验证了其预测能力。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于LLM集成（ensemble）的性能提升预测，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM集成性能预测；应用场景：大语言模型投票集成；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 不适用。论文研究的是LLM集成（ensemble）的多样性提升，而非单一模型架构设计。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：SuperGPQA、GPQA Diamond（研究生级科学基准）、自建网络安全取证基准（23,520次分级试验含弃权）；评价指标：Spearman's ρ、R²、accuracy adjusted correctness correlation (φ_adj)、accuracy gap、collective accuracy。

Experiments: 在767,520次推理上验证，校准后Spearman's ρ=0.84（SuperGPQA），迁移到未见数据集ρ=0.51（GPQA Diamond）和0.84（取证任务），swap mass跟踪lift的R²≥0.96。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。论文主题与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化完全无关。

Abstract: This paper provides an experimentally verified formal law for calculating the uplift that diversity of thought provides in Large Language Model (LLM) ensembles. From first principles, we derive an exact decomposition of LLM ensemble lift into rescue and damage masses, which yields a compact heuristic for calculating uplift. From this we extract the metrics which predict ensemble performance: an accuracy adjusted correctness correlation, $φ_{\mathrm{adj}}$, together with the accuracy gap and collective accuracy of the pair. We test the law on 767,520 inferences from ten open-weight models over two graduate-level science benchmarks, together with a novel agentic cybersecurity benchmark in which each model conducts digital-forensics investigations by multi-turn tool use in a network-isolated sandbox (23,520 graded trials including abstentions); all votes are released openly. Calibrated once on SuperGPQA at a 40:60 vote split, the heuristic predicts lift on the calibration set with Spearman's $ρ=0.84$ and, with its coefficients frozen, transfers to two datasets never used in calibration ($ρ=0.51$ on GPQA Diamond and $0.84$ on the forensic tasks), whilst the measured swap mass tracks realised lift with $R^2\ge 0.96$ throughout. Raw $φ$ has almost no predictive power ($R^2\le 0.09$ throughout); the accuracy-adjusted $φ_{\mathrm{adj}}$ is markedly superior ($R^2=0.67$ on SuperGPQA), and the heuristic combining these metrics is the most stable pre pooling predictor across the three datasets.

</details>

### [193] [Reinforcement Learning-Guided NSGA-II Enhanced with Gray Relational Coefficient for Multi-Objective Optimization: Application to NASDAQ Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16194)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Qinxu Ding,Ding Ding,Siying Zhu,Jing Ren,Yue Wang,Chong Hui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种RL引导的NSGA-II多目标优化算法（RL-NSGA-II-GRC），用于金融投资组合优化，在基准测试和NASDAQ案例上提升了收敛性和Pareto前沿多样性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是金融领域的多目标优化问题（投资组合优化），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然多目标优化思想在理论上可迁移，但摘要中没有任何遥感或轻量化相关内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：约束多目标优化（constrained MOO）；应用场景：金融投资组合优化（portfolio optimization）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: RL-NSGA-II-GRC：将强化学习（RL）智能体与NSGA-II遗传算法结合，引入灰色关联度（GRC）增强的锦标赛选择算子。RL智能体根据超体积（hypervolume）、可行性和多样性指标在线调整进化参数。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量化网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 基准测试：Kursawe和CONSTR标准MOO测试函数；实际应用：NASDAQ股票组合数据。评价指标：收敛性改进（约5.8%和4.4%对比NSGA-II）、Pareto前沿分布质量、夏普比率（年化Sharpe=1.92）。

Experiments: 在Kursawe和CONSTR基准上，RL-NSGA-II-GRC相比NSGA-II收敛性提升约5.8%和4.4%，且保持良好分布的Pareto非支配解。在NASDAQ投资组合案例中，生成了平滑密集的有效前沿，支持最大夏普比率组合和不同风险偏好下的最优组合识别。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要未说明计算开销、推理速度、模型大小等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。多目标优化思想（如Pareto前沿搜索）理论上可用于轻量化中精度与速度的权衡优化，但该论文本身不涉及遥感或轻量化，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: In modern financial markets, decision-makers increasingly rely on quantitative methods to navigate complex trade-offs among multiple, often conflicting objectives. This paper addresses constrained multi-objective optimization (MOO) with an application to portfolio optimization for minimizing risk and maximizing return. To address existing gaps, we propose a novel reinforcement learning (RL)-guided non-dominated sorting genetic algorithm II (NSGA-II) enhanced with gray relational coefficients (GRC), termed RL-NSGA-II-GRC, which combines an RL agent controller and GRC-based selection to improve convergence and diversity of Pareto fronts. The agent adapts evolutionary parameters online using metrics of hypervolume, feasibility, and diversity, while the GRC tournament operator ranks parents via a unified score considering dominance rank, crowding distance, and proximity to ideal reference. We evaluate the framework on the Kursawe and CONSTR benchmarks and a NASDAQ portfolio application. On the benchmarks, RL-NSGA-II-GRC achieves convergence improvements of about 5.8% and 4.4% over NSGA-II, while preserving well-distributed non-dominated solutions. In the portfolio application, it produces a smooth, densely populated efficient frontier supporting identification of the maximum Sharpe ratio portfolio (annualized Sharpe =1.92) and utility-optimal portfolios for different risk-aversion levels. The main contributions are three-fold: 1) we propose an RL-NSGA-II-GRC method integrating an RL agent into the evolutionary framework to adaptively control parameters via generational feedback; 2) we design a GRC-enhanced binary tournament operator providing a comprehensive indicator to guide the search toward the Pareto front; 3) we demonstrate, on benchmark MOO and a NASDAQ case study, that the method delivers improved convergence and well-populated frontiers supporting actionable insights.

</details>


### [194] [DocOCR-Eval: A Correction-Based Framework for OCR Tool Selection Without Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16203)
*Zihan Xu,Puzhen Wu,Lawrence Chun Man Lau,Wei Liu,Sirui Li,Yifan Peng,Yihao Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DocOCR-Eval，一个无需标注的OCR引擎与多模态大模型在文档解析任务上的自动评估与选择框架。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦文档解析（document parsing）和OCR引擎评估，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：文档解析（document parsing）、OCR引擎评估与选择；场景：扫描文档理解（visual question answering, key information extraction）；传感器类型：扫描文档图像；目标类别：文本、视觉布局信息。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 评估对象包括多种OCR引擎和多模态大语言模型（MLLMs），但论文本身不提出新的检测或识别模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多个跨领域、跨语言的扫描文档基准（scanned document benchmarks）；评价指标：基于文本识别性能的自动评估与排序对齐度（alignment with annotation-based rankings）。

Experiments: 实验表明：聚合多个MLLM可逐步提升与人工标注排序的一致性；在标签稀缺场景下可实现可靠的OCR工具选择。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于文档理解/OCR领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无直接或间接关联。

Abstract: Document parsing is a foundational step for document understanding tasks such as visual question answering and key information extraction, as it transforms unstructured scanned images into structured representations by extracting textual, visual, and layout information. While numerous Optical Character Recognition (OCR) engines and multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have been developed for this purpose, selecting an appropriate document parsing solution for a given document collection remains challenging, particularly in label-scarce settings. In this work, we conduct a systematic evaluation of text recognition performance across a diverse set of OCR engines and state-of-the-art MLLMs on multiple scanned document benchmarks spanning different domains and languages. Motivated by the limited contextual reasoning capabilities of many OCR engines and the high cost of manual annotations, we propose DocOCR-Eval, an annotation-free evaluation framework for automatic OCR assessment and selection. DocOCR-Eval employs a three-staged correction and ranking strategy to approximate annotation-based tool ordering without ground-truth labels. We show that aggregating across multiple MLLMs progressively improves alignment with annotation-based rankings. Extensive experiments further demonstrate that reliable OCR tool selection can be achieved in realistic, label-limited settings, providing practical guidance for deploying document parsing systems across diverse real-world document collections.

</details>


### [195] [Fully-sensorized smart-eyewear platform for on-device Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16222)
*Andrea Giudici,Christian Veronesi,Pietro Bartoli,Mario Caliò,Aurelio Teliti,Giacomo Gervasoni,Diana Trojaniello,Franco Zappa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ARGO智能眼镜平台，基于STM32N6微控制器和集成NPU部署优化后的YOLOv11模型，实现实时城市障碍物识别，强调硬件-固件-AI协同设计。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于可穿戴智能眼镜（smart eyewear）和城市障碍物识别（urban obstacle recognition），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及在轨部署或星上智能。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，仅作为边缘AI部署的参考案例，阅读优先级最低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：实时城市障碍物识别（urban obstacle recognition）；应用场景：可穿戴辅助设备（assistive device）；传感器类型：RGB相机、ToF传感器、麦克风、环境传感器；目标类别：摘要未说明具体类别（WOTR数据集）。

Model architecture: YOLOv11作为基础检测模型；提出Head-wise Parallel Attention (HPA)架构改进，使模型适配NPU高效执行，同时保留原始计算逻辑。

Lightweight method: 模型轻量化主要通过NPU部署适配实现：使用STM32N6集成NPU进行端侧推理；引入HPA架构改进以适配NPU加速器；最终模型内存占用仅2.483 MB。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 部署平台：STM32N6微控制器+集成NPU（非FPGA/NPU/TensorRT的卫星部署场景）；推理速度：10 FPS；功耗：200 mAh电池下连续工作约113分钟；内存占用：2.483 MB；参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟等摘要未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Walking On The Road (WOTR) 数据集（非遥感数据集）；评价指标：mAP50-95 = 24（在严格内存约束下）。

Experiments: 摘要仅给出最终部署配置的mAP50-95=24和内存占用2.483 MB，未提供与其他方法的对比实验或消融实验结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 数据集WOTR为地面行人视角，非遥感俯视/卫星视角；3. 未涉及遥感常见目标（舰船、飞机、车辆等）；4. 未涉及旋转框检测、小目标检测等遥感特有挑战；5. 轻量化方法仅针对特定NPU架构适配，通用性有限。

Ideas for my research: 1. HPA（Head-wise Parallel Attention）的设计思路可借鉴：在保持计算逻辑不变的前提下适配特定硬件加速器，这种"硬件感知的架构设计"思路可用于遥感模型在星上NPU/FPGA的部署；2. STM32N6+NPU的端侧部署方案展示了极低资源下的实时检测可行性（2.483 MB, 10 FPS），可作为星上部署资源预算的参考下限；3. 硬件-固件-AI协同设计（co-design）方法论值得借鉴，在遥感卫星场景中可类比为"载荷-星载计算机-算法"的协同优化。

Abstract: This paper presents ARGO, a smart eyewear platform designed to bridge ergonomic comfort, high computational throughput, and energy efficiency. Unlike cloud-dependent solutions, ARGO leverages the STM32N6 microcontroller and its integrated Neural Processing Unit (NPU) to enable on-device machine learning, minimizing latency and preserving user privacy through local data processing. The primary contribution lies in the holistic co-design of hardware, firmware, and artificial intelligence, centered on the deployment of an optimized YOLOv11 model for real-time urban obstacle recognition. To ensure compatibility with the target NPU, we introduce Head-wise Parallel Attention (HPA), an architectural refinement that enables efficient accelerator execution while preserving the original computational logic. The model is trained on the Walking On The Road (WOTR) dataset, and the final deployed configuration achieves an mAP50-95 of 24 under strict memory constraints, with a memory footprint of only 2.483 MB. The platform integrates a multimodal sensor suite, RGB cameras, Time-of-Flight sensors, microphones, and ambient sensors, and delivers 10 FPS at a continuous autonomy of ~113 minutes on a 200 mAh battery. These results demonstrate the feasibility of a high-performance, privacy-preserving, and socially acceptable assistive device, and highlight how competitive edge AI solutions increasingly demand tightly integrated, multidisciplinary co-design approaches.

</details>


### [196] [LLM Unlearning for Cyber Defense: A Survey on Methods, Challenges, and Emerging Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16227)
*Ruppikha Sree Shankar,Abhishek Bhardwaj,Arnav Doshi,Anusri Nagarajan,Troy Paulus Asia,Saptarshi Sengupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一篇关于大语言模型(LLM)遗忘机制的综述，聚焦基于梯度的遗忘方法及其安全性、鲁棒性和可验证性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是LLM遗忘机制，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM遗忘（unlearning）；应用场景：医疗、金融、教育、决策支持等安全关键系统；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，提及billion-parameter models（十亿参数级模型），但未给出具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是LLM遗忘而非模型轻量化。虽然提及"retraining billion-parameter models is computationally infeasible"，但这是作为遗忘动机的背景，并非轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。这是一篇综述论文，未提及实验设置。

Experiments: 这是一篇综述论文，摘要未报告实验结果、对比方法或消融实验。

Limitations: 摘要指出核心未解决问题：当前方法是否真正移除知识，还是仅阻止模型在普通提示条件下表达该知识。但该局限性与遥感目标检测轻量化无关。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴点极少。唯一可能的间接联系是：LLM遗忘中"knowledge distributed and entangled across parameters"的表述，与轻量化中参数冗余和剪枝策略有一定概念上的类比，但关联非常间接，不建议深入参考。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed in security-critical systems across healthcare, finance, education, and decision support, yet their inability to forget creates serious cybersecurity, privacy, and safety risks. Sensitive personal information, copyrighted material, hazardous domain knowledge, and memorized training data remain encoded across billions of parameters long after deployment, leaving models vulnerable to extraction, jailbreak attacks, membership inference, and regulatory non-compliance. Real-world incidents, from chatbots regenerating private information to fabricated legal citations producing direct legal and financial cost, place the problem at the center of the emerging-threats landscape rather than the realm of speculation. Because retraining billion-parameter models on revised corpora is computationally infeasible, and because knowledge within an LLM is distributed and entangled across parameters rather than localized to identifiable units, LLM unlearning has emerged as the principal cyber defense response, aiming to remove or suppress targeted knowledge from a trained model without retraining and without eroding what the model should still know. A central question, however, remains unresolved. Do current methods genuinely remove knowledge, or do they only stop the model from expressing it under ordinary prompting conditions? This survey examines LLM unlearning through the lens of security, robustness, and verifiable forgetting, with primary focus on gradient-based methods, which have come to dominate the field due to their compatibility with existing training pipelines and their scalability to billion-parameter models.

</details>


### [197] [Operator-Aware Mixed-Precision Tolerance Calibration for Tensor Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16228)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于GPU云运行数据挖掘的自动校准张量核正确性测试容差方法，通过分析元素级误差分布来替代人工设定的固定容差阈值，从而提升bug检测召回率。

Research relevance: 1分：与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于GPU张量核（tensor kernel）的正确性测试与容差校准，属于深度学习系统测试/软件工程领域，不涉及遥感图像、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分：与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：张量核正确性测试（tensor-kernel correctness test）；应用场景：GPU云运行环境下的深度学习算子测试；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及具体模型架构设计。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是测试容差校准，与模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）无关。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何部署相关指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等）。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：gpuemu corpus（26个条目，2种dtype，共8,076行结果）；评价指标：bug-detection recall（召回率）、false-positive count（误报数）、容差收紧倍数（如attention_triton fp16收紧2,184倍）。

Experiments: 在gpuemu corpus上实验，校准后的容差将bug检测召回率从73.2%（1,805/2,467）提升至82.4%（2,034/2,467），绝对提升9.3个百分点（+229个新检测）；误报从0增至20/1,882（+1.1个百分点）。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对gpuemu corpus中的26个测试条目和2种dtype，泛化性有限；2. 误报率有小幅上升（0→20）；3. 与遥感、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：在遥感在轨模型测试中，如果涉及FPGA/NPU上的算子正确性验证，可参考其数据驱动的容差校准思路，但与本课题核心方向（遥感目标检测模型轻量化）无直接关联。

Abstract: Most tensor-kernel correctness tests go through a fixed-shape all close-style check with hand-picked absolute and relative tolerances. The thresholds are copied across the corpus and rarely revisited. We mine the element-wise error distribution of every test case from accumulated cloud GPU runs across the 26-entry gpuemu corpus and 2 dtypes (8,076 result rows). We then ask one empirical question: what absolute tolerance would the kernel itself, observed under its correct implementation, justify?
  The answer is much tighter than the current hand-picked atol. The largest tightening is attention_triton fp16 at $2{,}184\times$. Restricted to the seven LLM-style buggy variants for which the corpus ships a paired correct counterpart, calibrated per-(op, dtype) tolerances raise bug-detection recall from 73.2% (1,805 of 2,467) to 82.4% (2,034 of 2,467), an absolute gain of 9.3 percentage points (+229 new detections). The control false-positive count rises from 0 to 20 out of 1,882 correct-control cases (+1.1 percentage points).

</details>


### [198] [RouteCost: A Production-Inspired Multi-Stage Framework for Pre-Order Shipping Cost Estimation in E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16230)
*Xianling Zeng,Zihan Yu,Sichen Zhao,Yalun Qi,Zhiming Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个面向电商物流的多阶段运费预估框架 RouteCost，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1 分。该论文研究的是电商物流中的运费预估问题，涉及需求预测、费用卡定价、残差修正和包裹合并推理，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化、在轨部署等方向完全无关。

Reading priority: 1 分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感相关任务。实际任务为电商物流运费预估，场景为电商平台，不涉及任何遥感传感器或目标类别。

Model architecture: RouteCost 多阶段框架：时间感知需求预测 → 费用卡基线定价 → Stage 2 残差修正 → 基于代理的包裹合并推理。与遥感目标检测网络架构无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 25万+订单、260个产品、18个月订单历史数据。评价指标为预测质量和聚合校准（aggregate calibration），与遥感目标检测的 mAP、F1、速度等指标无关。

Experiments: 在电商订单数据上验证了多阶段框架相比静态查找表和单体回归器的优势，保持了路线级别的可解释性。无遥感相关实验。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型压缩、检测或遥感相关技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于电商物流运筹优化领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题的研究范式、技术路线和应用场景均不匹配。

Abstract: Accurate pre-order shipping cost estimation is important in e-commerce because it affects price presentation, margin planning, and conversion. In practice, shipping cost is shaped not only by distance but also by destination demand mix, billable weight, dimensional pricing, surcharge triggers, and latent operational effects such as shipment consolidation. Static lookup methods therefore miss important sources of variation, while monolithic regressors may exploit strong but non-causal correlations. We propose RouteCost, a production-inspired multi-stage framework that decomposes the problem into time-aware demand forecasting, fee-card-informed baseline pricing, Stage 2 residual correction, and proxy-based box-consolidation inference. Route-level cost estimates are aggregated through a route-weighted expectation formulation to produce product-level shipping cost predictions. Across over 250,000 orders, 260 products, and 18 months of order history, the framework improves predictive quality and aggregate calibration while preserving route-level interpretability.

</details>


### [199] [Orthogonal Gradient Constraints Shape Noisy-Label Memorization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16231)
*Richard Mai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OrthoGrad方法，通过去除梯度中与当前权重向量平行的分量来抑制噪声标签下的过拟合，在MNIST和CIFAR-10上验证了其正则化效果，但发现效果具有数据规模依赖性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是噪声标签学习中的记忆驱动过拟合问题，属于通用深度学习/机器学习范畴，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：噪声标签下的图像分类；应用场景：通用图像分类（MNIST、CIFAR-10）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：手写数字、通用物体分类。

Model architecture: CNN（具体结构未详细说明）、ResNet-18。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。OrthoGrad是一种优化器层面的几何正则化方法，与模型压缩/加速（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MNIST（小数据量设置）、CIFAR-10；评价指标：测试准确率（test accuracy）、噪声标签拟合程度（corrupted-label fitting）；诊断指标：权重范数（weight norms）、梯度-权重余弦相似度（gradient-weight cosine similarity）。

Experiments: 在MNIST小数据量下，OrthoGrad对CNN提升测试准确率最明显，同时减少了对噪声标签的拟合；CIFAR-10 ResNet-18实验表明该方法可以改变记忆轨迹，但无法完全阻止噪声标签的最终记忆。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. OrthoGrad效果具有数据规模依赖性，在大数据量下效果减弱；3. 无法完全阻止噪声标签记忆，正则化能力有限。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文不涉及遥感场景、目标检测任务或任何轻量化技术，与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无关联。

Abstract: Modern neural networks can fit corrupted training labels, making noisy-label learning a useful setting for studying memorization-driven overfitting. Most regularization methods modify the objective, architecture, or data distribution; here we instead study a geometric intervention on the optimizer update itself. We evaluate OrthoGrad, which removes the component of each weight gradient parallel to the current weight vector, in noisy-label image classification. On MNIST with small-data regimes, OrthoGrad improves test accuracy most clearly for CNNs while reducing corrupted-label fitting. Mechanism diagnostics based on weight norms and gradient-weight cosine similarity suggest that the projection has the strongest effect when the raw gradient contains a nontrivial radial component, and becomes weaker in larger-data regimes where gradients are already nearly orthogonal to weights. Additional CIFAR-10 ResNet-18 experiments show that the method can alter memorization trajectories but does not prevent eventual noisy-label memorization. These results support orthogonal update constraints as a useful diagnostic for studying learning dynamics, while showing that OrthoGrad is regime-dependent rather than universally regularizing.

</details>


### [200] [From Weights to Words: Expressing and Editing Preference Model Inferences in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16232)
*Zachary Wojtowicz,Ayush Nayak,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"weights to words"方法，从高维选择数据中自动提取用自然语言描述的偏好维度，解决模型不可解释和偏好归因不明确的问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。研究的是人类偏好学习与可解释性，属于行为经济学/机器学习可解释性领域，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：人类偏好推断与可解释性建模；场景：道德困境、电影选择、葡萄酒偏好、LLM响应评估；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及在模型表征空间中学习偏好维度向量。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署，无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：道德困境（N=450）、电影选择（N=449）的人类偏好数据；评价指标：预测准确率（held-out choices）、用户偏好评价。

Experiments: 在两个预注册人类受试者实验中验证：(1) 用学习到的偏好基对模型正则化可提高预测准确率；(2) 加入受试者的结构化编辑可进一步提升准确率。用户更偏好该方法推断的偏好画像并认可其预测更准确。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设计。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文研究人类偏好可解释性，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: The growing use of statistical learning algorithms to infer human preferences from high-dimensional choice data runs up against a fundamental challenge: choice alternatives typically differ in many ways simultaneously, so it is generally unclear which factors actually drove an observed decision and should be credited as preferences. Compounding this problem, the opacity of these methods leaves human operators unable to inspect, contest, or correct models when they err. We introduce \emph{weights to words}, a method that takes a dataset of choice problems as input and automatically discovers a collection of domain-relevant preference dimensions, each described in natural language and paired with a vector in the model's representational space. These dimensions address both under-determination and opacity: they can be applied to concentrate attribution on a small set of meaningful factors, and they can externalize the model's inferences in natural language so that users can inspect and edit them in real time. We first qualitatively illustrate the method's versatility on four diverse domains: moral dilemmas, movies, wines, and free-form LLM responses. We then report two pre-registered human-subjects experiments, on moral dilemmas ($N=450$) and movie selection ($N=449$), that demonstrate its benefits for learning preference models: (1) regularizing a preference model toward the learned basis increases prediction accuracy on held-out choices, and (2) incorporating participants' structured edits further improves accuracy. In head-to-head comparisons, participants prefer the method's inferred preference profiles and endorse its predictions as more accurate.

</details>


### [201] [Token-Level Cross-Modal Transformer with Contrastive Multi-Task Learning for Breast Cancer Subtype Classification and Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16233)

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于精准肿瘤学（precision oncology）领域，研究多模态基因组与临床数据融合用于癌症亚型分类和生存预测。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等任何轻量化或部署技术。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：癌症亚型分类（cancer subtype classification）与生存预测（survival prediction）；场景：精准肿瘤学；模态：基因组数据（genomic modalities）+ 临床数据（clinical modalities）。与遥感/卫星/目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及现有方法将每种模态视为整体特征向量（monolithic feature vector），缺乏细粒度token级交互，且采用线性加权或后期平均进行跨模态融合。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等内容。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明实验结果、对比方法或消融实验。

Limitations: 该论文与本研究方向完全无关，其局限性（如模态融合方式粗粒度、分类与生存目标独立优化）属于生物医学信息学领域问题，对遥感在轨目标检测无参考价值。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究的是基因组+临床多模态融合的癌症分类与生存预测，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化在任务场景、数据模态、技术方法上均无交集。

Abstract: Integrating heterogeneous genomic and clinical modalities for joint cancer subtype classification and survival prediction remains a key challenge in precision oncology. Existing approaches suffer from three limitations: (1) they treat each modality as a monolithic feature vector, precluding fine-grained token-level interactions across modalities; (2) cross-modal fusion is typically performed through linear weighting or late averaging rather than structured token exchange; and (3) survival and classification objectives are optimized independently, missing a joint regularization signal.

</details>


### [202] [HantaWatch: Federated Learning for Hantavirus Genomic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16234)
*Shanika Iroshi Nanayakkara,Shiva Raj Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个用于汉坦病毒基因组监测的联邦学习框架HantaWatch，实现多实验室协同训练而不共享原始数据。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。研究主题是病毒基因组监测和联邦学习，属于生物信息学/公共卫生领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：病毒基因组序列分类（高风险筛查、爆发关联预测、进化枝分类、临床综合征分类）；场景：汉坦病毒监测；传感器/数据类型：基因组序列数据；目标类别：病毒序列类别。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提及k-mer特征提取、DU-FedProx优化、联邦学习框架。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文使用联邦学习（federated learning）进行隐私保护训练，但未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。任务为二分类和多分类，评价指标涉及预测性能、假阴性风险、更新稳定性，但未给出具体指标名称（如mAP、F1等）。

Experiments: 在二分类和多分类任务上进行了实验，支持高风险筛查、爆发预测、进化枝分类和临床综合征分类，同时平衡预测性能、假阴性风险和更新稳定性。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测技术。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于生物信息学领域的联邦学习应用，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Hantavirus genomic surveillance is limited by the distribution of sequence data, non-IID source heterogeneity, and constrained expert-review capacity. We propose HantaWatch, a federated learning framework that enables laboratories and surveillance sites to collaboratively train sequence-based models without sharing raw data. HantaWatch integrates k-mer feature extraction, source-aware federated client construction, adaptive DU-FedProx optimization, surveillance-specific model selection, and prediction-only triage. Experiments on binary and multi-class tasks show that HantaWatch supports high-risk screening, outbreak-associated prediction, clade classification, and clinical-syndrome categorization while balancing predictive performance, false-negative risk, and update stability. The framework converts model output into risk scores, confidence estimates, uncertainty flags, and ranked expert-review priorities. HantaWatch therefore provides a practical federated decision-support layer for decentralized Hantavirus surveillance, supporting expert prioritization without replacing laboratory or public-health interpretation.

</details>


### [203] [OpenMHC: Accelerating the Science of Wearable Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16235)
*Narayan Schuetz,Yuze Bai,Lianggang Pan,Edgar Eggert,Favour Nerrise,Juan Delgado-SanMartin,Max Rosenblattl,Milana Gurbanova,Mohammad Asadi,Anders Johnson,Paul Schmiedmayer,Dennis Wang,Allan Lawrie,Daniel Seung Kim,Xin Liu,Akshay Paruchuri,Ehsan Adeli,Euan Ashley,Kelly W. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发布了一个大规模可穿戴健康数据集OpenMHC及开源基准，用于可穿戴健康AI研究，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何模型压缩或部署技术。论文聚焦于可穿戴健康设备（wearable devices）的生理信号（心率、步数、睡眠等）数据集构建与基础模型开源，属于医疗健康/可穿戴计算领域，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文属于可穿戴健康计算领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：健康行为下游预测、多变量数据插补、时间序列预测。应用场景：可穿戴健康监测（Mobile and wearable devices）。传感器类型：可穿戴设备传感器（step count, heart rate, sleep, workouts等19个通道）。目标类别：无目标检测相关目标。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。仅提及"wearable foundation models"和"multivariate time series foundation models"，但未给出网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：OpenMyHeartCounts (OpenMHC)，包含>6000万小时可穿戴数据，19个传感器通道，169个关联变量，来自11,894名参与者。评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称。实验设置：三个评测轨道——健康行为下游预测、多变量数据插补、时间序列预测。

Experiments: 摘要仅说明对经典方法以及近期可穿戴和多变量时间序列基础模型进行了基准测试，未给出具体实验结果、对比方法或消融实验数据。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法评估其在遥感目标检测轻量化方面的局限性。从摘要本身看，该工作不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩，对在轨遥感任务无直接参考价值。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，无可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：该工作强调开源数据集和基准对推动领域发展的重要性，提示在遥感轻量化检测领域也可考虑构建标准化开源基准来促进公平对比。

Abstract: Mobile and wearable devices offer an unprecedented opportunity for continuous, passive health monitoring and active health coaching. However, the largest wearable datasets are not publicly available for research, and leading wearable foundation models trained on such datasets are rarely open-weight or come with reproducible training code. To accelerate open science in wearable health, we release OpenMyHeartCounts (OpenMHC), the largest and most comprehensive open-access wearable health dataset to date, alongside open-source implementations of recent wearable foundation models. OpenMHC, derived from over a decade of data collected through the My Heart Counts study app, includes >60 million hours of wearable data across 19 sensor channels (e.g., step count, heart rate, sleep, workouts) and up to 169 linked variables, including health, lifestyle, mood, and behavior from 11,894 consenting participants. Furthermore, we introduce a unified, open benchmark that enables standardized comparison of wearable health models across three tracks: health and behavior downstream prediction, multivariate data imputation, and time-series forecasting. We benchmark classical methods alongside recent wearable and multivariate time series foundation models. By open-sourcing data, code, and model weights at this unprecedented scale, we aim to democratize wearable health AI research and enable the community to drive open progress in this domain.

</details>


### [204] [The Failures of Marginal Influence-Based Attribution Methods for Global Time Series Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16236)
*Amadeo Tunyi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出时序模型的可解释性方法存在计算失配问题，并定义了DAG忠实性概念，与遥感目标检测及轻量化无关。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是通用时间序列模型的可解释性，未涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文未描述具体模型结构，仅提及SHAP等通用可解释性方法。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未提及任何数据集或评价指标。

Limitations: 论文完全聚焦于时序模型可解释性的理论缺陷（DAG忠实性），与遥感在轨目标检测和轻量化无任何交集，无法提供相关借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向完全无关，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署中的任何一项。

Abstract: Explainability methods for time series models predominantly produce flat attribution scores: they quantify the direct influence of a feature at a timestamp by a scalar. We prove that the dominant failure mode of such methods is not the scalar format itself but a fundamental computational mismatch: existing methods compute scores via marginal conditioning or off-manifold gradients, both of which conflate direct temporal dependencies with mediated ones under autocorrelation. We also define DAG-faithfulness: an explanation is DAG-faithful if the temporal dependency graph it encodes is Markov-equivalent to the temporal directed acyclic graph (DAG) implicitly learned by the model. Particularly, we observe that standard attribution methods, specifically SHAP, are not DAG-faithful in general, and that recent time-series-aware extensions inherit the same computational limitation.

</details>


### [205] [Quantizing Recursive Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16237)
*Thorir Mar Ingolfsson,Wajeeha Tahir,Anna Tegon,Lionnus Kesting,Gamze İslamoğlu,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MXInt4块级整数量化格式，解决递归推理模型中因4-bit量化误差累积导致的精度崩溃问题，在数独和ARC-AGI任务上恢复性能。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文聚焦于递归推理模型（如数独求解、ARC-AGI通用推理）的量化问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然量化方法（MXInt4块级量化）对轻量化有技术参考价值，但应用场景和任务类型与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向基本无关，仅量化策略（块级量化）有微弱技术参考价值，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：递归推理/约束求解（数独、ARC-AGI抽象推理）；应用场景：非遥感、非视觉；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 递归推理模型（recursive reasoning models），包括EqR（equilibrium model）等深度递归架构，使用weight-tied blocks多次迭代推理。

Lightweight method: 量化（quantization）：提出MXInt4块级整数量化格式（blockwise integer activation format），采用per-block scaling策略解决4-bit量化误差累积问题。与blockwise float格式对比，保持整数元素和2的幂次块尺度。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及8-bit量化可保持精度，4-bit per-tensor量化导致精度崩溃（数独从84.1%降至0.0%），而per-block scaling可恢复精度。未涉及星上/边缘部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：数独（Sudoku）求解任务、ARC-AGI（Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus）基准；评价指标：exact-solution accuracy（精确求解准确率）、cell-level accuracy（单元级准确率）。

Experiments: 主要发现：8-bit量化（integer或float）可保持精度；per-tensor 4-bit量化导致系统性偏差累积，数独精确求解准确率从84.1%降至0.0%（仅~25%单元格正确）；per-block scaling完全恢复精度。递归深度和复用次数影响量化敏感性，EqR模型最敏感，但块级量化可克服此脆弱性。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景，任务类型差异巨大；2. 仅针对递归推理模型的量化问题，未讨论卷积/Transformer等检测骨干网络的量化；3. 未提供计算效率指标（FLOPs、延迟、模型大小等）；4. 仅在数独和ARC-AGI上验证，未在视觉任务上测试。

Ideas for my research: 1. 块级量化策略（per-block scaling）可借鉴：在遥感目标检测模型的4-bit或更低比特量化中，避免per-tensor量化导致的精度崩溃，采用块级粒度控制量化误差；2. MXInt4格式的整数元素+2的幂次块尺度设计对FPGA/NPU部署友好，可探索在遥感检测模型上的适用性；3. 量化误差累积分析思路可迁移：递归/循环结构（如轻量检测头中的多次refinement）的量化敏感性需特别关注。

Abstract: Recursive reasoning models solve hard puzzles by applying compact, weight-tied blocks over many refinement steps. Because these blocks are reused many times, quantizing them creates a unique dynamical problem: the quantization error is incurred at every step. While 8-bit quantization (integer or float) preserves accuracy, moving to a per-tensor 4-bit format causes a systematic bias to accumulate. The ensuing drift catastrophically degrades exact-solution accuracy on Sudoku from 84.1% to 0.0% (only ~25% of cells correct). In this work, we show that this collapse is caused by activation-scaling granularity rather than bit-width or number format. Crucially, moving to per-block scaling completely restores the transition. To implement this, we apply MXInt4, a blockwise integer activation format, to recursive reasoning models. It is competitive with blockwise float formats on our tasks, while keeping integer elements and power-of-two block scales. Finally, recursion depth and reuse modulate quantization sensitivity, with the deepest architecture we test (the EqR equilibrium model) the most sensitive. Yet blockwise scaling overcomes this vulnerability, staying robust across these architectures and transferring to the open-ended ARC-AGI benchmark.

</details>
