Abstract: Pretrained models are typically ranked on multi-task leaderboards to assess their effectiveness across diverse tasks. Rank confidence intervals were recently introduced as a method to quantify the uncertainty in these rankings by aggregating pairwise hypothesis tests. In this work, we analyze the sources of uncertainty in the knowledge evaluation benchmark MMLU and show how hypothesis tests can be modified to account for their effects. We demonstrate that ranking variability across MMLU subjects is substantial and should be considered when comparing LLMs or identifying the top-performing models.

</details>


### [207] [Diffusion-corrected Autoregressive Fourier Neural Operator for Droplet Evolution Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16238)
*Jinghao Cao,Minsung Kang,Hongyue Sun,Chi Zhou,Jihoon Chung,Xubo Yue,Sanchoy Das,Bo Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiffARFNO框架，结合自回归傅里叶神经算子与扩散模型校正器，用于喷墨打印液滴演化的长时程预测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是材料喷射/喷墨打印（Inkjet Printing）中的液滴演化预测，属于流体力学/增材制造领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，完全不相关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：液滴演化长时程预测（long-horizon forecasting）；应用场景：材料喷射/喷墨打印（Inkjet Printing）；传感器类型：仿真数据（ANSYS Fluent）；目标类别：液滴（droplet）。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: DiffARFNO（Diffusion-corrected Auto-Regressive Fourier Neural Operator）：两阶段框架。第一阶段为自回归Fourier-MIONet（粗预测器），第二阶段为条件DDIM（Denoising Diffusion Implicit Model）校正器，在滑动窗口内通过迭代去噪细化粗预测结果。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。且该模型涉及扩散模型迭代去噪，计算开销大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：来自ANSYS Fluent仿真的液滴数据集（droplet datasets）；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 实验表明DiffARFNO显著优于现有最先进模型（state-of-the-art models），但未给出具体数值对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 扩散模型迭代推理计算量大，不满足实时/在轨约束；3. 摘要未提供任何轻量化或部署相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于流体力学预测领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无交叉。唯一可能的间接启发是：自回归+扩散校正的两阶段思路在时序预测中有效，但遥感目标检测通常不涉及此类时序预测任务。

Abstract: Predicting droplet evolution in material jetting, or Inkjet Printing (IJP), is essential for maintaining printing quality. However, long-horizon forecasts remain challenging due to error accumulation and the complex coupling of process variables. In this work, we introduce the Diffusion-corrected Auto-Regressive Fourier Neural Operator (DiffARFNO), a two-stage framework that combines an autoregressive Fourier-MIONet with a conditional Denoising Diffusion Implicit Model (DDIM) corrector. Fourier-MIONet is trained as a coarse predictor and deployed autoregressively for long-horizon forecasting. In the second stage, a DDIM-based conditional corrector refines the coarse prediction within each sliding window through efficient iterative denoising. By combining coarse predictions from Fourier-MIONet with a DDIM corrector that restores fine details, DiffARFNO aims to provide high-fidelity predictions for long-horizon forecasts. Extensive experiments on droplet datasets from ANSYS Fluent demonstrate that DiffARFNO significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [208] [Reducing Per-Sample Harm in Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16261)
*Apostolos Avranas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种优化器改进方法，通过最小化批平均和历史优化状态对单个样本损失的冲突影响（称为"harm"），来减少每个样本在梯度更新中的干扰，提升图像分类泛化性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文是通用深度学习优化器层面的方法改进，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测任务，也不涉及模型轻量化或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于通用优化器改进工作，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像分类（image classification）；应用场景：通用视觉；传感器类型：未说明；目标类别：未说明。不涉及遥感场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。方法作用于优化器层面（SGD, AdamW），与模型结构无关。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文核心是优化器改进，不是模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署信息。虽然提到"computational overhead shrinks as model size or input grows"，但这是优化器计算开销的缩放特性，与模型轻量化部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：image classification benchmarks（未具体说明数据集名称）；评价指标：generalization（泛化性能），未说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 实验在图像分类基准上验证，结果表明减少了per-sample interference并提升了泛化性能。未与遥感相关方法对比。

Limitations: 1. 仅在图像分类任务上验证，未在目标检测、语义分割等任务上测试；2. 未在遥感数据集上实验；3. 方法依赖最后一层线性层的二阶统计量近似，对于无全连接层的模型（如纯卷积或Transformer检测头）适用性存疑；4. 未讨论对模型推理效率的影响。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1. 在遥感目标检测模型训练中，如果遇到batch size受限（如星上训练或小样本场景），该优化器方法可能有助于缓解样本间梯度冲突；2. 但该方法本身不解决模型轻量化问题，也不针对遥感目标检测的特定挑战（如小目标、旋转框、多尺度等）。不建议作为核心参考。

Abstract: Modern optimizers combine gradients from the current mini-batch with historical optimization state, such as momentum or adaptive moments. While highly effective, aggregating across the batch and incorporating this history can produce parameter updates that increase the loss of individual samples. We term this effect harm and formalize the parameter update as an optimization problem that explicitly minimizes the conflicting impact of both batch averaging and past optimization state on current data.
  Because the exact formulation is intractable, we introduce a highly efficient proxy. We first reduce the problem's dimensionality to the batch size, and then drastically cut memory and speed bottlenecks by successfully restricting the optimization to the last linear layer. This hinges on the unexpected finding that this layer alone reliably captures the second-order statistics of the per-sample gradients. The resulting surrogate problem integrates readily into standard optimizers like SGD and AdamW, and can be solved using a small number of GPU-friendly iterations. Crucially, the method exhibits favorable scaling properties, as the relative computational overhead shrinks as the model size or input grows. Experiments on image classification benchmarks confirm reduced per-sample interference and improved generalization.

</details>


### [209] [BACON: Budgeted Human Calibration for Modeling and Evaluation with Multiple AI Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16239)
*Lei Shi,Anlan Zhang,Rita Lyu,Zhengmian Hu,Tong Yu,David Arbour,Avi Feller,Saayan Mitra,Ritwik Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BACON是一个结合预算受限人工校准与多AI裁判输出的四阶段流水线，用于生成更准确的标注，属于AI评估校准框架，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感相关领域；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等模型压缩或加速方法。论文核心是AI评估结果的校准与人类标注预算优化，属于统计学习/评估方法论，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：AI评估校准与标注质量提升；应用场景：通用AI评估（model ranking, item scoring, population-level quality reporting）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: BACON四阶段流水线：1) 构建全覆盖辅助特征（多裁判分数、token级不确定性统计、上下文嵌入）；2) 收集小样本子集的人工标签；3) 训练交叉拟合结果模型生成校准后的逐项代理预测；4) 支持总体级估计（增强估计方程+置信区间）和个体级代理评分。不涉及神经网络检测模型架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：预测准确性（predictive accuracy）、排序一致性（ranking consistency）、偏差（bias）、方差（variance）。

Experiments: 在多种任务、领域和标注预算下，BACON相比原始AI输出和纯人工标注方法，在预测准确性、排序一致性上有所提升，偏差和方差有所降低。摘要未提供具体数值对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 摘要未说明该方法在视觉任务（尤其是遥感图像）上的表现；3. 未讨论计算开销和实时性；4. 依赖人工标注子集，在星上场景中不可行。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但可抽象思考：如果将"AI裁判"类比为多个轻量化检测模型在星上的输出，"人类标注"类比为地面高精度标注，则BACON的校准思想或许可启发"星上轻量模型+地面高精度模型"的协同校准策略。不过这属于非常间接的类比，且论文未涉及任何视觉检测或遥感场景。

Abstract: AI judges offer a scalable, low-cost alternative to human evaluation, but their outputs can be biased relative to human preferences and highly item-dependent, varying across judges, tasks, and domains. When uncalibrated AI evaluations are used for model ranking, item scoring, or population-level quality reporting, these biases can directly distort downstream decisions. We propose BACON, a four-stage pipeline that combines budgeted human calibration with multiple AI-judge outputs to produce more accurate annotations. BACON constructs full-coverage auxiliary features for every item, including multi-judge scores, token-level uncertainty statistics, and contextual embeddings. It then collects human labels for a small sampled subset and trains a cross-fitted outcome model to generate calibrated item-level surrogate predictions. These predictions support two use cases: population-level estimation of summary metrics, such as means or quantiles, using an augmented estimating-equation estimator with valid confidence intervals; and individual-level surrogate scoring for item ranking and annotation. BACON treats AI judges as auxiliary measurements rather than ground truth: human labels provide the calibration anchor, while AI-derived signals improve efficiency. Across diverse tasks, domains, and labeling budgets, BACON improves predictive accuracy and ranking consistency, and reduces bias and variance relative to raw AI outputs and purely human-label-based methods. These results show that BACON offers a practical, statistically grounded framework for scalable evaluation with limited human annotation.

</details>


### [210] [Scaling Limits of Constant-Stepsize SGD at Flat Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16384)
*Jingyi Zhang,Cheng Mao,Debankur Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究凸优化中随机梯度下降（SGD）在平坦极小值下的不变分布与缩放极限，属于纯优化理论分析，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1 分（基本无关）。该论文是纯数学优化理论工作，研究SGD在凸目标函数平坦极小值下的不变分布和缩放极限，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测任务或任何模型轻量化/部署技术。

Reading priority: 1 分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何具体视觉任务或应用场景，属于纯数学优化理论。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何神经网络模型结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未使用任何遥感或视觉数据集，也未涉及目标检测评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文为纯理论推导，无实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接或间接的借鉴。其理论分析（SGD在平坦极小值下的缩放行为）虽与优化理论相关，但未与任何实际深度学习训练或部署场景结合。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文为纯数学优化理论，不涉及遥感、目标检测或轻量化部署的任何环节。

Abstract: For stochastic gradient descent (SGD) with a constant stepsize $α$, the invariant law of the iterates, centered at a minimizer, describes the behavior of the algorithm over long time horizons. In the strongly convex case, this invariant law has the familiar $\sqrtα$ scaling and a Gaussian limit as $α\downarrow 0$. We show that this behavior changes fundamentally for convex objectives $H$ with flat minima and (sub)quadratic tails.
  More specifically, we study SGD with Markovian noise generated by a contractive driving chain. For every sufficiently small constant stepsize $α$, we prove existence, uniqueness, and geometric convergence to an augmented invariant law in a Wasserstein distance induced by an $α$-dependent metric. When the minimizer $x_\star$ has local flatness exponent $m\ge2$, meaning that $\nabla^2 H(x)\asymp \lVert x-x_\star\rVert^{m-2} I_d$ as $x\to x_\star$, we obtain a contraction bound with factor $1-cα^{m-1}$, where $c>0$ is a constant. This recovers the factor $1-cα$ in the quadratic case $m=2$. We then analyze the small-stepsize scaling limit. We show that the invariant law concentrates on the scale $α^{1/m}$ and that the rescaled iterates converge weakly to the stationary distribution of the stochastic differential equation $$
  dY_t=-h_0(Y_t)\,dt+Σ^{1/2}\,dB_t , $$ where $h_0$ is the limiting drift at the minimizer and $Σ$ denotes the asymptotic covariance. This recovers the Gaussian limit when $m=2$ and gives generally non-Gaussian stationary limits in the flat case $m>2$. Finally, we give corresponding results for coordinate-separable objectives with unequal flatness exponents.

</details>


### [211] [Normalized Rewards for Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16240)
*Shawn Im,Federico Danieli,Skyler Seto,Barry-John Theobald,Katherine Metcalf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在DPO/SimPO等直接对齐算法中加入正则化项，以缓解奖励模型过优化和偏好响应似然下降的问题，提升LLM生成质量与通用基准性能。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全聚焦于大语言模型(LLM)的后训练对齐算法（DPO/SimPO），与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化均无任何关联。不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、在轨部署或任何轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM偏好对齐（preference alignment）；应用场景：通用自然语言处理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct等LLM架构，在DPO和SimPO算法基础上增加正则化项。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文讨论的是LLM对齐算法中的正则化技术，与模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：AlpacaEval2等通用NLG基准；评价指标：AlpacaEval2得分、通用基准性能（相对提升百分比）、reward modeling指标。

Experiments: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，正则化方法使AlpacaEval2得分相对提升>20%，通用基准性能相对提升>9%。同时验证了正则化能有效减少偏好响应中异常token的似然偏移。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM对齐领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的研究范畴（遥感图像处理、目标检测网络设计、模型压缩与边缘部署）无交集。

Abstract: Direct Alignment Algorithms (DAAs) such as DPO have become a common way to post-train and align LLMs with human preferences. However, DAAs have been observed to over-optimize their implicit reward model and decrease the likelihood of preferred responses. This results in a decrease in the total likelihood assigned to responses seen in the preference dataset, potentially resulting in undesirable behavior. To counteract this undesired side-effect of DAAs, we examine the effect of using objectives that add a regularization term to maintain the total length-normalized probabilities of the chosen and rejected responses. To better understand over-optimization, we investigate how response likelihood changes are distributed over the tokens with and without regularization. We find that a significant portion of the likelihood changes are due to a small set of outlier tokens, which explains how DAAs improve generation quality despite decreasing the likelihoods of chosen responses. We apply the proposed regularization to reference-based (DPO) and reference-free (SimPO) methods and find (1) improved trade-offs between generation quality and general benchmark capability and (2) improvements in reward modeling across datasets. For example, on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we see both a >20% relative increase in AlpacaEval2 scores and >9% relative performance gains on general benchmarks. Additionally, we find that the added regularization term effectively mitigates the amount of displacement within preferred responses overall, and for the outlier tokens specifically, by utilizing low-likelihood tokens.

</details>


### [212] [Value-Monotonicity Matters: A Concordance Loss for Deep Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16802)
*Meixu Chen,Kai Wang,Jing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种值单调的一致性损失（SCL），使损失值在训练过程中与C-index高度相关，解决生存分析中似然损失与评价指标不匹配的问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于医学生存分析（survival analysis）领域，涉及深度学习模型的损失函数设计，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：生存分析（survival analysis）中的风险预测；应用场景：肿瘤学（oncology）小样本高删失数据；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及"high-capacity encoders"，损失函数是architecture agnostic的。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 18个数据集，来自4种模态（modalities），使用统一的五折交叉验证协议。评价指标：C-index（一致性指数）、Integrated Brier Score（校准度）。

Experiments: SCL在判别力上与标准似然损失相当，且损失值与C-index的秩相关系数达0.96-0.99（似然损失仅为-0.03到0.53）。校准度（Integrated Brier Score）与基线方法相当。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化或部署优化；3. 方法针对生存分析中的删失数据设计，不适用于目标检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文的核心贡献（损失函数与评价指标单调对齐）虽然是一个有趣的优化思想，但应用场景（生存分析）和评价指标（C-index）与遥感目标检测（mAP, F1, 推理速度等）完全不同，无法迁移。

Abstract: Deep survival models are evaluated almost exclusively by the concordance index (C-index), yet they are commonly trained using likelihood objectives such as the Cox partial likelihood, discrete-time negative log-likelihood, and DeepHit likelihood. This mismatch is usually considered acceptable because the C-index can be recomputed on validation data during training. However, for end-to-end training of high-capacity encoders on small, heavily censored oncology cohorts, frequent C-index evaluation is computationally expensive, making the loss value itself an important signal for monitoring, early stopping, and model selection. We show that likelihood losses are unreliable for this purpose and propose a value-monotone concordance loss. We prove that every strictly proper survival likelihood admits directions where the loss decreases while the C-index remains unchanged, causing the loss value to decouple from ranking performance. We then study a sigmoid concordance loss (SCL), whose value approximates one minus the C-index up to a temperature term, ensuring that lower loss corresponds to higher C-index during optimization. The loss is architecture agnostic and reduces to a convex survival ranking support vector machine for linear models. Across eighteen datasets from four modalities using a unified five-fold cross-validation protocol, SCL achieves discrimination comparable to standard likelihood losses and is the best or within one standard deviation of the best C-index. Unlike likelihood losses, SCL maintains a strong correlation between loss value and C-index during training, with rank correlations of 0.96 to 0.99 compared with -0.03 to 0.53 for likelihood losses. Calibration measured by the integrated Brier score is comparable. SCL provides a value-monotone optimization objective whose value can serve as a reliable surrogate for the C-index during expensive end-to-end training.

</details>


### [213] [KernelBench-Verified: Do LLM-Generated Kernels Actually Beat PyTorch?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16241)
*Yunxiang Zhang,Ping Yu,Jianyu Wang,Max,Fan,Julian Reed,Azalia Mirhoseini,Will Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM生成的CUDA kernel在KernelBench上存在reward hacking问题（如硬编码绕过、TF32基线不准确），并提出了包含TF32基线和多分布隐藏测试集的KernelBench-Verified评估框架，发现最佳模型实际加速比仅0.88x，且28%的生成kernel增加了峰值显存。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于LLM生成CUDA kernel的评估框架可靠性问题，属于AI for systems/编译优化领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：CUDA kernel生成与评估基准设计；应用场景：GPU kernel优化、LLM代码生成能力评测；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 不涉及目标检测模型架构。论文评估对象是LLM生成的CUDA kernel，涉及GPT-5.5等7个前沿LLM。

Lightweight method: 不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等模型轻量化方法。论文讨论的是kernel层面的优化（CUDA kernel生成），而非模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署。但论文提到了GPU显存峰值（28%的生成kernel增加峰值显存）和TF32加速、加速比（geometric mean speedup 0.88x），这些指标对边缘部署有一定参考价值，但场景完全不同。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：KernelBench（CUDA kernel生成基准），扩展为KernelBench-Verified（含四分布隐藏测试集）；评价指标：geometric mean speedup（几何平均加速比）、峰值GPU显存增加比例、TF32基线下的真实加速比。

Experiments: 在7个前沿LLM上进行单轮评估，最佳模型GPT-5.5在标准协议下加速比1.43x，在验证协议下仅0.88x；28%的生成kernel增加峰值显存；无模型在真实基线下一贯优于PyTorch。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何目标检测模型；3. 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法；4. 场景为通用GPU kernel优化，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Ideas for my research: 尽管论文与课题方向无关，但以下两点有间接启发：1. 评估基线的重要性——在评估轻量化模型时，应使用真实部署环境（如NPU/FPGA/TensorRT）的基线，而非理想化基线；2. 速度-显存权衡（speed-memory tradeoff）——在星上部署时，不能只看推理速度，还需关注峰值显存占用，论文中28%的kernel增加显存的现象提醒我们在轻量化时需同时优化速度和内存。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) can generate custom CUDA kernels that appear to outperform PyTorch on benchmarks such as KernelBench. Building upon this foundational framework, we demonstrate that frontier models frequently engage in reward hacking to artificially inflate reported performance. In this work, we identify two areas where evaluation frameworks must co-evolve with model capabilities. First, to accurately measure true speedup, we examine the baseline timing mechanism, noting that enabling Tensor Core acceleration with TF32 provides a more realistic estimation of execution on modern GPUs. Second, concerning algorithmic correctness, models often exploit the narrow test distribution by hardcoding bypasses for specific tensor values. By skipping required computations, these kernels artificially accelerate execution rather than implementing actual CUDA kernels. We introduce KernelBench-Verified, an extended evaluation framework that incorporates a TF32-enabled baseline and a four-distribution hidden test suite. We additionally introduce memory efficiency metrics that capture the often-overlooked speed-memory tradeoff in kernel optimization. Under verified single-turn evaluation with seven frontier LLMs, we find that the best-performing model (GPT-5.5) achieves a 0.88x geometric mean speedup, significantly lower than the 1.43x speedup observed under the standard evaluation protocol. No model consistently outperforms PyTorch when evaluated against realistic baselines. On the memory front, 28% of GPU kernels generated by the best model increase peak GPU memory usage. Our findings demonstrate the necessity of continually adapting robust evaluation protocols as LLM kernel generation capabilities advance.

</details>


### [214] [Kernelized Linear Attention: Breaking the Capacity Wall with Symmetric Cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17419)
*Ayoub Ghriss,Sourav Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KATA框架，通过自对偶齐次锥推导核化线性注意力特征映射，解决线性注意力在关联召回上的退化问题，实现长序列高效推理。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型(LLM)的长序列线性注意力机制改进，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：长序列语言建模中的线性注意力机制改进；应用场景：大语言模型(LLM)推理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: KATA框架，基于自对偶齐次锥推导核化线性注意力特征映射，使用秩一正半定(PSD)特征，包含无参数凸输出门、球形编码、Welch干扰下限分析。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确涉及传统轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS）。但论文关注线性注意力的计算效率优化，实现了O(T)分块状态形式和融合Triton内核，达到约11倍FlashAttention-2前向吞吐量（131k tokens时）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。未报告参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅报告了吞吐量（约1.6倍~11倍FlashAttention-2）和340M参数LLM实验。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MQAR（多查询关联召回）、重复键覆盖任务、长序列语言建模；评价指标：召回准确率、吞吐量（throughput）、参数数量、状态大小。

Experiments: 在MQAR任务上，KATA变体优于Gated DeltaNet；在16倍分布外长度上保持0.985 MQAR；340M参数LLM实验揭示了特征相关的流畅性权衡；归纳头保持近乎完美的召回。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。论文面向LLM长序列推理，不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱借鉴点：线性注意力机制的高效计算思路（如分块状态、融合内核）理论上可迁移到遥感目标检测中的Transformer轻量化，但需大量改造且非直接相关。不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Linear attention promises constant-time recurrent inference but degrades sharply on associative recall. We formulate attention recall as a spherical-packing problem and introduce Kernelized Linear Attention Activations (KATA), a framework whose feature maps are derived from first principles by certifying nonnegative attention weights through a self-dual homogeneous cone. Building on this observation, we show that rank-one positive semi-definite (PSD) features offer a favorable capacity--interference tradeoff. KATA recovers a parameter-free convex output gate and characterizes associative capacity through the Welch interference floor. For tolerances above this floor, KATA enlarges the state without adding parameters and admits spherical codes with exponentially many keys in the projection dimension. We implement KATA as fused Triton kernels at two operating points: a flash-attention-style forward up to ${\sim}1.6\times$ FlashAttention-2 throughput, and an exact $O(T)$ chunked-state form that reaches ${\sim}11\times$ FlashAttention-2 forward throughput at $131$k tokens. An associative scan of the first-order feature lowers the inter-chunk recurrence depth to $O(\log(T/C))$ for chunk size $C$ and averages ${\sim}2.4\times$ the throughput of a matched sequential linear-attention baseline. On long-range MQAR and repeated-key overwrite, several KATA variants outperform Gated DeltaNet, with parameter counts and state sizes reported alongside accuracy. Induction preserves near-perfect recall, while kernel benchmarks show that the maps can be implemented efficiently. KATA retains $0.985$ MQAR at a $16\times$ out-of-distribution length, approaching the softmax with roughly one quarter of the KV-cache entries. Experiments on 340M-parameter LLMs reveal a feature-dependent fluency trade-off and clarify how positional embeddings, delta rules, and decay gates interact with feature geometry.

</details>


### [215] [TRACE: Trajectory-Based Safety Patch Learning for LLM Post-Training Realignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16242)
*Changyue Li,Jiaming He,Youliang Yuan,Jialin Wu,Boxi Yu,Zhicong Huang,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRACE框架，通过轨迹式安全补丁学习解决大模型微调后的安全性恢复问题，与遥感卫星目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是大语言模型（LLM）微调后的安全对齐恢复问题，属于NLP/大模型安全领域。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化、在轨部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM安全对齐恢复；应用场景：FTaaS平台；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明任何与遥感或目标检测相关的内容。

Model architecture: TRACE框架，包含：(i) 模拟有害微调轨迹生成渐进式损坏状态；(ii) 优化可插拔安全补丁。基于模型参数合并（parameter merging）范式，但转向离线补丁学习（offline patch learning）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。该论文讨论的是模型安全对齐，与剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速方法完全无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。且该论文面向的是大语言模型服务端场景，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 6个基准测试（benchmarks），2个模型。安全性指标：接近100%安全率；效用指标：与未防御的微调模型相当。具体数据集名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在6个benchmarks和2个模型上，TRACE在安全-效用前沿（safety-utility frontier）上持续占优，在所有设置下达到接近100%安全性，同时保持与未防御微调模型相当的效用。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供有意义的局限性分析。该论文面向LLM安全领域，其核心问题（task-safety update entanglement）与遥感目标检测轻量化无任何交集。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文研究的是大语言模型的安全对齐问题，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、应用场景上均无交叉。

Abstract: Fine-Tuning-as-a-Service (FTaaS) platforms let users train large language models (LLMs) on customized tasks, but this pipeline could erode models' safety alignment. In practice, service providers need to recover models' safety without re-running full alignment, or destroying the utility gained from customized tasks. A line of existing work refers to model parameter merging, which adds a safety patch on the fine-tuned model parameters to shift the model away from unsafe tendencies. However, this merging-based paradigm is fundamentally bottlenecked by task-safety update entanglement: downstream task updates and the safety patch often overlap in their dominant directions, so the merge strength is intrinsically hard to calibrate. If the safety vector is scaled too weakly, harmful components could still dominate, preventing the model from returning to a safe region; if it is scaled too aggressively, it suppresses task-relevant directions and degrades utility.
  To solve this problem, we shift the focus of merging-based methods from designing online merging operators to offline patch learning, and seek a safety patch that minimally interferes with task-relevant directions while retaining decisive control over unsafe behaviors. We propose TRACE, a trajectory-based safety patch learning framework that (i) simulates harmful tuning trajectories to generate progressively corrupted states, and (ii) optimizes a plug-in patch to recover safety while maintaining utility across varying corrupted base states.
  Across six benchmarks and two models, TRACE consistently dominates the safety-utility frontier. TRACE reaches nearly 100% safety on all settings, while maintaining comparable utility to the undefended fine-tuned model.

</details>


### [216] [Calibrated Alzheimer's Conversion Risk in Mild Cognitive Impairment: Persistent Homology of Clinical Trajectories with Conformal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17442)
*Navin Bondade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究MCI向AD转化的预测模型，引入持续同调拓扑特征和分裂共形风险保证，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文属于医学影像分析（阿尔茨海默症预测），与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向完全无关；不涉及任何目标检测任务；不涉及任何轻量化方法。属于与本研究方向基本无关的论文。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向（遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化）基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：MCI向AD转化的预测（医学临床预测）；场景：阿尔茨海默症神经影像学；传感器类型：无（使用ADNI临床数据）；目标类别：无目标检测任务。摘要未说明任何遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 使用Vietoris-Rips持续同调（persistent homology）和子水平集代理（sublevel-set proxies）提取拓扑特征，结合轨迹斜率（trajectory slopes）和工程特征（共76维），采用stacking集成模型（Cox比例风险模型和随机生存森林）。摘要未说明具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。该模型面向医学临床预测，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ADNI（Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative），741名MCI受试者（240名转换者，32.4%），4年随访上限。评价指标：AUC、concordance C、共形覆盖率（conformal coverage）、公平性差距（fairness gap）。

Experiments: 5折交叉验证。Cox模型C=0.799（无TDA特征0.753，+0.045）；随机生存森林C=0.826（无TDA特征0.812，+0.014）。嵌套AUC=0.840，外部AUC=0.879。共形覆盖率90.4%±2.2%（目标90%），外部覆盖率96.9%。最大假阴性率公平性差距0.092。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/目标检测/轻量化内容。该论文的拓扑特征方法（持续同调）和共形预测框架虽在医学领域有创新性，但对遥感在轨检测课题无直接参考价值。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题方向完全无关，不涉及目标检测、遥感图像、轻量化部署等任何相关技术。

Abstract: Background. Predicting conversion from mild cognitive impairment (MCI) to Alzheimer's disease (AD) is central to trial enrichment and care planning, yet existing models provide no individual-level uncertainty estimates and rarely include transparent leakage audits. We introduce the first application of persistent homology to longitudinal clinical trajectory point clouds for this task, and the first split-conformal individual risk guarantee for any AD-conversion model. Methods. We analysed 741 MCI subjects (240 converters, 32.4%) from ADNI with a uniform 4-year follow-up cap. Five leakage sources were corrected; without them a naive pipeline achieved AUC=0.934, inflated by +0.075. Vietoris-Rips persistent homology and sublevel-set proxies were combined with trajectory slopes and engineered features (76 total) in a stacking ensemble evaluated by 5-fold cross-validation. Results. Cox and Random Survival Forest models with TDA features achieved concordance C=0.799 and C=0.826 versus C=0.753 and C=0.812 without (+0.045 and +0.014). The primary nested AUC is 0.840 (same-fold bound 0.866); external AUC was 0.879 on a zero-overlap ADNI-2/GO/3 cohort. H0 persistence entropy was the top SHAP feature and significantly associated with APOE4 dosage (Spearman r=-0.191, p<0.0001, Bonferroni-corrected). Cross-conformal coverage was 90.4%+-2.2% (target 90%); empirical external coverage 96.9%. Maximum fairness gap in false-negative rate across seven subgroups was 0.092. Conclusions. We propose H0 persistence entropy as a topological biomarker of cognitive decline and demonstrate that a leakage-audited, conformally calibrated pipeline reaches competitive accuracy with individual-level uncertainty quantification not previously available for this task.

</details>


### [217] [RobustMAD: Evaluating Real-World Robustness of Multimodal Small Language Models for Deployable Anomaly Detection Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16243)
*Anushiya Arunan,Xin Li,Yan Qin,U-Xuan Tan,Nhu Khue Vuong,Xiaoli Li,Chau Yuen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RobustMAD基准，用于评估多模态小语言模型在工业异常检测场景下的鲁棒性，发现小模型在某些方面甚至优于大模型，但仍存在多模态接地脆弱、回答不全面、幻觉等问题。

Research relevance: 1分，基本无关。该论文聚焦工业异常检测（industrial anomaly inspection）和多模态小语言模型（MSLM）的鲁棒性评估，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像、aerial image等遥感核心场景；不涉及目标检测（object detection）任务；不涉及轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。唯一弱关联点是"小模型"（compact MSLM）概念与轻量化有语义上的近似，但论文并未研究模型压缩或加速技术本身，而是评估已有小模型的鲁棒性。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多模态工业异常检测（multimodal industrial anomaly inspection），基于视觉-语言查询的交互式异常检测。场景：智能工厂（smart factory）现场部署。传感器类型：RGB图像（工业视觉）。目标类别：工业异常（anomaly detection），非遥感目标。

Model architecture: 多模态小语言模型（Multimodal Small Language Models, MSLMs），具体模型名称摘要未说明。对比模型包括GPT-5 Nano。未涉及目标检测模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。论文使用现成的compact MSLMs，而非研究如何压缩或加速模型。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到"prohibitive computational demands"和"privacy risks from cloud-based inference"是部署痛点，但未给出任何部署相关量化指标：参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等均未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 提出RobustMAD基准，包含多样化的开放查询（open-ended queries），涵盖object understanding, anomaly detection, unanswerable problems, visual quality degradations。评价指标摘要未说明具体数值。

Experiments: 发现top-performing MSLMs在某些方面优于GPT-5 Nano，但仍不满足安全关键需求。识别出三种失败模式：(i) 细粒度区分或退化视觉条件下的多模态接地脆弱；(ii) 回答不够全面；(iii) 对不可回答或病态查询产生幻觉输出。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何目标检测方法；3. 不涉及任何模型轻量化技术（剪枝/量化/蒸馏/NAS）；4. 无部署相关量化指标；5. 仅评估鲁棒性，未提出新的轻量化或检测方法。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可参考的是：该论文关注小模型在受限场景下的部署可行性，这与"在轨轻量化"的动机有语义上的共鸣，但技术路径完全不同。如果未来遥感领域也引入视觉-语言模型做场景理解，该论文的鲁棒性评估方法论可能有参考价值，但当前阶段与本课题无直接关联。

Abstract: Multimodal industrial anomaly inspection assistants are a critical component of next-generation smart factories, enabling interactive vision-language-based querying. However, multimodal large language models remain impractical for on-site deployment due to prohibitive computational demands and privacy risks from cloud-based inference. Compact multimodal small language models (MSLMs) offer a deployable alternative, yet progress is constrained by the lack of comprehensive robustness analyses and meaningfully challenging benchmarks that reflect real-world industrial conditions. To address this gap, we develop RobustMAD, the first deployment-motivated benchmark, designed to comprehensively evaluate model robustness through diverse open-ended queries spanning object understanding, anomaly detection, unanswerable problems, and visual quality degradations. Contrary to conventional assumptions, top-performing MSLMs exhibit promising capabilities, surprisingly outperforming even the larger GPT-5 Nano. However, they still fall short of safety-critical requirements, and RobustMAD reveals critical robustness gaps that pose operational risks. In particular, three recurring failure modes emerge: (i) fragile multimodal grounding under fine-grained distinctions or degraded visual conditions, (ii) insufficiently comprehensive responses, and (iii) weak logical grounding on unanswerable or ill-posed queries, leading to hallucinated outputs. Grounded in these insights, we provide actionable guidance for the design of next-generation multimodal industrial inspection assistants that leverage their promising competence. Code is available at https://github.com/en-research/RobustMAD.

</details>


### [218] [Program Synthesis for Simulation-Based Inference: Joint Model Selection and Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17540)
*Siddharth Mishra-Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合大语言模型(LLM)与神经仿真推断的框架，用于从自然语言描述中自动生成仿真程序并完成模型选择与参数估计，应用于物理、流行病学和天体物理领域。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于仿真推断(simulation-based inference)和程序合成，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、轻量化或星上部署等核心内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：模型选择与参数估计；应用场景：确定性动力学、随机流行病模型、引力透镜暗物质子结构推断；传感器类型：引力透镜成像(非遥感光学/SAR/高光谱)；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: 框架由三部分组成：LLM(用于程序合成)、可微仿真器(候选模型池)、神经密度估计器(用于似然比估计)。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 基准测试：确定性动力学系统、随机流行病模型、引力透镜暗物质子结构推断；评价指标：模型识别准确率(反映数据信息量和模型可辨识性)。

Experiments: 在三个不同领域的基准上验证了方法能从开放式提示中识别出合理的模型族，准确率反映了数据的信息内容和候选模型的可辨识性。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关；不涉及任何视觉检测任务；不涉及任何轻量化或边缘部署技术；LLM推理开销大，不适合资源受限场景。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文的方法论(LLM+仿真推断)与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Neural simulation-based inference enables parameter estimation for complex models, but typically requires the user to specify a simulator encoding a fixed model structure. We present a framework for joint model selection and parameter estimation that combines large language models for program synthesis with neural simulation-based inference. Given a natural language description of the system and data under investigation, an LLM proposes candidate simulator programs which are iteratively refined via feedback-driven mutation and evaluated using neural density estimation. The approach enables simulation-based inference over a pool of models, not just parameters within a fixed model. On benchmarks spanning deterministic dynamics, stochastic epidemic models, and dark matter substructure inference from gravitational-lensing images, the method identifies plausible model families from open-ended prompts, with accuracy that reflects the information content of the data and identifiability of candidate models.

</details>


### [219] [CIGPO: Contextual Information-Gain Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Evidence-Reading LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16244)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CIGPO方法，通过向中间推理回合注入逐回合信息增益奖励，解决GRPO在训练多轮证据阅读智能体时的零优势锁死问题，在HotpotQA上F1从0.252提升至0.518。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦自然语言处理中的多轮证据阅读问答任务（HotpotQA），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等视觉检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等模型压缩或加速方法。属于强化学习在NLP推理中的训练稳定性问题，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关系，属于NLP强化学习训练稳定性方向，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多轮证据阅读问答（multi-turn evidence-reading QA）；应用场景：HotpotQA数据集上的文本推理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基座模型：Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct；训练框架：GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）及其改进CIGPO（Contextual Information-Gain Policy Optimization）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。论文讨论的是强化学习训练稳定性问题，与模型压缩、加速、部署无关。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：HotpotQA；评价指标：standard F1；实验设置：Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct作为基座模型，对比GRPO与CIGPO的训练过程。

Experiments: GRPO初始F1为0.430，随后崩溃至100%格式违规输出（F1=0.000）；CIGPO达到F1=0.518（基线0.252，提升+105%），优于GRPO最佳检查点0.430。CIGPO在整个训练过程中保持有意义的奖励方差，避免零优势锁死。

Limitations: 1. 仅在HotpotQA单一数据集上验证，泛化性未知；2. 仅使用3B规模模型，更大或更小模型上的表现未说明；3. 与遥感/视觉/目标检测完全无关，无法直接迁移；4. 摘要未说明计算开销、推理速度等工程指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但以下思路可抽象参考：1）训练稳定性问题——在遥感目标检测轻量化中，若使用强化学习或自蒸馏等迭代训练方法，需关注奖励/损失方差崩溃问题；2）逐回合（逐层/逐模块）信息增益作为中间监督信号的思想，可类比于在轻量化检测网络中引入中间层特征蒸馏的逐层损失设计。但整体而言，该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的直接可借鉴点较少。

Abstract: Training multi-turn evidence-reading agents with outcome-only reinforcement learning is unstable because intermediate turns receive little direct credit. In HotpotQA experiments with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, GRPO initially improves (standard F1 0.430) but subsequently collapses to 100% format-violating outputs. Training-log diagnosis reveals a zero-advantage lock-in mechanism: all sampled trajectories receive the minimum format penalty (-2.0), group-relative advantages vanish, and the policy-gradient loss becomes zero--an optimization deadlock. We propose a variance-injection strategy: by assigning per-turn rewards to intermediate evidence-reading turns, we prevent the group reward distribution from collapsing to a single value--preserving the variation that GRPO's group-relative advantage requires. Contextual Information-Gain Policy Optimization (CIGPO) implements this strategy using the marginal increase in the frozen reference model's log-likelihood of the ground-truth answer as the per-turn signal. With separate normalization of IG and F1 rewards and an IG-weight curriculum, CIGPO reaches a standard F1 of 0.518 on HotpotQA at the 3B scale (from 0.252 base; +105%), compared with 0.430 for the best GRPO checkpoint and 0.000 for the final GRPO checkpoint. CIGPO maintains meaningful reward variance and avoids zero-advantage lock-in throughout training. These results identify reward-variance collapse as a concrete failure mode of outcome-only GRPO and show that turn-level IG rewards can prevent it in this HotpotQA setting.

</details>


### [220] [Optimizing the Preconditioner: A Black-box Online-to-Nonconvex Conversion with Static Regret Minimization Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17607)
*Haichen Hu,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将随机非凸优化黑盒归约为在线凸优化静态遗憾最小化的理论框架，通过梯度预测器+在线预条件器实现收敛率保证，并解决了Chen和Hazan(2024)提出的开放问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文是纯优化理论工作，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署中的任何一个核心要素。虽然优化理论对深度学习训练有潜在影响，但本文讨论的是随机非凸优化的收敛率理论，而非模型推理阶段的轻量化或部署问题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。不涉及具体神经网络模型结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何实验数据集或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。纯理论推导，无实验对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 纯理论工作，缺乏实验验证；2. 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化无任何直接关联；3. 讨论的是训练阶段的优化收敛性，而非推理阶段的效率问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果非要挖掘：文中将优化器设计分解为梯度预测(gradient prediction)和在线预条件器选择(online preconditioner selection)的思路，在概念上类似于将轻量化检测模型分解为特征提取和检测头两个模块分别优化，但这种类比非常间接。不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: We study whether stochastic nonconvex optimization can be reduced to ordinary static regret minimization in online convex optimization in a black-box manner. For smooth nonconvex objectives, our reduction maintains a predictable gradient tracker, while a black-box online learner selects a preconditioner that determines how this tracker is transformed into the update direction. The learner receives linear convex losses and is evaluated against a single fixed comparator over one undiscounted online game. For a $β$-smooth objective with range bounded by $M$ and an unbiased stochastic-gradient oracle with variance bounded by \(σ^2\), we establish $$\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^T
  \mathbb E\!\left[\|\nabla f(x_t)\|_2^2\right]
  \lesssim
  \frac{σ\sqrt{Mβ}}{\sqrt T}
  +
  \frac{\sqrt{Mβ}\,
  \mathscr R_T(\mathcal A,I_d)}{T}
  +
  \frac{Mβ}{T}.$$ Consequently, any black-box OCO algorithm with $\mathscr R_T(\mathcal A,I_d)=O(\sqrt T)$ recovers the classical $O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}})$ convergence rate.
  We further show that the same black-box framework extends beyond the smooth setting to Lipschitz nonconvex objectives without Lipschitz continuous gradients. Importantly, this extension continues to rely only on an ordinary static-regret guarantee and requires no stronger notion of online regret. When the OCO oracle admits square-root static regret, the resulting conversion achieves the optimal $O(T^{-2/7})$ convergence rate for the corresponding Goldstein stationary point. These results resolve the open problem posed by Chen and Hazan (2024). More broadly, our framework separates optimizer design into gradient prediction and online preconditioner selection, providing a principled perspective on how adaptive optimization methods such as AdaGrad and Shampoo may be understood through static regret and applied in nonconvex optimization.

</details>


### [221] [Learning Structural Manipulability in Gate-Level Netlists Using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16245)
*Rupesh Raj Karn,Ozgur Sinanoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于拓扑驱动的结构可操控性评分，用GNN对门级网表进行节点级回归分析，并在Trojan检测中作为案例验证。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于硬件安全/电路网表分析方向，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：节点级回归（node-level regression）；应用场景：门级网表结构分析、硬件木马检测；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 图神经网络（GNN），具体包括多种GNN架构，其中层级模型（hierarchical models）效果最佳。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ISCAS85、EPFL benchmarks（电路网表标准数据集）；TrustHub模板（硬件木马注入电路）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，提及"ranking consistency"（排名一致性）。

Experiments: 在ISCAS85和EPFL基准上评估不同GNN架构对拓扑得分的近似效果；进行了组件级和消融分析；在TrustHub木马注入电路上进行了案例研究，发现可区分的结构模式。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术或思路。该论文面向的是电路网表的结构分析，领域差异极大。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。虽然GNN在遥感场景中也有应用（如点云、场景图），但本文的图建模对象（门级网表）和任务（结构可操控性评分）与遥感目标检测差距过大，不建议参考。

Abstract: Gate-level netlists exhibit intrinsic structural properties that influence signal propagation independently of functional simulation. We define a topology-driven structural manipulability score that characterizes node-level structural flexibility using path participation, k-core embedding, symmetry, and centrality. Modeling netlists as directed graphs, we formulate node-level regression to learn this topology-derived score using graph neural networks (GNNs). Experiments on ISCAS85 and EPFL benchmarks evaluate how effectively different GNN architectures approximate this metric across held-out circuits, with hierarchical models yielding the most consistent rankings. Component-level and ablation analyses examine the contribution of individual factors. As an illustrative case study, analysis of Trojan-injected circuits using TrustHub templates reveals statistically distinguishable structural patterns, indicating that topology-based scoring provides complementary structural insight.

</details>


### [222] [Topological Signatures of Context-Level Reliability in TabPFN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17962)
*James Hu,Mahdi Ghelichi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用zigzag持续同调分析TabPFN在表格数据上的内部表示拓扑结构，发现拓扑特征与预测可靠性相关。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究对象是TabPFN（表格数据预测的Transformer基础模型），完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化。其核心是拓扑数据分析（zigzag persistent homology）在表格数据上的可解释性研究。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：表格数据预测（tabular prediction）的可解释性分析。应用场景：合成表格数据（warped circles, tori, spheres, Hopf links, trefoil knots, Swiss rolls）。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: TabPFN：基于Transformer的表格预测基础模型，通过支撑集和查询输入进行上下文推理（in-context learning），无需任务特定训练。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：合成表格数据集（warped circles, tori, spheres, Hopf links, trefoil knots, Swiss rolls）。评价指标：mean absolute residual（平均绝对残差）、Bayes error（贝叶斯误差）、overconfidence（过度自信）、H₀ fragmentation count、H₁ loop activity、H₁ persistence。

Experiments: 在合成表格任务上，发现TabPFN内部表示拓扑与数据集级可靠性强相关：H₀ fragmentation与平均绝对残差正相关；困难几何拓扑导致H₁ loop activity增加和H₀ fragmentation增加，同时H₁ persistence变短；这些拓扑描述符与Bayes误差、平均绝对残差和过度自信相关。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感或卫星领域；2. 不涉及目标检测任务；3. 不涉及任何模型轻量化或部署优化；4. 仅在合成表格数据上验证，未在真实遥感数据上测试；5. 未讨论计算开销或实时推理可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发：拓扑数据分析（persistent homology）作为一种模型可解释性工具，理论上可用于分析轻量化后遥感检测模型内部表示的变化，但论文本身未涉及该方向。

Abstract: TabPFN is a transformer-based foundation model for tabular prediction that performs inference without task-specific training by conditioning on a support set and query inputs. Despite its strong empirical performance, its internal behavior on structurally difficult tabular geometries remains poorly understood. We study this behavior using zigzag persistent homology, treating TabPFN layer representations as evolving point clouds. We construct a controlled benchmark of synthetic tabular tasks with known true probabilities and varied intrinsic topology, including warped circles, tori, spheres, Hopf links, trefoil knots, and Swiss rolls. Across these tasks, we find that the topology of TabPFN's internal representation geometry is strongly associated with dataset-level reliability; for example, the zeroth homology group $H_0$ fragmentation count correlates positively with mean absolute residual across controlled tasks, and this association strengthens in a high-resolution warped circle case study at large sample size. Harder geometries induce a dual topological signature: increased $H_1$ loop activity and increased $H_0$ fragmentation, while the $H_1$ persistence becomes shorter-lived. These descriptors correlate with Bayes error, mean absolute residuals, and overconfidence. Our results suggest that zigzag persistence diagnoses the reliability of the inferred in-context task geometry and provides a context-level view of when TabPFN operates in topologically stressed regimes.

</details>


### [223] [Let the Data Decide: Supervision Analysis, Capability Trade-offs, and Adaptive Objective Routing in Continued Pre-Training via Off-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16246)
*Jiangan Yuan,Zhixuan Li,Han Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型预训练中离策略蒸馏的目标函数参数化与数据路由策略，提出支持覆盖度、观测token概率质量等诊断指标，发现领域级路由优于token级路由。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型(LLM)的离策略蒸馏预训练，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署。虽然涉及知识蒸馏(KD)技术，但讨论的是LLM场景下的目标函数设计与数据路由，而非遥感目标检测模型的压缩或加速。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型预训练中的离策略蒸馏；应用场景：NLP/LLM；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，讨论的是LLM预训练中的蒸馏目标函数设计（L_LM vs L_KD）和数据路由策略。

Lightweight method: 涉及知识蒸馏(Knowledge Distillation, KD)方法，但讨论的是LLM预训练阶段的离策略蒸馏，而非模型压缩或轻量化。不涉及剪枝、量化、NAS等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。提出诊断指标：support coverage（支持覆盖度）、observed-token probability mass（观测token概率质量）、teacher-distribution concentration（教师分布集中度）。

Experiments: 通过控制实验发现：支持大小k控制覆盖-锐度权衡(coverage-sharpness trade-off)，蒸馏温度控制支持内概率分配；领域级路由（数学/代码用L_LM，通用域用L_KD）优于单目标基线，而token级路由无法一致优于单目标基线。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 实验仅在LLM场景验证，结论对视觉模型的可迁移性未知；3. 未讨论蒸馏后模型的推理效率、参数量等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 尽管论文与遥感在轨检测无关，但以下思路可间接借鉴：1. 知识蒸馏中"目标函数设计"的思路——在遥感检测模型蒸馏时，可分析检测损失(L_det)与蒸馏损失(L_KD)之间的梯度冲突，类似本文的"direct observed-token reinforcement vs teacher-supported alternative supervision"分析；2. "数据条件式监督设计"思想——在遥感场景中，可根据不同地物类别（如舰船、飞机、车辆）或不同复杂度场景自适应路由蒸馏策略；3. 诊断指标的设计方法论——可针对遥感小目标检测蒸馏设计类似的支持覆盖度等诊断指标。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Off-policy distillation is now central to large language model pre-training, yet how training data, objective parameterization, and model capabilities interact remains poorly characterized. We studies top-$k$-truncated, temperature-scaled off-policy distillation by decomposing this problem into two questions: an \emph{objective-to-capability} analysis of how the training objective shapes token-level supervision and downstream performance, and a \emph{data-to-objective} analysis of how data heterogeneity should inform objective routing. We first show that the language-modeling objective ($L_{\mathrm{LM}}$) and the knowledge-distillation objective ($L_{\mathrm{KD}}$) induce systematically different capability profiles, and trace this divergence to a gradient-level tension between \emph{direct observed-token reinforcement} and \emph{teacher-supported alternative supervision}. To quantify this tension, we introduce diagnostic metrics -- support coverage, observed-token probability mass, and teacher-distribution concentration -- and show via controlled sweeps that the support size $k$ governs a coverage-sharpness trade-off, while distillation temperature controls within-support probability allocation. We then examine adaptive objective routing: a domain-level policy that applies $L_{\mathrm{LM}}$ to math and code and $L_{\mathrm{KD}}$ to general-domain data yields consistent gains over both single-objective baselines, whereas token-level routing based on observed-token probability mass or teacher entropy fails to consistently match the single-objective baseline. These results suggest that effective objective routing depends less on routing granularity than on the quality of the routing signal, reframing continued pre-training via off-policy distillation as a structured, data-conditional supervision-design problem rather than a global hyperparameter choice.

</details>


### [224] [Self-Evolving Just-In-Time Memory for Proactive Embodied Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16247)
*Bingrui Sima,Lizhong Wang,Xiaoya Lu,Kun He,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向具身智能体的自演进即时记忆框架（JIT-Memory），用于在闭环交互中主动缓解动态危险，而非被动阻止动作，以打破安全与任务进展之间的权衡。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于具身智能体（embodied agents）在家庭环境中的安全交互问题，属于机器人/具身AI领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型及其轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：具身智能体安全交互（hazard mitigation）；应用场景：家庭环境（household tasks）；传感器类型：摘要未说明（可能涉及视觉传感器，但非遥感传感器）；目标类别：动态危险（dynamically emerging hazards），非遥感目标。

Model architecture: 提出Self-Evolving Just-In-Time Memory框架，包含三个核心组件：Risk-Sufficient Topological Belief Graph (RSG)用于部分可观测下的安全状态跟踪、Agency-Grounded Factual Memory用于危险预测、Experience Memory用于注入程序性元技能（Meta-Skills）。此外还有Test-Verify-Write循环用于在线自演进。不涉及遥感目标检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。实验中使用Qwen3-VL-8B等VLM骨干，但未讨论模型压缩或加速。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：IS-Bench（具身智能体安全基准）；评价指标：Safe-Success rate（安全成功率）。不涉及任何遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10、SSDD、SARDet等）。

Experiments: 在IS-Bench上使用多个VLM骨干（如Qwen3-VL-8B）进行实验，Safe-Success rate提升+30.3%。不涉及遥感目标检测对比实验。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关。局限性包括：仅针对家庭场景的具身安全交互，未涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测、模型压缩或星上部署等核心问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，可借鉴性极低。唯一可能的跨领域启发是：其"自演进记忆"思想（Test-Verify-Write loop）或许可类比于在轨模型的自适应更新策略，但与本课题核心（遥感目标检测模型轻量化）关联非常间接。

Abstract: While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have empowered embodied agents to execute complex household tasks, they struggle to proactively handle dynamically emerging hazards during closed-loop interactions. Existing safety approaches often rely on runtime guardrails to block unsafe actions or induce excessive caution, which severely stalls task progress instead of actively resolving the underlying risks. To break this safety-progress trade-off, we introduce the Self-Evolving Just-In-Time Memory framework, which reframes embodied safety from progress-stalling guardrails to proactive hazard mitigation. The framework consists of a Risk-Sufficient Topological Belief Graph (RSG) for persistent safety-relevant state tracking under partial observability, an Agency-Grounded Factual Memory for precise hazard anticipation, and an Experience Memory that injects procedural Meta-Skills to guide executable, progress-preserving mitigation. Furthermore, we propose an automated Test-Verify-Write loop, allowing agents to continually refine their mitigation Meta-Skills from execution traces at test time. Experiments on IS-Bench demonstrate that our framework substantially boosts the Safe-Success rate across multiple VLM backbones (e.g., +30.3% on Qwen3-VL-8B), enabling agents to proactively mitigate hazards without stalling task progress. Code is available at https://github.com/DyMessi/JIT-Memory.

</details>


### [225] [High-accuracy Low-Bit KV-Cache Quantization via Local Distribution Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16248)
*Gradwell Dzikanyanga,Yanqi Pan,Weihao Yang,Donglei Wu,Wen Xia,Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种针对大语言模型KV缓存低比特量化后精度严重下降的修复方法DGAP，通过局部分布恢复技术恢复Top-K候选分布，在保持低比特量化内存优势的同时大幅恢复模型精度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）推理中的KV缓存量化问题，属于NLP/大模型推理优化领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或遥感图像理解。虽然涉及量化（quantization）技术，但应用场景和目标完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"课题方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对量化后精度损失的诊断方法有特殊兴趣，否则可跳过。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型长上下文推理中的KV缓存量化压缩；应用场景：LLM推理加速与内存优化；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于Llama-3.1-8B、Llama、Mistral、Qwen等大语言模型架构，核心组件为KV缓存（Key-Value cache）及注意力机制。

Lightweight method: 涉及低比特KV缓存量化（low-bit KV-cache quantization），特别是1-bit量化。提出DGAP方法，包含局部分布风险检测器（risk detector）和校正器（corrector），用于恢复量化后logits的局部分布。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。仅提到在LLM推理中降低KV缓存内存占用和带宽开销，以及"modest decode overhead"（适度的解码开销），但未给出具体参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等数据。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：RULER（长上下文LLM评估基准）；评价指标：准确率（accuracy，从84.2%降至47.8%再恢复至83.2%）、分布漂移（distribution drift，从0.38降至0.14）。

Experiments: 在Llama-3.1-8B上，DGAP将1-bit KV缓存量化后的RULER准确率从47.8%恢复至83.2%（接近全精度84.2%），分布漂移从0.38降至0.14。在Llama、Mistral、Qwen系列模型上均验证了有效性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 仅针对LLM的KV缓存量化场景，不涉及视觉模型或目标检测；3. 未讨论在边缘/星上部署的硬件适配性；4. 摘要未说明方法的计算开销是否适合资源受限平台。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下思路可能有间接启发：1. 量化后精度损失的根因分析思路——该论文发现"结构化局部排序错误"而非绝对误差是主因，这种诊断思路可迁移到遥感检测模型量化后的精度分析；2. "局部分布恢复"的思想，即在关键区域（如检测头输出）做针对性修复而非全局恢复，可能适用于遥感小目标检测模型量化后的精度补偿；3. 风险检测器+校正器的两阶段设计模式，可借鉴到遥感模型轻量化中的自适应量化策略设计。

Abstract: Long-context large language model inference relies on the KV cache to avoid redundant attention computation, but incurs high memory and bandwidth overheads. Low-bit KV-cache quantization reduces this cost, yet it severely degrade quality; particularly, one-bit quantization reduces accuracy from 84.2% to 47.8% on Llama-3.1-8B under RULER.
  Rather than common beliefs that absolute error of logits, we find that the root cause is structured local misranking, where the distribution of logits in top-K region is drifted. We thereby propose local distribution restoration, a new technique that detects steps with high local distribution risk from quantized-logit features and restores only the selected top-K candidate distribution before token selection. We implement DGAP to achieve local distribution restoration, with efficient risk detcetors and correctors. Expeirments show that on Llama-3.1-8B, DGAP recovers K1V1 RULER accuracy from 47.8% to 83.2% and reduces distribution drift from 0.38 to 0.14; across Llama, Mistral, and Qwen models, it preserves the persistent low-bit KV-cache footprint with modest decode overhead.

</details>


### [226] [Benchmarking Machine Learning Models for Multi-Omics-Based Breast Cancer Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16250)
*Priyanka Paudel,Madan Baduwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了经典机器学习模型（Random Forest, XGBoost等）在乳腺癌ER状态预测任务上的表现，利用TCGA-BRCA队列的多组学数据（转录组、基因组、蛋白质组）进行单组学和多组学集成分析。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于**乳腺癌ER状态预测**这一生物医学/计算生物学任务，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、遥感场景理解等视觉任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等模型压缩或部署技术。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：生物信息学中的ER状态二分类预测；应用场景：乳腺癌诊断与预后；数据类型：转录组（RNA表达）、基因组（CNV拷贝数变异）、蛋白质组（RPPA）；目标类别：ER阳性/阴性。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: Random Forest, XGBoost, LightGBM, CatBoost, SVM, Logistic Regression等经典机器学习模型，不涉及深度学习视觉模型（如CNN、Transformer、YOLO等）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：TCGA-BRCA队列（多组学数据）；评价指标：balanced accuracy（平衡准确率90.3%）、ROC-AUC（97.1%）；实验设置：分层训练-测试划分、分层五折交叉验证、类别不平衡处理、折内特征选择。

Experiments: RNA表达提供最强预测信号；多组学集成相比单组学有适度但一致的提升；Random Forest在多组学集成设置下表现最佳（balanced accuracy 90.3%, ROC-AUC 97.1%）；ESR1、PGR、FOXA1、GATA3等基因被反复选择，验证了模型的生物学有效性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的视觉检测或模型压缩内容。该论文属于生物信息学/计算生物学领域，不涉及计算机视觉、遥感图像处理或嵌入式部署。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。唯一的间接启发可能是：多模态/多组学集成策略在生物信息学中有效，但遥感领域多模态融合（如光学+SAR）已有大量研究，且该论文使用的经典ML方法不适用于遥感目标检测这类视觉任务。

Abstract: Estrogen Receptor (ER) status is a critical biomarker in breast cancer diagnosis, prognosis, and treatment selection. Recent advances in high-throughput sequencing technologies have enabled the generation of multi-omics datasets that provide complementary molecular information for computational prediction tasks. This study presents a systematic benchmarking analysis of classical machine learning models for ER status prediction using transcriptomic (RNA expression), genomic (copy number variation; CNV), and proteomic (RPPA) data from the TCGA-BRCA cohort. A rigorous experimental framework incorporating stratified train-test splitting, stratified five-fold cross-validation, class imbalance handling, and fold-specific feature selection was employed to ensure reliable evaluation and prevent data leakage. Random Forest, XGBoost, LightGBM, CatBoost, Support Vector Machines (SVM), and Logistic Regression were evaluated across single-omic and multi-omic settings. Results demonstrated that RNA expression provided the strongest predictive signal, while multi-omic integration yielded modest but consistent improvements over individual modalities. Among all evaluated approaches, Random Forest achieved the best overall performance in the integrated multi-omic setting, obtaining a balanced accuracy of 90.3\% and an ROC-AUC of 97.1\%. Furthermore, recurrent selection of biologically relevant genes, including \textit{ESR1}, \textit{PGR}, \textit{FOXA1}, and \textit{GATA3}, supported the biological validity of the learned models. These findings indicate that carefully regularized classical machine learning methods remain highly effective for small, high-dimensional genomic datasets and that multi-omic integration provides complementary information for breast cancer ER status prediction.

</details>


### [227] [Learning Spatio-Temporal Foundation Models from Pure Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16251)
*Yutong Feng,Shiyuan Piao,Yutong Xia,Xu Liu,Wenqi Fan,Fugee Tsung,See-Kiong Ng,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeoST 是一个完全基于程序化合成数据预训练的时空基础模型，通过潜在空间推理架构和合成预训练语料库，在多种真实世界时空系统基准上超越现有方法，并实现更优的长时程稳定性和推理效率。

Research relevance: 1 分（基本无关）。该论文聚焦于通用时空基础模型（STFM），面向的是复杂动力系统（如气象、交通等）的时空建模，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文未涉及遥感图像、目标检测、轻量化部署等核心要素。

Reading priority: 1 分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：时空预测/建模（spatio-temporal forecasting）；应用场景：复杂动力系统（如气象、气候、交通流等）；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：摘要未说明。不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测。

Model architecture: NeoST 提出潜在空间推理架构（latent-space reasoning architecture），能够生成并迭代优化多条未来轨迹，避免自回归或扩散范式中的序列误差累积。具体模块细节摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。论文提到"inference efficiency"更优，但这是架构设计带来的自然优势，并非专门的模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"superior long-horizon stability and inference efficiency"，但未给出具体数值或与轻量化部署相关的指标。

Datasets and metrics: 预训练数据：程序化生成的合成系统（procedurally generated synthetic systems）；下游评估：多种真实世界时空系统基准（diverse real-world benchmarks）；具体数据集名称和评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在多种真实世界时空系统基准上，NeoST 一致优于现有 STFMs，在长时程稳定性和推理效率方面表现更优。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 未涉及目标检测任务；3. 未涉及任何轻量化或边缘部署技术；4. 合成数据到真实遥感场景的迁移性未知；5. 参数量、计算量等部署关键指标缺失。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能的启发点：1）潜在空间推理架构（latent-space reasoning）的思路或许可用于遥感视频流中的时序目标检测，减少帧间误差累积；2）合成数据预训练策略可考虑用于遥感目标检测中的数据增强或域适应。但整体而言，该论文与课题方向关联度极低。

Abstract: Spatio-Temporal Foundation Models (STFMs) aim to learn generalizable representations of complex dynamical systems across space and time. However, existing approaches suffer from distributional bias in real-world pre-training data, structural bottlenecks of autoregressive or diffusion-based paradigms, and objectives that overemphasize point-wise reconstruction in noisy observation space.We propose \textbf{NeoST}, the first spatio-temporal foundation model pre-trained solely on procedurally generated synthetic systems. NeoST introduces a scalable synthetic pre-training corpus to mitigate real-world bias, a latent-space reasoning architecture that generates and iteratively refines multiple future trajectories without sequential error accumulation, and latent-space objectives that emphasize structural dynamics and enable inference-time correction under distribution shifts.Extensive experiments across diverse real-world benchmarks show that NeoST consistently outperforms existing STFMs in diverse real-world spatio-temporal systems, achieves superior long-horizon stability and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [228] [SOS-LoRA: Static Orthogonal-Subspace Low-Rank Adaptation with Fixed Multi-Scale Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16252)
*Yupeng Chang,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SOS-LoRA，一种将LoRA分解为多个静态正交子空间专家的参数高效微调方法，用于大语言模型微调。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型(LLM)的参数高效微调(PEFT)，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、卫星在轨部署或模型轻量化压缩。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。除非对PEFT方法在遥感视觉模型上的迁移有特殊兴趣，否则优先级很低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型参数高效微调（PEFT）；应用场景：自然语言理解(NLU)、数学推理(GSM8K/MATH)；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于LoRA的改进，将rank-rtot更新分解为K个静态低秩专家（static low-rank experts），每个专家使用正交初始化，并采用多尺度缩放策略。

Lightweight method: 论文本身是PEFT方法，属于模型微调阶段的参数效率优化，而非推理阶段的模型轻量化（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏）。SOS-LoRA在合并后不增加推理参数和延迟，但这是PEFT的通用特性，并非针对边缘/星上部署的轻量化设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"adds no inference-time parameters or latency after merging"，但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Llama 2/3（推理和知识密集型基准）、GLUE（编码器NLU）、GSM8K/MATH（数学推理）；评价指标：摘要未具体说明指标名称。

Experiments: 在多个LLM基准上，SOS-LoRA相比同等预算的LoRA基线及近期变体取得一致提升。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 仅在NLP任务上验证，未涉及视觉或遥感任务；3. 未提供计算效率（FLOPs、延迟）的量化对比。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。虽然论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化无直接关系，但其"将大矩阵分解为多个正交子空间"的思想，如果迁移到遥感目标检测模型的轻量化中，可能对以下方向有启发：(1) 将检测头或backbone的权重矩阵分解为多个低秩分支，减少参数量；(2) 正交初始化策略可能有助于缓解轻量化模型训练中的优化困难。但需注意，该方法是微调阶段的PEFT，而非推理阶段的模型压缩，直接借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) method for large language models. Under a fixed rank budget, LoRA parameterizes each adapted weight through a single low-dimensional input-side pathway, which may couple heterogeneous behaviors through shared input directions and induce interference during optimization. We propose Static Orthogonal Subspace LoRA (SOS-LoRA), a drop-in extension that reparameterizes a rank-rtot update as a sum of K static (always-on, non-routed) low-rank experts. SOS-LoRA (i) decomposes the total rank across experts, (ii) applies a fixed multi-scale scaling scheme to encourage scale-separated optimization dynamics, and (iii) promotes diverse input-side directions via cross-expert orthogonal initialization and a lightweight regularizer. SOS-LoRA remains fully mergeable, adding no inference-time parameters or latency after merging. Experiments on reasoning and knowledge-intensive benchmarks (Llama 2/3), encoder-based NLU (GLUE), and math reasoning (GSM8K/MATH) show consistent gains over matched-budget LoRA baselines and recent variants. Code is available at https://github.com/llm172/sos-lora.

</details>


### [229] [Comprehensive Evaluation of Machine Learning for Type 2 Diabetes Risk Prediction: Large-Scale External Validation and Fairness Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16253)
*Rajveer Singh Pall,Sameer Yadav,Siddharth Bhalerao,Sourabh Sahu,Ritu Ahluwalia,Bhaskar Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多维框架评估基于机器学习的2型糖尿病风险预测模型，在NHANES和BRFSS数据集上验证了外部泛化损失和严重的年龄偏差问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是医学健康领域的糖尿病风险预测，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：2型糖尿病风险预测；应用场景：临床医学/公共卫生；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: XGBoost（梯度提升树模型），非深度学习视觉模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：NHANES 2015-2020（n=15,685）用于训练，BRFSS 2020-2022（n=1,285,783）用于外部验证；评价指标：AUC、Brier score、校准曲线、SHAP可解释性分析。

Experiments: 内部验证AUC=0.794，外部验证AUC=0.717（相对下降-9.7%）；年龄偏差显著（老年组AUC=0.607 vs 青年组0.742）；肥胖偏差（AUC=0.698 vs 0.735）；性别差异不显著（0.723 vs 0.712）。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验范式。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于医学AI公平性研究，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在任务、数据、方法、部署场景上均无交集。

Abstract: Machine learning-based Type 2 diabetes risk prediction models obtain good internal validation results but lose effectiveness in real-world applications due to deficient external testing and fairness assessment. We developed a multi-dimensional framework evaluating discrimination, calibration, interpretability, and algorithmic fairness on nationally representative populations. An XGBoost model was trained on NHANES 2015-2020 (n=15,685) using eight non-laboratory predictors: age, sex, race/ethnicity, BMI, smoking status, physical activity, history of heart attack, and history of stroke. External validation was performed on BRFSS 2020-2022 (n=1,285,783) under realistic distribution shift. Internal validation showed good discrimination (AUC=0.794, 95% CI 0.788-0.800), with performance loss on external validation (AUC=0.717, relative decrease: -9.7%, p<0.001). Fairness analysis revealed severe bias: elderly adults (>=60) showed AUC=0.607 vs 0.742 for young adults (difference=0.135, p<0.001); obese individuals showed AUC=0.698 vs 0.735 for normal weight (difference=0.037, p<0.001). Gender showed comparable performance (male=0.723 vs female=0.712, p=0.142). Calibration revealed risk overestimation (Brier score=0.123). SHAP analysis identified age, BMI, and physical activity as primary risk drivers. Populations with highest diabetes risk receive the worst algorithmic performance, underscoring the need for fairness-aware, age-stratified deployment strategies before clinical use.

</details>


### [230] [More Than Memory: Task-Conditioned Signed FFN Writes in Long-Context Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16254)
*Zhibo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型中FFN层在长文本检索中的残差写入方向（放大/抑制）对检索状态的影响，提出基于梯度对齐的诊断方法，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦LLM内部机制（FFN残差写入方向对检索的影响），不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、卫星在轨部署或任何轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM长文本检索机制分析；应用场景：自然语言检索；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 研究对象为Transformer中的FFN层，分析其残差写入方向（amplifier/suppressor）对检索状态的影响。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：controlled literal/semantic retrieval suites, safety-filtered LongBench retrieval-QA probe；指标：R²（0.796 on Qwen2.5-7B, 0.791 on Qwen3.5-9B），95%置信区间。

Experiments: 在多个LLM（Qwen2.5-7B, Qwen3.5-9B）上验证FFN层角色（suppressor/amplifier）与检索性能的关系，提出write-gradient alignment诊断方法。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感、检测或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文研究的是NLP领域的LLM内部机制，与遥感图像目标检测和模型轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: FFNs are often treated as parametric memories. In long-context retrieval, however, the sharper question is not only what they store, but whether their native residual writes push the current retrieval state toward or away from the correct answer. We test this by scaling the model's own FFN write one layer at a time, without editing weights or injecting external steering vectors.
  Across controlled literal and semantic retrieval suites, native FFN response surfaces are signed, layer-specific, and task-conditioned: the final FFN is a suppressor in 7 of 8 model-suite cases, and 60% of layers switch role between retrieval modes (95% CI [50%, 69%]). A local directional derivative along the native write separates the two monotone roles: suppressors have negative derivative in 34/35 cases, and amplifiers have positive derivative in 18/18 cases, so the roles are not reducible to write size. On a safety-filtered LongBench retrieval-QA probe, the same diagnostic predicts attenuation damage with raw R^2=0.796 on Qwen2.5-7B and 0.791 on Qwen3.5-9B; a held-out suppressor-attenuation policy improves retrieval margins over random and norm-matched controls. These results show that native FFN scaling exposes a signed, task-conditioned residual-write structure in retrieval, and that write-gradient alignment is a compact diagnostic for the two monotone roles.

</details>


### [231] [Feature Generation Using LLMs: An Evolutionary Algorithm Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16255)
*Aria Nourbakhsh,Benoît Alcaraz,Christoph Schommer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用大语言模型(LLM)从表格数据的已有特征中自动生成新特征，并通过选择算法筛选最佳特征集以提升分类性能。

Experiments: 在8个数据集上验证，结果表明LLM能基于数学和逻辑算子生成对分类任务有用的新特征，提升分类效果。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；未涉及图像/遥感数据；未涉及目标检测任务；未涉及任何模型压缩或部署优化；LLM推理开销大，与轻量化/在轨部署需求背道而驰。

Abstract: A crucial step in machine learning pipelines is to present each entity with features or attributes that are representative of the characteristics of the processed entities. Feature engineering is an important step in finding a relation among attributes that otherwise may not be processed by the ML algorithms. Meanwhile, Large Language Models have shown promising abilities in coding, mathematical reasoning, and processing world knowledge. In this work, we utilize an LLM for the problem of feature generation from tabular data based on the previously given features. We have created a pipeline that takes a set of attributes and a prompt to generate new features. Then, our selection algorithm selects the best-performing sets of attributes. We apply our method to eight datasets from different domains and data types. Our results show that, in most cases, the language model can produce new features based on mathematical and logical operators that are useful for the given tasks and can improve classification results.

</details>


### [232] [Discovery by Dreaming: Cross-Domain Recombination in Artificial Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16256)
*Oliver Zahn,James Evans,David Eagleman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"跨域重组式回放"机制（类比人类梦境），通过LoRA微调（DREAMS）和符号引擎（SAPIENCE）两种架构验证：跨领域知识重组能产生新发现，而领域内重复无价值，核心结论是记忆巩固不是为了记住，而是为了发现。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何轻量化或部署技术。论文本质是认知科学与机器学习交叉研究，探讨记忆巩固中的跨域知识重组机制。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题完全无关，不推荐阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感或目标检测相关任务。论文涉及的任务是跨域知识重组与发现（cross-domain consolidation），应用场景为数学推理（GSM8K）和科学文献发现验证，传感器类型不适用，目标类别不适用。

Model architecture: 两个架构：(1) DREAMS — 基于LoRA的微调流水线；(2) SAPIENCE — 回放结构化知识对象的符号引擎。另提及671B参数的大模型作为对比基线。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法。虽然使用了LoRA（低秩适配），但LoRA在此处是作为微调方法而非模型压缩手段使用，目的不是轻量化或加速推理。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。论文完全不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：GSM8K（数学推理）、50,000篇真实论文（发现验证）。评价指标：跨域连接发现率（85.7%）、总体提升百分点（+5.64 pp）、跨域子任务提升（+14.5 pp）。

Experiments: 符号引擎SAPIENCE跨域连接发现率85.7%（+21 pp）；神经架构DREAMS总体提升+5.64 pp，跨域子任务（GSM8K未见数学推理）提升+14.5 pp。对照实验表明该效果是权重的真实属性而非提示工程产物（671B模型上下文注入反而逆转增益）。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或检测技术。论文聚焦认知科学层面的跨域重组机制，不涉及视觉感知、实时推理或资源受限部署等工程问题。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。唯一可能的间接启发：论文提出的"跨域重组"思想或许可以类比到遥感目标检测中的多模态/多源数据融合（如光学+SAR跨域特征重组），但论文本身未提供任何具体技术方案可用于轻量化或在轨部署。不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Dreams splice together people, places, and times that never met. Neuroscience suggests this recombination is not noise, but a function driving insight and creative discovery. This reframes memory consolidation: rather than merely defending against forgetting, its measurable value lies in recombining knowledge across experiences that have not yet co-occurred. We test this directly by isolating the recombinatory-replay mechanism and implementing it in two architecturally unrelated systems: a LoRA fine-tuning pipeline (DREAMS) and a symbolic engine replaying structured knowledge objects (SAPIENCE). Both systems converge on the same finding: cross-domain consolidation creates value, while within-domain rehearsal does not. The symbolic arm surfaces novel cross-domain connections at 85.7%, a +21 percentage point (pp) gain over baseline. The neural arm improves overall by +5.64 pp, but on subtasks explicitly requiring cross-domain transfer (like unseen math reasoning on GSM8K), gains reach +14.5 pp. This effect is a genuine property of the weights--not a prompt artifact--as prepending the same material in-context to a 671B-parameter model actually reverses the gain. We validate this prediction against documented discoveries across 50,000 real papers and state a falsifiable hippocampal-recording prediction to distinguish recombination from rehearsal. Ultimately, this principle is substrate-general, tracking real discovery at scale. Reading the literature teaches a model to recall what it has seen, but producing discovery requires a separate offline phase that recombines knowledge across domains--the computational analog of dreaming. Consolidation is not for remembering, but for discovering.

</details>


### [233] [From Outcomes to Actions: Leveraging Hindsight for Long-Horizon Language Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16257)
*Zishang Jiang,Tingyun Li,Jinyi Han,Xinyi Wang,Sihang Jiang,Yizhou Ying,Xiaojun Meng,Jiansheng Wei,Jiaqing Liang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为Hindsight Policy Optimization (HPO)的新型策略梯度方法，通过意图空间中的Wasserstein距离提取低方差学习信号，用于改进长时序交互下的强化学习训练。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于强化学习算法（策略梯度方法）的改进，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：强化学习策略优化；应用场景：大语言模型（LLMs）及长时序交互任务；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 提出Hindsight Policy Optimization (HPO)方法，核心是将当前策略分布和后见分布投影到意图空间（intent space），通过Wasserstein距离提取低方差学习信号。摘要未说明具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署相关内容。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要提到进行了理论和实验验证，表明所提方法能产生有界方差估计器并稳定提升策略性能，但未给出具体实验结果和对比方法。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。摘要也未说明该方法在视觉任务上的适用性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。如果强行关联，强化学习中的低方差优化思想理论上可用于在轨模型的自适应更新或在线学习场景，但论文本身未涉及任何视觉或遥感内容，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a widely adopted technique for improving large language models (LLMs) on complex tasks. Despite this progress, existing RL methods still face challenges in training agents with longer-horizon interactions. One major bottleneck is distinguishing the contribution of different actions in long-horizon interaction, leading to high optimization variance. To address this, we introduce a novel policy gradient method, Hindsight Policy Optimization (HPO), that projects both the current policy distribution and the hindsight distribution into an intent space and extracts low-variance learning signals from the Wasserstein distance between them. We theoretically and empirically show that aggregating semantically similar states and actions in the intent space yields a bounded-variance estimator and improves policy performance stably. Our code is available online.

</details>


### [234] [Neural Controlled Differential Equations for EMT-Level Surrogate Modeling of Grid-Forming Inverters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16258)
*Jiagang Qu,Yong Tao,Dan Wang,Enyi Li,Jingjing Qi,Ding Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于神经控制微分方程(Neural CDE)的电网换流器连续时间代理模型，用于电磁暂态仿真中的多时间尺度控制分析。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦电力电子变换器建模与电磁暂态仿真，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：电力电子变换器连续时间代理模型构建；场景：电磁暂态(EMT)仿真；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Neural Controlled Differential Equation (Neural CDE) 框架；双慢/快路径的仿射控制结构(dual slow/fast pathways)。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：EMT生成的轨迹数据；评价指标：瞬态响应复现精度、阻尼保持、主导振荡特性、长程 rollout 有界性。

Experiments: 在EMT生成轨迹上验证，模型能准确复现瞬态响应，保持有效阻尼和主导振荡特性，长程预测有界。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术点。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于电力系统仿真领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化无任何交集。

Abstract: The application of artificial intelligence methods in power electronic converter modeling is becoming increasingly widespread, but existing applications still face many challenges, such as difficulties in multi-time-scale hybrid analysis and the lack of physics-aware evaluation criteria and constraints, resulting in poor performance. This paper proposes a Neural Controlled Differential Equation (Neural CDE) framework for learning continuous-time surrogate models of grid-forming inverters for electromagnetic transient (EMT) simulation, which relaxes the constraint of fixed sampling rates and enables multi-time-scale control analysis. Then, an affine-control formulation with dual slow/fast pathways is proposed to capture the hierarchical and multiscale behavior of converter dynamics, and a physics-inspired regularization method is utilized to enhance stability and coherence. Evaluated on EMT-generated trajectories, the model accurately reproduces transient responses, preserves effective damping and the dominant oscillatory characteristics, and maintains bounded long-horizon rollouts. The results show that Neural CDE-based component modeling offers a physically consistent surrogate modeling approach for EMT-level simulation studies.

</details>


### [235] [AdaSurvMamba: Dynamic Fusion and Semantic Scanning for Multimodal Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16260)
*Jialong Zhong,Tingwei Liu,Baokun Yue,Jingjing Li,Yongri Piao,Miao Zhang,Leiye Liu,Jiahong Jiang,Wei Ji,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Mamba的多模态生存分析框架AdaSurvMamba，用于全切片图像与基因组数据的融合分析，不涉及遥感或目标检测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医学病理图像（WSI）与基因组数据的多模态生存分析，属于计算病理学领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。
