Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态生存分析（multimodal survival analysis）；应用场景：癌症预后（cancer prognosis）；传感器类型：全切片图像（WSI）与基因组数据；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 基于Mamba（状态空间模型）的框架，包含Dual-Scale Importance-Aware Reconstruction (DSIR)模块和Semantic Aggregation Scanning (SAS)模块。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。且该论文面向医学分析，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：五个TCGA（The Cancer Genome Atlas）队列；评价指标：摘要未具体说明指标名称。

Experiments: 在五个TCGA队列上取得一致优于现有方法的结果。代码已开源。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术细节。Mamba架构在遥感目标检测中的应用可能有一定参考价值，但本文未涉及遥感场景。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。Mamba架构在遥感目标检测领域近期有探索（如遥感Mamba类方法），但本文聚焦医学多模态生存分析，未涉及目标检测或轻量化，对在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题的直接借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Multimodal survival analysis utilizing whole slide images (WSIs) and genomic profiles is fundamental for cancer prognosis. Recently, state-space models like Mamba have emerged as powerful tools for sequence modeling. However, translating this success to complex multimodal tasks is hindered by two critical limitations. First, conventional fusion strategies assume a static multimodal interaction strength, ignoring the fluctuating diagnostic importance of each modality across different patients and local regions. Second, the standard Mamba architecture processes tokens along predefined physical paths. This rigid scanning disrupts the semantic continuity of spatially scattered medical features and exacerbates long-range decay. To address these challenges, we introduce AdaSurvMamba as a novel adaptive framework for multimodal survival analysis. The framework features a Dual-Scale Importance-Aware Reconstruction (DSIR) module to dynamically modulate cross-modal interaction strength. It evaluates diagnostic importance at both the sequence and token levels to reconstruct the input representations. Furthermore, we propose a Semantic Aggregation Scanning (SAS) module to overcome contextual fragmentation. The SAS module dynamically reorganizes discrete tokens into semantically continuous sequences via a shared prototype pool. It explicitly modulates the state transition step size using global modality context and semantic priors to adaptively control the information absorption rate. Experiments across five TCGA cohorts demonstrate consistent gains over existing methods. Code is available at https://github.com/zjlGO/AdaSurvMamba.

</details>


### [236] [Autonomous mechanistic discovery of colorectal cancer vulnerabilities via multi-scale AI swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16262)
*Christopher Baker,Tianyu Ren,Karen Rafferty,Hui Wang,Simon McDade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Octopus的多尺度自主发现引擎，将LLM与物理引擎结合，用于生物医学领域的自动化假设生成和临床转化预测，与遥感目标检测和轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文聚焦生物医学领域（结直肠癌、CRISPR、药物耐药性、临床生存预测），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。属于生物信息学/计算生物学方向。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：生物医学自主科学发现（假设生成、因果推断、临床生存预测）。应用场景：结直肠癌转录组分析、药物耐药性发现、体内外验证。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: Octopus架构：神经符号架构，包含零泄漏本地LLM群（LLM swarms）与算法物理引擎（algorithmic physics engines）的结合。使用XGBoost SHAP向量进行机械可解释性分析。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CCLE（CRISPR依赖数据）、PDX（小鼠肿瘤模型）、Marisa（人类生存数据）。评价指标：Benjamini-Hochberg FDR校正（q=0.0292）、Log-Rank检验（p=0.0007）、Mann-Whitney检验（p=0.0373）。

Experiments: 在结直肠癌转录组数据上全无监督扫描，自主识别出IGF2作为5-FU耐药性的严格有界脆弱性，并在独立小鼠队列中验证了肿瘤体积显著缩小。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。摘要未说明模型计算开销、推理速度等工程部署信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于生物医学AI方向，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题在任务、数据模态、部署约束上均无交集。

Abstract: The acceleration of automated scientific discovery has been fundamentally bottlenecked by the epistemic gap between the semantic reasoning of large language models (LLMs) and the deterministic physics of mammalian biology. While recent multi-agent frameworks have achieved autonomous hypothesis generation and in vitro experimental analysis, they lack the mathematically grounded, causal constraints required for multi-scale clinical translation. Furthermore, while algorithmic clinical digital twins successfully forecast biological states, they rely on black-box latent spaces, sacrificing mechanistic interpretability for predictive accuracy. Here, we introduce the Multi-Scale Autonomous Discovery Engine (Octopus), a neuro-symbolic architecture that unites zero-leakage, local LLM swarms with strict algorithmic physics engines. Rather than stopping at isolated cellular assays, the system autonomously generated therapeutic hypotheses against in vitro CRISPR dependency data (CCLE), traced dynamic causal cascades using mechanistic interpretability (XGBoost SHAP vectors), and orthogonally translated the emergent vulnerabilities in silico to predict in vivo mammalian tumor trajectory (PDX) and human overall survival (Marisa). In a fully unsupervised sweep of colorectal cancer transcriptomes, the pipeline autonomously identified Insulin-like Growth Factor 2 (IGF2) as a strictly bounded vulnerability to 5-Fluorouracil resistance. The discovery maintained significance after rigorous Benjamini-Hochberg false discovery rate correction (q=0.0292, Log-Rank p=0.0007 ) and successfully predicted significant in vivo tumor volume shrinkage in an independent mouse cohort (Mann-Whitney p=0.0373). By bridging the chasm between multi-agent reasoning and mathematically bounded clinical survival, this framework establishes a verifiable, zero-leakage paradigm for automated, end-to-end biomedical discovery.

</details>


### [237] [Preference-based Antibody Expression Ranking: Scaling with Large-scale Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16263)
*Josh Qixuan Sun,Morteza Babaie,Wenyang Hou,Mark Crowley,David Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于偏好学习的抗体表达排名框架，利用DPO适配蛋白质语言模型，解决标记数据稀缺问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究抗体设计中的表达排名任务，属于计算生物学/蛋白质工程领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：抗体表达排名（antibody expression ranking）；场景：抗体设计（antibody design）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：抗体序列。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 基于蛋白质语言模型（protein language model），引入union-masked log-likelihood approximation和IMGT-based alignment，适配Direct Preference Optimization (DPO)。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 内部数据集：1254条标记序列 + 400万条未标记骆驼科抗体序列；评价指标：摘要未具体说明指标名称。

Experiments: 在大多数指标上优于基线方法，证明偏好学习可从弱监督中有效学习。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术或思路。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于生物信息学/蛋白质工程领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Antibody expression ranking is a critical task in antibody design, yet its modelling is severely hindered by the scarcity of labeled experimental data. To address this, we propose a unified preference-based learning framework that integrates scarce quantitative expression data with large-scale weak positive supervision from immunization data. We adapt Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to protein language models by introducing a union-masked log-likelihood approximation and IMGT-based alignment, enabling efficient training on variable-length sequences. Evaluating on a diverse internal dataset of 1254 labeled sequences and 4 million unlabeled camelid-derived antibodies, we show that our method consistently outperforms baselines on most metrics. Our results demonstrate that preference learning can effectively learn from weak supervision, providing a scalable solution for antibody expressibility optimization in data-constrained settings. Project page: https://kisoji-biotechnology-inc.github.io/Preference-Expression-Ranking/.

</details>


### [238] [PsiLogic: Chaos-Aware Active Cancellation for Adam with a Fair Cross-Domain Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16268)
*Ali Sultonov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为PsiLogic的自适应优化器，通过双指数移动平均门控的动态主动抵消项增强Adam，在NLP、ViT、ResNet和扩散模型上取得更好或相当的验证指标，但引入1.2-1.8x的额外时间开销。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于通用深度学习优化器（Adam改进），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化/部署。虽然优化器改进可能间接影响任何模型的训练效率，但论文本身没有讨论遥感场景、目标检测任务或轻量化部署问题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：通用深度学习优化器设计。应用场景：NLP（语言建模）、ViT图像分类、ResNet图像分类、扩散模型。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 不涉及目标检测模型架构。核心贡献是优化器PsiLogic，在Adam基础上增加了一个由双EMA门控的动态Active Cancellation Term，作为混沌检测器，在训练不稳定时增强阻尼，稳定后衰减为零，实现隐式warmup。

Lightweight method: 不涉及模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。论文讨论的是优化器效率，但PsiLogic反而引入了1.2-1.8x的额外时间开销。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。GPU显存与Adam/AdamW相当，但wall-clock开销增加1.2-1.8x（受限于实现）。参数量、FLOPs、延迟、能耗等信息均未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 使用FairBench基准协议，包含4个arena：NLP（perplexity）、ViT（top-1 accuracy）、ResNet（top-1 accuracy）、扩散模型（validation MSE）。每个优化器进行学习率扫描，相同初始化种子，使用Welch t-test进行统计显著性检验。硬件：NVIDIA H100 80GB，2000步，bf16 AMP。

Experiments: PsiLogic在3/4的arena中取得最佳验证指标：NLP perplexity 7.79±0.18 vs 8.17±0.08 (AdamW, p=0.049)；ViT top-1 0.244±0.006 vs 0.223±0.002 (AdamW, p=0.015)；ResNet top-1 0.222±0.001 vs 0.172±0.004 (Adam, p=0.001)。扩散模型MSE与Adam/AdamW统计持平(p=0.49)。ResNet vs AdamW无显著差异(p=0.44)。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. PsiLogic引入1.2-1.8x额外时间开销，对资源受限的星上部署不利；3. 仅验证了2000步的短训练，未在遥感大模型或检测任务上验证；4. 实现层面的开销可能通过工程优化改善，但论文未讨论。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。如果未来研究方向涉及训练遥感目标检测模型时的优化器选择，可关注PsiLogic的隐式warmup机制是否有助于遥感小样本或域适应场景的训练稳定性。但考虑到PsiLogic增加了时间开销且未在检测任务上验证，当前不建议优先关注。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers such as Adam and AdamW apply the same update rule regardless of whether training is in a chaotic early phase or near convergence. We introduce PsiLogic, an optimizer that augments Adam with a dynamic Active Cancellation Term gated by a dual exponential moving average (EMA) of scale-normalized gradient norms. The resulting chaos detector strengthens damping when gradient statistics are unstable and fades to zero as training stabilizes, providing an implicit warmup without a hand-tuned schedule.
  We evaluate PsiLogic against Adam, AdamW, and Lion using FairBench -- a reproducible benchmark protocol with per-optimizer learning-rate sweeps, identical initialization per seed, and Welch t-tests. On an NVIDIA H100 80GB reference run (4 arenas, 3 seeds, 2000 steps, bf16 AMP), PsiLogic achieves the best validation metric in three of four arenas: NLP perplexity 7.79 +/- 0.18 vs. 8.17 +/- 0.08 (AdamW, p = 0.049), ViT top-1 accuracy 0.244 +/- 0.006 vs. 0.223 +/- 0.002 (AdamW, p = 0.015), and ResNet top-1 accuracy 0.222 +/- 0.001 vs. 0.172 +/- 0.004 (Adam, p = 0.001). On diffusion, validation MSE is statistically tied with Adam/AdamW (p = 0.49). ResNet accuracy vs. AdamW is a numerical tie without significance at three seeds (p = 0.44). Peak GPU memory is comparable across optimizers; PsiLogic incurs 1.2--1.8x wall-clock overhead on transformer-heavy arenas (implementation-bound).
  We release an open-source PyTorch implementation, the full FairBench harness, and all raw CSV outputs to support independent verification.

</details>


### [239] [A Predict-then-Correct Loop Based on Few-Shot Continuous Contextual Bandit for Demand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16354)
*Zhiwei Lei,Benedict Jun Ma,Ilya Jackson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个"先预测后修正"(PtC)框架，用少样本连续上下文bandit策略修正零售需求预测，不涉及遥感或目标检测。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦零售需求预测(retail demand forecasting)，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测(object detection)或模型轻量化(lightweight)方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：零售需求预测(retail demand forecasting)；应用场景：零售库存管理；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: Predict-then-Correct (PtC)框架：第一阶段为静态ML预测模型，第二阶段为few-shot continuous contextual bandit修正策略，包含similar-SKUs augmentation和top-p masked updating。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Walmart零售数据 + 独家饮料数据集(beverage dataset)；评价指标：MAPE, MAE, RMSE；实验设置：覆盖稳定高销量、稳定低销量、间歇性需求模式。

Experiments: PtC相比纯ML基线在消融实验中平均RMSE提升9.52%；相比base-stock、PPO、SAC策略在测试lead-time设置下库存成本更低；统计显著性检验通过。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感/检测/轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，无直接可借鉴点。但"先预测后修正"的在线校正思想在概念层面与在轨模型自适应更新有微弱类比可能，不过论文方法高度依赖零售领域特征(SKU相似性、需求模式分类)，难以迁移到遥感目标检测场景。

Abstract: Retail demand forecasting remains difficult when demand shifts faster than static forecasting models can be retrained, especially in early demand cycles where newly observed labels are sparse. To address this, this study aims to improve adaptive retail forecasting by proposing a predict-then-correct (PtC) framework that retains a first-stage machine learning (ML) forecast and applies a few-shot continuous contextual bandit correction policy with similar-SKUs augmentation and top-p masked updating. Across Walmart retail data and an exclusive beverage dataset, PtC delivers statistically significant reductions in MAPE, MAE, and RMSE across stable & high volume, stable & low volume, and erratic & intermittent demand patterns, improves average RMSE by 9.52% over the ML-only baseline in the ablation study, and yields lower inventory costs than base-stock, proximal policy optimization, and soft actor-critic policies under the tested lead-time settings. These findings show that online forecast correction can bridge offline demand learning and real-time retail decision-making by adapting to sparse feedback without fully retraining the base forecasting model.

</details>


### [240] [Retrieval is Enough: Training-Free Interpretability with a Tool-Using Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16448)
*Sriram Balasubramanian,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的假设驱动智能检索与探针方法（HARP），用于神经网络激活可解释性，在概念发现、检测、模型引导和秘密提取上优于基于训练的方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于神经网络激活可解释性（interpretability），属于AI可解释性/机械可解释性方向，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：神经网络激活可解释性（activation interpretability），概念发现与检测、模型引导、秘密提取。场景：LLM（大语言模型）内部表征分析。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: HARP框架：LLM agent + 向量数据库（存储激活及其文本上下文）+ 激活操作工具（投影、差分、平均）。核心是agent驱动的迭代检索-假设-验证循环，使用线性探针（linear probes）验证假设。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文强调"training-free"（无需训练），但这是指可解释性方法本身无需训练，与模型压缩/加速无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未明确说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：概念发现（concept discovery）、概念检测（concept detection）、模型引导（model steering）、秘密提取（secret elicitation）上的性能。

Experiments: HARP在概念发现、概念检测、模型引导和秘密提取四个任务上优于activation oracles和SAE-based agents。消融实验和对比细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测技术。论文聚焦于LLM内部表征的可解释性，属于完全不同的研究领域。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向无任何关联，不涉及目标检测、遥感图像处理、模型压缩或边缘部署等核心问题。

Abstract: Interpretability methods for neural network activations span a wide cost spectrum, from cheap, training-free techniques (such as linear probes, PCA, SVD) to more expensive training-based ones (such as SAEs and activation oracles). Training-based methods are typically more powerful, in part because they leverage large activation datasets during training. This raises a natural question - do they actually surface insights that go beyond what is recoverable from the training dataset itself? To address this, we equip an LLM agent with a vector database of activations paired with their textual contexts, along with tools for manipulating activations - projecting out directions in latent space, computing activation differences and averages. The agent iteratively queries the database, forms hypotheses from the retrieved samples, and validates them by constructing linear probes. We call this method HARP, for Hypothesis-driven Agentic Retrieval and Probing. Despite not involving any training, HARP outperforms both activation oracles and SAE-based agents on concept discovery, concept detection, model steering, and secret elicitation. The training-free design also makes HARP substantially cheaper and more flexible: new datasets can be indexed on demand whenever existing ones prove insufficient. More broadly, our results suggest that current training-based methods do not yet extract insights beyond their training data, and motivate benchmarks that explicitly require interpretability methods to demonstrate such insights. We release our code at https://github.com/SriramB-98/HARP

</details>


### [241] [EA-RMENet -- Path Loss Prediction in Urban Environments using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16449)
*Jonathan O'Shea,Conor Brennan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于U-Net+EfficientNetB5编码器+注意力门控跳跃连接+ASPP的无线电地图估计网络EA-RMENet，用于路径损耗预测，在RadioMapSeer3D数据集上取得0.0334 RMSE和0.022秒/样本的推理速度。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是无线通信中的路径损耗预测/无线电地图估计（Radio Map Estimation），属于无线网络规划领域，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景完全无关。不涉及目标检测（object detection）、小目标检测、旋转框检测等任何检测任务。不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、星上部署等模型压缩或边缘部署方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：无线电地图估计（Radio Map Estimation, RME）/路径损耗预测。场景：无线网络规划。传感器类型：无（基于图像数据的信号强度映射）。目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: U-Net框架，EfficientNetB5编码器，Attention Gated (AG) skip connections，Atrous Spatial Pyramid Pooling (ASPP)。

Lightweight method: 摘要未明确提及轻量化方法。EfficientNetB5本身通过compound scaling平衡精度与效率，但该模型并非为轻量化设计（EfficientNetB5参数量约30M，属于中等规模）。未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 推理速度：0.022秒/样本（约45 FPS），但这是在什么硬件平台上运行摘要未说明。参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等信息摘要均未说明。未涉及星上/边缘部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：RadioMapSeer3D。评价指标：RMSE（均方根误差）。竞赛：ICASSP 2023 Radio Map Prediction Challenge。

Experiments: 测试集RMSE 0.0334，推理时间0.022秒/样本。竞赛排名第三，RMSE 0.0406。摘要未提供与其他方法的详细对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴。2. 摘要未说明模型参数量、FLOPs等部署关键指标。3. 仅报告了RMSE，缺乏其他评价维度。4. 未说明硬件平台，推理速度的可比性存疑。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能参考的点：1. Attention Gated skip connections的设计思路可迁移到遥感目标检测的FPN/特征融合中，用于抑制背景噪声、增强目标区域特征。2. EfficientNet的compound scaling思想可用于设计遥感轻量检测网络的缩放策略。但整体而言，该论文与课题方向差异过大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Accurate path loss prediction is a critical component of wireless network planning. Current path loss prediction methods typically struggle to balance the trade-off between accuracy and computational efficiency. This paper proposes the Efficient Attention Radio Map Estimation Network (EA-RMENet) which is an image data-driven, deep learning (DL) model designed for radio map estimation (RME). EA-RMENet uses a U-Net framework with an EfficientNetB5 encoder, Attention Gated (AG) skip connections, and Atrous Spatial Pyramid Pooling (ASPP). The EfficientNet encoder uses compound scaling to balance accuracy and efficiency. AG skip connections suppress irrelevant features, and the ASPP captures a multi-scale context. The model has a test prediction RMSE of 0.0334 on the RadioMapSeer3D dataset with an inference time of 0.022 seconds/sample. In the ICASSP 2023 Radio Map Prediction Challenge, the model ranks third with a competitive RMSE of 0.0406 this highlights the models potential for real-world RME.

</details>


### [242] [Compact convolutional neural networks for AI-based drone detection system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16455)
*Gábor Farkas,Gábor Fazekas,Karakai Patrik,András Németh,Gábor Farkas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于轻量级CNN的无人机射频信号检测，将信号转换为光栅化时域图像，在嵌入式系统上实现实时RF监测，无需频域预处理。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文涉及轻量级CNN和嵌入式部署，但应用场景是无人机射频信号检测（RF detection），而非遥感卫星图像目标检测。不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感核心要素，也不涉及舰船/飞机/车辆等遥感目标检测任务。虽然轻量化方法有泛化参考价值，但场景完全不相关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，阅读优先级最低。仅当对RF信号检测与轻量CNN结合的边缘部署有交叉兴趣时可略读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：无人机射频信号检测（drone RF signal detection）；应用场景：电子战/频谱监测（electronic warfare, RF monitoring）；传感器类型：软件定义无线电（SDR）；目标类别：无人机射频信号。与遥感卫星目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 轻量级CNN（custom model architectures），具体结构摘要未说明。输入为光栅化时域图像（rasterized time-domain images），替代传统频谱图。

Lightweight method: 轻量级CNN架构设计（compact model architectures），具体轻量化手段（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）摘要未说明。模型设计目标是低计算需求、适合嵌入式系统。

Onboard deployability: 涉及嵌入式部署（embedded systems），集成到GNU Radio信号处理链进行实时评估（real-time evaluation）。但参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标摘要未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：约40,000张标注图像的自建数据集（drone RF signals）；评价指标：accuracy, model size, inference performance，具体数值摘要未说明。

Experiments: 设计了多种自定义模型架构进行benchmark对比，进行了离线测试和GNU Radio实时评估。与基于频谱图的RF检测方法相比，消除了频域预处理，计算成本显著降低且精度相当。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 轻量化方法的具体技术细节（如是否使用了剪枝/量化/蒸馏等）摘要未说明；3. 模型参数量、FLOPs、延迟等量化指标未给出；4. 数据集为自建RF信号数据集，非遥感图像数据集，无法直接迁移。

Ideas for my research: 1. 将信号转换为光栅化时域图像作为输入的思想，可类比遥感中不同模态数据的轻量级预处理策略；2. 在嵌入式系统上实现实时推理的部署思路（GNU Radio集成）对星上部署有参考价值；3. 但整体而言，该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的直接借鉴价值有限，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: The increasing use of first-person-view drones in modern conflicts has created a demand for compact and reliable detection systems capable of operating in complex electromagnetic environments. These drones continuously transmit video signals through onboard video transmitters, generating radio-frequency emissions that can be exploited for early detection. This study investigates the use of lightweight convolutional neural networks for automated detection of drone signals captured by a software-defined radio-based electronic warfare framework. Samples are converted into rasterized time-domain images, providing a computationally efficient input representation suitable for embedded systems. Several custom model architectures were designed and benchmarked in terms of accuracy, model size, and inference performance using a dataset containing approximately 40,000 labeled images. In addition to offline testing, the models were integrated into a GNU Radio signal processing chain for real-time evaluation. The results show that compact models can achieve high detection accuracy while maintaining low computational requirements, making them suitable for embedded radio-frequency monitoring applications. Compared with existing spectrogram-based RF detection methods, the proposed approach eliminates frequency-domain preprocessing and achieves comparable accuracy with significantly reduced computational cost.

</details>


### [243] [K-IPO: Kendall-constrained Importance Preserving Oversampling for Imbalanced Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16478)
*Marios Tyrovolas,Argiris Sofotasios,Dimitris Metaxakis,Georgios Mermigkis,George Georgoulas,Panagiotis Hadjidoukas,Chrysostomos Stylios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出K-IPO过采样框架，通过Kendall's tau约束保留特征重要性排序，解决类别不平衡分类中的解释失真问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于表格数据的类别不平衡过采样方法，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：表格二分类中的类别不平衡处理；应用场景：通用机器学习分类；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。K-IPO是一个"generate-then-select"框架，与生成器无关（generator-agnostic）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。仅提到"competitive computational overhead"（有竞争力的计算开销），但未给出具体数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：20个不平衡二分类表格数据集；评价指标：feature importance preservation（特征重要性保持）、explanation consistency（解释一致性）、class separability（类别可分性）、predictive performance（预测性能）。

Experiments: 使用3种分类器和多种解释方法在20个数据集上评估。K-IPO在特征重要性保持、解释一致性和类别可分性上达到最佳或并列最佳，同时通常能提升预测性能。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但该工作仅针对表格数据，未涉及图像/遥感数据；未讨论在目标检测任务上的适用性；未涉及任何轻量化或部署相关考量。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：如果遥感目标检测中存在类别不平衡（如小目标样本少），可考虑类似"generate-then-select"的样本筛选思路，但需将Kendall's tau约束适配到特征空间或检测框分布上。然而，该论文的方法本身不涉及遥感、目标检测或轻量化，直接借鉴意义不大。

Abstract: Oversampling is widely used to address class imbalance in tabular classification, but existing methods can distort the feature importance ranking underlying model explanations. Although recent studies have quantified this distortion by comparing real and synthetic data, none have actively sought to prevent it. In this paper, we introduce Kendall-constrained Importance-Preserving Oversampling (K-IPO), a generator-agnostic, "generate-then-select" framework that preserves the original data's feature importance ranking during augmentation. K-IPO iteratively generates minority-class candidates and accepts them only if their inclusion maintains a user-defined minimum Kendall's tau (τ) correlation with the reference ranking. Optionally, stricter constraints can be applied to the highest-ranked features. We evaluated K-IPO on 20 imbalanced binary classification datasets using three classifiers and multiple explanation methods. In most cases, K-IPO achieved the best or tied-best results in feature importance preservation, explanation consistency, and class separability. It also generally improved predictive performance while maintaining competitive computational overhead.

</details>


### [244] [Leakage-Robust Evaluation and Data-Scale Sensitivity of Attention-Enhanced Multi-Task Learning for Joint Fault Diagnosis and Remaining Useful Life Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16493)
*Md Mahamudur Rahaman Shamim,Md. Nuruzzaman,Zannatul Ferdus,Md Rajib Ahmed,Abieer Nwshad Anward,Mohammad Tooneer,Johir Uddin Khan,Khalid Hossen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究多任务深度学习模型在预测性维护中的故障分类与剩余寿命回归问题，关注滑动窗口数据划分导致性能虚高的问题，并提出无泄漏划分协议。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是工业预测性维护（predictive maintenance）中的故障分类和剩余寿命（RUL）回归，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱或空间在轨智能等遥感场景。也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等视觉检测任务。轻量化方面仅提及参数量（约1/3参数用于卷积分支），但未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等轻量化核心方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多任务学习（故障分类 + RUL回归）；应用场景：工业预测性维护（predictive maintenance）；传感器类型：时序传感器数据（非遥感图像）；目标类别：机械故障类别。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: AMTLNet（Attention-enhanced Multi-Task Learning Network），包含multi-head attention分支和convolutional分支。多任务共享特征提取，分别输出分类和回归结果。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法。未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。仅提及卷积分支约占1/3参数但对回归贡献小，但未给出具体参数量数值。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：NASA C-MAPSS（航空发动机）、NASA IMS（轴承）、UCI Hydraulic System（液压系统）。评价指标：分类准确率（accuracy）、R²（回归指标）、Tukey HSD检验、one-way ANOVA。均为时序传感器数据集，非遥感图像数据集。

Experiments: 主要发现：naive数据划分可导致分类准确率从真实20-60%虚高至99.9%或降至0%。提出chunk-based leakage-audited splitting protocol。在C-MAPSS上AMTLNet分类准确率84.12±0.96%，R²=0.86±0.01。消融实验表明multi-head attention分支对回归稳定性贡献大，移除后R²从0.861降至0.766，分类方差翻倍。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可借鉴的技术方法。研究的是时序数据多任务学习中的数据泄漏问题，非视觉检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点：（1）多任务学习中数据划分避免泄漏的思想，在遥感多任务（如检测+分割）中也有参考价值；（2）消融实验分析各分支贡献的方法论可借鉴，用于分析轻量化模型中各模块的重要性。但整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接借鉴价值很低。

Abstract: Multi-task deep learning models that jointly perform fault classification and remaining useful life (RUL) regression are increasingly used in predictive maintenance, yet reported performance can be strongly affected by how sliding-window sequences are split into training and test sets. We investigate this issue using AMTLNet, an attention-enhanced multi-task architecture, on three public benchmarks: NASA C-MAPSS, NASA IMS, and the UCI Hydraulic System dataset. We show that naive splitting can inflate classification accuracy from a genuine 20-60 percent to 99.9 percent, or reduce it to 0 percent through degenerate class representation. To address this, we introduce a chunk-based, leakage-audited splitting protocol and evaluate all models using five seeds, one-way ANOVA, and Tukey HSD tests. On C-MAPSS, with 19,976 leakage-free training windows, AMTLNet matches a single-task CNN-LSTM baseline in classification, achieving 84.12 +/- 0.96 percent accuracy with Tukey p = 1.0, and reaches an R2 of 0.86 +/- 0.01 while significantly outperforming a naive multi-task baseline. On the smaller Bearing and Hydraulic datasets, multi-task training is unstable, but the failure mode differs: classification degrades for Bearing, whereas regression degrades for Hydraulic. We relate this asymmetry to label provenance and propose a practical framework for deciding when joint training is appropriate under data scarcity. Ablation results show that the multi-head attention branch is the main contributor to regression stability. Removing it reduces R2 from 0.861 to 0.766 and more than doubles classification variance, whereas the convolutional branch contributes little to regression despite using about one-third of the parameters. This study contributes a reusable leakage-audit protocol, seed-transparent evaluation, and evidence that task-specific stability depends more on label provenance than on task type.

</details>


### [245] [Feedback Attribution and Representation Geometry: Metrics for Comparing Individual and Shared Rewards in MARL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16524)
*Tasha Pais,Richard Higgins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体强化学习中团队平均奖励与个体奖励对表征几何和行为的影响，提出EffRank/n和D_act作为低开销诊断指标，并在SMACv2星际争霸环境中验证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究多智能体强化学习（MARL）中的奖励分配机制对表征的影响，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 多智能体强化学习（MARL），星际争霸SMACv2环境，不涉及遥感场景、目标检测或任何视觉感知任务。

Model architecture: MAPPO（Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization）智能体，不涉及遥感目标检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文提出的EffRank/n和D_act是低开销诊断指标（<5% overhead），但这是针对MARL表征分析的，与模型压缩/轻量化无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: SMACv2 protoss_5_vs_5环境；指标包括EffRank/n（有效秩归一化）、D_act（智能体动作分布间的平均KL散度）、探针准确率（probe accuracy）。

Experiments: 在SMACv2上对比观测类型（单位类型可见vs.掩码）× 奖励类型（个体伤害贡献奖励vs.共享团队奖励）四种条件，发现表征几何主要由观测决定而非奖励类型。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于多智能体强化学习领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent RL systems routinely use team-averaged rewards, a feedback-attribution choice that gives each agent the team outcome regardless of its individual contribution. We ask whether this leaves a measurable signature, geometric or behavioral, on learned representations. We propose EffRank/$n$ (effective rank normalized by agent count) and $D_\text{act}$ (mean pairwise KL divergence between agents' action distributions) as low-overhead diagnostics for reward-attribution effects, then test them on competent MAPPO agents in SMACv2 \texttt{protoss\_5\_vs\_5}, where unit type is encoded in the observation. In an observation $\times$ reward-attribution comparison (unit type observed vs.\ masked; individual damage-contribution reward vs.\ shared team reward), geometry follows observation rather than reward. With unit type observed, shared and individual rewards have similar EffRank/$n$ ($0.31{\pm}0.03$ vs.\ $0.29{\pm}0.02$) and probe accuracy ($0.75{\pm}0.05$ vs.\ $0.73{\pm}0.05$, both $\gg 1/3$ chance), while $D_\text{act}$ leans higher under individual rewards ($1.23{\pm}0.06$ vs.\ $1.07{\pm}0.20$). Masking unit type cuts the above-chance probe signal by more than half, to $0.49$ in both reward arms. In short: individually rewarded agents are competent and separable by role, but on SMACv2 the observation explains the geometry and reward attribution shows up mainly in behavior. Thus geometric diagnostics must control for observed role information and test persistent roles that are not directly observed. EffRank/$n$ and $D_\text{act}$ add $<$5\% overhead.

</details>


### [246] [Hierarchical Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16528)
*Chenxiao Yang,Zhiyuan Li,Shai Ben-David,Nathan Srebro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究层次域泛化的理论问题，探讨从有限观测区域到整个实例空间的外推，与遥感、目标检测或轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文是纯机器学习理论（domain generalization）研究，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个方向，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文为纯理论工作，未涉及任何具体任务、场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何模型结构或网络设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未提及任何数据集或评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文未提供任何实验结果、对比方法或消融实验。

Limitations: 该论文为纯理论分析，不涉及任何工程应用或具体算法实现，无法直接为遥感在轨目标检测提供技术支撑。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。该论文讨论的层次域泛化理论虽然可能在遥感场景泛化（如不同卫星传感器、不同地理区域的域偏移）中有潜在理论联系，但摘要中未给出任何与遥感或检测相关的具体方法或结论，无法直接借鉴。

Abstract: We study hierarchical domain generalization as a problem of extrapolation from finite observed regions to an entire instance space, replacing i.i.d. sampling with arbitrary domain hierarchies. We show that the central obstruction is not only the complexity of the hypothesis class, but the train/test domain partition through which evidence is revealed. In particular, no matter how small the class or how large the training size, some partition makes generalization fail for some target. These results suggest that modern generalization theory must treat domain structure as a first-class object.

</details>


### [247] [Building2Building: A Large Scale Benchmark for Generalizable Real-World Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16534)
*Vincent Taboga,Justin Veilleux,Doseok Jang,Anushree Rankawat,Pierre-Luc Bacon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于EnergyPlus建筑模拟器的HVAC控制环境套件B2B，用于研究强化学习中的泛化与迁移问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦建筑暖通空调（HVAC）控制中的强化学习泛化问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：强化学习控制（HVAC控制）；应用场景：建筑能源管理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及使用RL策略。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文讨论的是建筑环境中的RL部署，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: B2B套件（基于EnergyPlus的建筑模拟器），Gymnasium接口；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标。

Experiments: 定义了目标适应、动力学适应、动作空间迁移、跨域迁移等基准任务；具体实验结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于强化学习控制领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无交叉。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved strong results in control, yet learned policies remain brittle to changes in dynamics, action spaces, observation spaces, or goals, a critical limitation for real-world deployment. Existing benchmarks offer limited diversity and complexity, making it difficult to rigorously study transfer, multi-task learning, and meta-learning in RL. We introduce Building2Building (B2B), a large-scale suite of realistic Heating, Ventilation, and Air Conditioning (HVAC) control environments built on EnergyPlus, a state-of-the-art building simulator. B2B is fully compatible with the Gymnasium interface and features a parametric building generator, enabling the systematic generation of diverse building configurations with heterogeneous observation and action spaces. Based on this suite, we define benchmark tasks targeting key open challenges in RL, including goal adaptation, dynamics adaptation, action-space shifts, and cross-domain transfer. By providing a large-scale, diverse, and physically grounded testbed with standardized evaluation protocols, B2B enables systematic investigation of generalization and transfer in continuous control. Beyond advancing research on generalization in RL, this new benchmark also carries significant societal implications by enabling improved HVAC control at scale, one of the most energy-intensive systems in buildings.

</details>


### [248] [Discrete Ricci Curvature on Protein Contact Graphs for Lightweight Fold Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16553)
*Jianru Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究蛋白质折叠分类，提出基于离散Ricci曲率的轻量级结构描述符（22维），在CATH和SCOPe数据集上以极低维度超越ESM-2蛋白质语言模型嵌入。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究对象是蛋白质折叠分类（protein fold classification），属于计算生物学/生物信息学领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然论文标题和摘要中出现了"lightweight"一词，但这里的轻量化指的是轻量级结构描述符（22维特征向量），与深度学习模型压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS）或星上/边缘部署完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对蛋白质结构分类有交叉兴趣，否则对遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题帮助极小。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：蛋白质折叠分类（protein fold classification）；应用场景：计算生物学/结构生物学；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：CATH拓扑分类、SCOPe折叠分类。

Model architecture: 基于离散Ricci曲率（Ollivier-Ricci和Forman-Ricci）的轻量级结构描述符，从Cα接触图的边曲率分布中提取22维统计特征（摘要统计量和分位数）。对比基线包括几何特征、接触图统计、持续同调（persistent homology）以及ESM-2（150M参数）蛋白质语言模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及深度学习模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。论文中的"lightweight"指手工设计的22维特征描述符本身维度低、计算轻量，而非模型压缩技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CATH top-10 Topology分类数据集、ASTRAL 40%-identity SCOPe top-10 Fold基准数据集；评价指标：macro-F1。

Experiments: 1) 22维Ricci曲率描述符在CATH和SCOPe上均显著优于均值池化的ESM-2（150M）嵌入；2) Ricci描述符仅用ESM-2基线维度的3.4%即实现超越；3) Ricci + 持续同调组合最优，CATH上macro-F1=0.71，SCOPe上macro-F1=0.68，特征向量112维。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2) 研究对象为蛋白质结构数据，非图像/遥感数据；3) 轻量化的定义不同，本文是手工特征设计而非模型压缩；4) 未涉及任何部署相关指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。可能的间接启发：1) 手工设计轻量特征与深度特征对比的思路可以类比到遥感领域，例如设计轻量级遥感图像特征描述符与CNN/ViT对比；2) 低维特征在高维预训练模型面前仍有竞争力的现象，提示在星上资源受限场景下可探索手工特征+轻量分类器的方案。但整体而言，本文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的方法论差异较大。

Abstract: Protein fold classification can be approached via sequence-based representations or structural descriptors, but direct comparisons between lightweight handcrafted descriptors and pretrained protein language model embeddings remain limited. We investigate discrete Ricci curvature on Calpha contact graphs as a lightweight structural descriptor for fold classification. Each protein domain is represented by a 22-dimensional fixed-length feature derived from summary statistics and quantiles of Ollivier-Ricci and Forman-Ricci edge curvature distributions. We evaluate on CATH top-10 Topology classification and on the ASTRAL 40%-identity SCOPe top-10 Fold benchmark, comparing against geometry, contact-graph statistics, persistent homology, and mean-pooled ESM-2 (150M) baselines. On both datasets, lightweight structural descriptors substantially outperform mean-pooled ESM-2 embeddings, with a larger performance gap on the ASTRAL 40% SCOPe benchmark. Ricci alone uses 22 dimensions, or 3.4% of the ESM-2 baseline dimensionality, and already outperforms mean-pooled ESM-2 on both datasets. Combining Ricci with persistent homology yields the strongest performance, achieving macro-F1 of 0.71 on CATH and 0.68 on SCOPe with a 112-dimensional feature vector. These results identify a regime where lightweight interpretable graph descriptors offer a practical alternative to pretrained protein language model embeddings.

</details>


### [249] [Capacity and Redundancy Trade-offs in Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16554)
*Asif Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度提出容量-冗余（CR）恒等式，分析多任务学习中的负迁移问题，并通过聚类LoRA方法降低任务间干扰。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦多任务学习（MTL）的理论分析，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。未提及遥感、卫星、目标检测、场景理解等任何具体任务或应用场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提及了LoRA（Low-Rank Adaptation）作为聚类共享的实现方式。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。LoRA本身是一种参数高效微调方法，但论文并未从轻量化或部署角度讨论。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅提及"validation residual correlations"作为估计残差耦合的手段。

Experiments: 实验表明聚类LoRA降低了残差耦合Δ̂，优于随机划分，且具有统计显著性（多种子置信区间）。但未提供与遥感或目标检测相关的实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未讨论轻量化部署；3. 理论分析基于高斯多任务模型假设，实际遥感场景的适用性未知；4. 未提供计算效率指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。可借鉴点有限：1. CR恒等式中关于任务冗余（total correlation）的分析思路，可能对遥感多任务模型（如同时检测+分割）的任务相关性分析有理论参考价值；2. 聚类LoRA的思路可用于遥感多任务模型的参数高效微调，但需验证在遥感场景下的有效性。整体借鉴价值较低。

Abstract: In multi-task learning (MTL) negative transfer is often considered as an optimization artifact, but it can also be viewed as a consequence of limited shared capacity and weak task redundancy. We investigate this effect through a Capacity--Redundancy (CR) identity that decomposes the sum of per-task predictive informations into joint predictive information that includes label redundancy defined via total correlation (TC), and a residual coupling term that quantifies interference left unresolved by the shared representation. Additionally, we show two key results: (i) a clustering-gap decomposition that gives a necessary and sufficient condition for clustered sharing to outperform global sharing, and (ii) a gradient--TC bridge in a Gaussian multi-task model that formally justifies gradient cosine similarity as a proxy for redundancy ordering. Empirically, we estimate the residual coupling $Δ$ from validation residual correlations, showing that clustered LoRA substantially reduces $\widehatΔ$, outperforms size-matched random partitions, and results in statistically significant gains with multi-seed confidence intervals.

</details>


### [250] [Learning from World Feedback: Why Model Uncertainty Fails as a Risk Signal in Model-Based RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16591)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RLxF框架，主张学习信号应来自世界反馈而非模型内部代理，在基于模型的安全控制中验证，发现MBRL安全代理与真实安全性反相关，用世界反馈信号替代后可大幅降低碰撞率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于基于模型的安全控制（model-based safe control）和强化学习中的安全代理问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然提到了"proxy"（代理）概念与轻量化中的代理指标有抽象相似性，但应用场景、技术路线、评估指标均无关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：基于模型的安全控制（model-based safe control）；应用场景：机器人/自动驾驶安全控制；传感器类型：LiDAR；目标类别：碰撞/安全边界。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 四种world-model架构（跨度2x MSE范围）；MPC规划器；dynamics-based uncertainty penalty；outcome-trained feedback model g_psi。与遥感目标检测模型无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 实验设置：n=200的TOST统计检验；碰撞率（collision rates 26%→34%→1-14%）；MSE范围；相关性r < 0.15。与遥感数据集无关。

Experiments: 主要发现：MBRL安全代理与真实安全性反相关（碰撞率从26%升至34%）；用三种世界反馈信号（LiDAR margin、time-to-collision、outcome-supervised feedback model）替代后碰撞率降至1-14%。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供直接借鉴。局限性在于其结论（安全代理反相关）仅在特定控制场景下验证，不适用于视觉检测任务。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点：论文提出的"验证代理指标与真实目标的一致性"这一思想具有通用性——在轻量化模型部署时，需要验证压缩后的代理指标（如FLOPs减少）是否与真实部署性能（如推理延迟、检测精度）正相关。但整体而言，对遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的直接可借鉴点极少。

Abstract: The RLxF programme argues that learning signals should come from world feedback rather than from internal model proxies. We instantiate this position in safe model-based control and distil it into three concrete design principles. Empirically, across four world-model architectures spanning a 2x MSE range, MPC planning is statistically equivalent (TOST, n=200), and dynamics-based uncertainty penalties increase collision rates from 26% to 34%: the standard MBRL safety proxy is anti-correlated with safety in this regime. Replacing the model-internal proxy with three world-feedback signals (a sensor-derived margin via minimum lidar, a temporal signal via time-to-collision, and an outcome-supervised feedback model g_psi trained on prior collision labels, structurally analogous to outcome-trained reward models in RLHF) reduces collisions to 1-14% without retraining the world model or the planner. The mechanism is structural: model uncertainty has support over state-prediction space, whereas task risk has support over constraint boundaries, with empirical correlation r < 0.15. From this we extract three RLxF principles (ground risk in world outcomes, validate proxies before deployment, and substitute outcome-trained feedback models when direct world signals are unavailable) and argue they apply equally to model-based control and to verifier-based or RLHF approaches in LLM alignment.

</details>


### [251] [Privacy Cost as Equity Input: A Group Fairness Criterion for Differentially Private Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16620)
*Rakshit Naidu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出隐私-成本公平性指标PCER，用于审计差分隐私机器学习系统中不同群体间的隐私泄露不平等问题，与遥感在轨目标检测无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。核心问题是差分隐私（DP）系统中的群体公平性审计，属于机器学习公平性与隐私保护交叉领域，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文涉及的是差分隐私机器学习系统中的公平性审计，任务为群体隐私泄露风险评估，应用场景为tabular和NLP领域的基准数据集（如COMPAS），不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及任何目标检测或遥感模型架构，仅提及DP-SGD作为差分隐私训练方法。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计、实时推理或边缘部署等任何模型轻量化或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等任何与星上/边缘部署相关的指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：COMPAS及tabular和NLP领域的六个基准数据集。评价指标：PCER（Privacy-Cost Equity Ratio，隐私-成本公平性比率），基于每组的正预测率除以每组的过拟合差距（overfitting gap），以及标准公平性指标。不涉及遥感数据集或目标检测评价指标。

Experiments: 在DP-SGD不同隐私预算下，对六个基准数据集进行实验，验证PCER指标的有效性，并与标准公平性指标对比。发现COMPAS上受保护群体面临"双重劣势"：更高的隐私泄露风险和更差的预测结果。未涉及目标检测实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无可借鉴的检测或轻量化方法。2. 强隐私保护下过拟合差距趋近数值下限，导致基于暴露的审计失效。3. 仅在tabular和NLP数据上验证，未涉及图像或遥感数据。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人研究方向基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。但可间接思考：如果在轨目标检测系统中引入差分隐私保护（如保护卫星图像中的敏感目标位置信息），可借鉴其公平性审计思路，但这属于非常边缘的交叉方向，且论文本身未涉及遥感场景。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) is increasingly deployed to limit membership inference risk in machine-learning systems. Prior work has shown that DP-SGD can widen accuracy disparities across demographic groups, but this framing treats fairness as a purely outcome-side concern. We argue that privacy cost, the information leakage borne by each group, is itself a form of harm, and adopt a compensatory-fairness framework in which a group that involuntarily bears greater privacy exposure is owed proportionally greater benefit from the system. From this principle we derive the \emph{Privacy-Cost Equity Ratio} (PCER), a group fairness metric defined as a group's positive prediction rate normalized by its per-group overfitting gap. By a standard membership inference bound, this overfitting gap upper-bounds each group's vulnerability to inference attacks, making PCER a conservative measure of benefit relative to exposure. PCER needs only per-group train and test accuracy (no shadow models), making it a practical post-hoc audit tool. We evaluate PCER alongside standard fairness metrics across six benchmark--attribute combinations spanning tabular and NLP domains, under DP-SGD at a range of privacy budgets, and validate the overfitting-gap proxy against a direct threshold membership-inference attack. The results reveal patterns that outcome-based metrics miss. On COMPAS, PCER uncovers a persistent double disadvantage: the protected group bears both greater privacy exposure and worse predictive outcomes, something demographic parity gap masks entirely. Sensitivity analysis shows very strong privacy guarantees collapse both groups' overfitting to a numerical floor, rendering exposure-based audits uninformative in that regime. Together, these findings show that fairness audits of privacy-preserving systems must account for who bears the cost of protection, not only who benefits from its outcomes.

</details>


### [252] [Multimodal Attention-based Deep Learning for Emergency Triage with Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16662)
*Hazqeel Afyq Athaillah Kamarul Aryffin,Kamarul Aryffin Baharuddin,Mohd Halim Mohd Noor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多模态深度学习架构，融合表格数据（生命体征）和文本数据（主诉）进行急诊分诊决策，利用自注意力机制捕捉特征间局部与全局关系。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医疗急诊分诊（emergency triage），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关领域，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分，与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：急诊分诊决策（emergency triage decision）；场景：医院急诊科；数据类型：表格数据（生命体征）+ 文本数据（主诉）；目标类别：分诊优先级。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 多模态深度学习架构，融合tabular data和textual data，使用self-attention机制捕捉局部与全局特征关系。具体网络结构摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：马来西亚理科大学医院急诊科收集的11,102条分诊数据；评价指标：Accuracy、F1-score、ROC AUC。

Experiments: 与baseline模型相比，Accuracy提升1.95%，F1-score提升2.49%，ROC AUC提升1.41%。摘要未说明baseline具体方法，也未提供消融实验或对比多种方法的结果。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感、检测或轻量化内容。摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无直接可借鉴点。唯一可能间接相关的点是其多模态融合思路（tabular + textual），但遥感领域多模态通常指光学+SAR、多光谱+高光谱等，场景差异极大，借鉴价值极低。

Abstract: Accurate emergency triage decision is critical to avoid clinical deterioration, morbidity, and mortality. Machine learning-based triage system involves acquiring the main presenting complaint in text form and assessing vital signs in numerical data, enabling an automated and efficient analysis of patient information for timely and accurate prioritization of medical attention. However, modelling the intricacies of both data types requires a comprehensive understanding of the temporal structure and dependencies within the data. Thus, the aim of this study is to propose a multimodal deep learning architecture that can effectively handle both tabular and textual data. Furthermore, the proposed model exploits self-attention to to capture both local and global relationships between the features. A dataset consisting of 11,102 triage data collected from emergency department of Hospital Universiti Sains Malaysia is used for model development and validation. The proposed model demonstrated an increase of 1.95% in accuracy, 2.49% in F1-score, and 1.41% in ROC AUC compared to the baseline model. The experimental results demonstrated the potential of the proposed model in predicting triage decisions.

</details>


### [253] [CLDRoute: Conditional Latent Diffusion for Routability Map Generation in Physical Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16674)
*Kiran Thorat,Nicole Meng,Caiwen Ding,Yingjie Lao,Zhijie Jerry Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CLDRoute，将物理设计中的可布线性估计建模为条件生成问题，使用潜扩散模型生成拥塞和DRC违规图，并输出不确定性估计。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于芯片物理设计（EDA领域）中的可布线性估计，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及模型轻量化或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：可布线性估计（routability estimation），属于EDA/芯片物理设计领域。场景：芯片布局阶段后的拥塞和DRC违规预测。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于条件潜扩散模型（Conditional Latent Diffusion），包含物理感知条件（physics-aware conditioning）和任务特定潜建模（task-specific latent modeling）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。且该任务面向EDA工具链，与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CircuitNet 2.0 (N28)。评价指标：SSIM、MAE、TopK@1%（DRC违规生成）；SSIM、MAE、NZ-Pearson（拥塞生成）。

Experiments: 在CircuitNet 2.0上，DRC违规生成SSIM=0.9678, MAE=0.0028, TopK@1%=0.3494；拥塞生成SSIM=0.9031, MAE=0.0286, NZ-Pearson=0.3692。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可借鉴的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。潜扩散模型用于生成不确定性估计的思路（mean prediction + spatial uncertainty）在遥感目标检测中可能有一定启发，例如对检测结果的不确定性建模，但与本课题核心方向（轻量化、在轨部署）无直接关联。

Abstract: Accurate routability estimation during physical design is important for reducing costly post-routing iterations. Prior learning-based methods treat this task as deterministic prediction, mapping placement-stage features to a single congestion or DRC outcome. We instead formulate routability estimation as a conditional generation problem, where both routing congestion and DRC violations are modeled as spatially structured routability fields. Our framework, Conditional Latent Diffusion for Routeability estimation (CLDRoute), uses physics-aware conditioning and task-specific latent modeling to handle the different characteristics of congestion and DRC maps. This allows our method to supports sample-based inference, producing both a mean prediction and a spatial uncertainty estimate for the same input design. On CircuitNet 2.0 (N28), our method achieves, for DRC violation generation, an SSIM of 0.9678, an MAE of 0.0028, and a TopK@1% of 0.3494; for congestion generation, it achieves an SSIM of 0.9031, an MAE of 0.0286, and an NZ-Pearson of 0.3692. Overall, our framework provides a more practical view of routability at placement by generating both the expected outcome and its uncertainty.

</details>


### [254] [A Framework for Early Sepsis Prediction via Self-Supervised (JEPA) and Federated Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16681)
*Umair bin Mansoor,Munaf Rashid,Roomi Naqvi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了多种自监督/半监督/监督范式用于早期脓毒症预测，基于MIMIC-III电子健康记录数据，与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是医学领域的早期脓毒症预测（early sepsis prediction），使用电子健康记录（EHR）数据，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题。也不涉及目标检测、轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等方向。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：早期脓毒症预测（时序分类/预测）；场景：临床医疗；传感器/数据源：电子健康记录（EHR）；目标类别：脓毒症发作预测。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构，掩码潜在预测）、VICReg（方差-不变性-协方差正则化，双视图增强）、半监督微调VICReg预训练编码器、有监督TCN（时序卷积网络）、XGBoost。与遥感目标检测模型无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。且应用场景为医疗而非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MIMIC-III；评价指标：AUPRC（主要）、AUPRC随时间衰减率（H0到H10）。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10、SSDD、SARDet等）无关。

Experiments: 最佳模型JEPA+XGBoost+mean pooling在发病时刻（H0）AUPRC=0.636，接近SupMix基准（0.667）但少用83%生物标志物；Tier1（VICReg预训练+半监督微调+XGBoost）AUPRC=0.510，比原始特征基线（0.165）提升3.1倍，比有监督TCN（0.474）提升7.6%；微调VICReg编码器时间鲁棒性最好（H0到H10仅衰减16.8%）。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感相关方法或结论。医学时序预测与遥感图像目标检测在数据模态、任务定义、评价体系上差异巨大。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。其自监督预训练（VICReg、JEPA）思路在医学时序数据上的应用方式与遥感图像目标检测差异较大，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Early sepsis prediction from electronic health records is challenged by irregular sampling, high missingness, and class imbalance. We systematically compare four modeling paradigms -- self-supervised Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) via masked latent prediction, self-supervised VICReg (variance-invariance-covariance regularization) with two-view augmentation, semi-supervised fine-tuning of a VICReg-pretrained encoder, and supervised Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) -- alongside raw-feature baselines. All models share a common preprocessing pipeline of hourly binning with forward-fill imputation applied to 7 biomarkers selected via sparsity analysis from the MIMIC-III dataset. Our best model (JEPA + XGBoost + mean pooling) achieves AUPRC 0.636 at the time of onset (H0), approaching the SupMix benchmark (0.667) while using 83\% fewer biomarkers. The Tier 1 pipeline -- VICReg pretraining followed by semi-supervised fine-tuning and XGBoost -- achieves AUPRC 0.510 at H0, a 3.1$\times$ improvement over the raw-feature baseline (0.165) and a 7.6\% improvement over the end-to-end supervised TCN (0.474). Crucially, the fine-tuned VICReg encoder exhibits the most temporally persistent representations, degrading only 16.8\% from H0 to H10 compared to 47.5\% for supervised TCN and 65.3\% for JEPA, demonstrating that self-supervised pretraining with task-aware fine-tuning yields features that are both sharp near onset and robust across prediction horizons.

</details>


### [255] [Building a Neural Network from Scratch: Implementation, Evaluation, and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16682)
*Yuanzhe Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从头实现了一个不依赖自动微分和深度学习库的神经网络框架，主要用于教学目的，帮助理解前向/反向传播、梯度动力学和优化机制。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于纯教学性质的深度学习底层实现工作。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。仅提及多分类任务，未涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体架构细节。提及多层架构、多种激活函数、正则化技术和优化器，但未给出具体网络结构名称。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅提及多分类任务验证了正确性、数值稳定性和泛化能力。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比方法。仅称在多分类任务上验证了框架的有效性。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但明显缺乏与遥感、目标检测、轻量化部署的任何关联，且未在真实大规模遥感数据集上验证。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。虽然"从零实现"的思想可用于教学或底层算子优化理解，但与在轨遥感目标检测轻量化的核心问题（检测精度-速度-资源约束权衡）无直接关系。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of high-level deep learning libraries, while accelerating model development, has increasingly abstracted away the internal mechanics of neural networks, creating a gap between practical usage and fundamental understanding. To address this, the paper presents a self-contained neural network framework implemented entirely from scratch -- without relying on automatic differentiation or pre-built deep learning modules. The implementation encompasses all essential components, including multi-layer architectures, diverse activation functions, regularization techniques, and state-of-the-art optimizers. Beyond serving as a pedagogical instrument that demystifies forward/backward propagation, gradient dynamics, and optimization landscapes, the framework demonstrates robust performance when applied to a multi-class classification task, successfully validating its correctness, numerical stability, and generalization across varied configurations. The extensible design and clean modularity further position it as a reliable baseline for educational purposes and future research exploration.

</details>


### [256] [Effects of width-dependent model hyperparameters and $\ell_2$-regularization on the loss landscape of two-layer ReLU networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16720)
*Haruka Eshima,Makoto Yamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论角度分析了两层ReLU网络在权重衰减下的全局极小值坍塌条件，以及AdamW和SGD优化器对参数坍塌的不同影响，并给出了输入维度为1时的全局最优解析解。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文是纯理论机器学习研究，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署中的任何一个核心要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 两层ReLU网络（two-layer ReLU networks），属于基础神经网络架构，非遥感目标检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是权重衰减（weight decay, $\ell_2$-regularization）对损失景观的理论影响，并非剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何遥感或视觉数据集及评价指标。

Experiments: 实验仅涉及AdamW与SGD优化器在两层ReLU网络上的参数坍塌对比，以及一维输入下的解析解推导。无遥感目标检测实验。

Limitations: 论文为纯理论分析，仅针对两层ReLU网络和一维输入特例，结论对深层网络、高维输入、实际遥感检测任务的泛化性未说明。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。唯一可能的间接借鉴点是：论文揭示了AdamW优化器在防止参数坍塌方面的优势，这或许对遥感在轨模型训练时的优化器选择有一定参考价值，但非常间接。整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题几乎没有直接可借鉴的技术点。

Abstract: Understanding deep neural networks remains a central challenge in machine learning. In particular, the theoretical properties of even two-layer ReLU networks, especially in the presence of weight decay, remain poorly understood. To this end, we derive a sufficient condition on the hyperparameter settings under which the global minima collapse to the zero solution. Interestingly, our experiments reveal that using AdamW as an optimizer prevents the collapse of the learned parameters, whereas using SGD does not, which may help explain the success of AdamW in deep learning training. In addition, when restricting the input dimension to one, we derive an analytical solution for the globally optimal parameter sets of two-layer ReLU networks and show that $\ell_2$-regularization has a width-invariant effect on connectivity, but its dimensionality-reducing effect becomes stronger as the network width increases. These results provide insight into how width-dependent hyperparameters influence the geometry of regularized loss landscapes.

</details>


### [257] [Half the Experts, All the Code: One-Shot Domain Pruning of Mixture-of-Experts LLMs for Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16721)
*Anik Jha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究MoE大语言模型中专家子网络的剪枝策略，发现可移除半数专家而不影响代码生成能力，但剪枝策略在不同模型族间不可迁移，且困惑度指标不可靠。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究对象是MoE大语言模型（Qwen、Gemma）的专家剪枝，面向代码生成任务，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然剪枝方法本身属于轻量化技术，但论文讨论的是LLM领域的专家剪枝，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，属于LLM领域的专家剪枝研究，不涉及遥感、目标检测或视觉任务。除非对剪枝方法论有广泛兴趣，否则不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：MoE大语言模型的专家剪枝（expert pruning）；应用场景：代码生成（coding）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: MoE（Mixture-of-Experts）网络架构，具体模型为Qwen3.6-35B-A3B和Gemma-4-26B-A4B，包含大量专家子网络（expert subnetworks），每个token仅激活少数专家。

Lightweight method: 专家剪枝（expert pruning）：从MoE模型中移除部分专家子网络以减少模型规模。比较了五种专家选择策略（selection strategies），并与量化（quantization）方法进行了对比。还尝试了轻量微调（lightweight fine-tune）来恢复剪枝损失。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及模型原始规模（35B-A3B和26B-A4B），以及剪枝后与量化到3 bits以下的内存对比。不涉及星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 主要评价指标：代码基准测试（code benchmark）的正确率；困惑度（perplexity）。摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 主要发现：(1) 两个模型均可移除半数专家而代码能力无统计显著下降；(2) 剪枝损失主要集中在编码能力之外的任务上；(3) 最优剪枝策略在两个模型族间不一致；(4) 困惑度指标不可靠，可能将损坏模型评为优于完整模型；(5) 轻量微调可恢复激进剪枝损失的一半；(6) 剪枝仅在量化需降至3 bits/weight以下时优于量化；(7) 五次尝试推翻该结论均失败。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；(2) 剪枝策略在不同模型族间不可迁移，需要逐模型验证；(3) 仅针对代码生成任务评估，未涉及视觉或遥感任务；(4) 未讨论剪枝对推理速度、FLOPs、延迟等部署指标的影响。

Ideas for my research: (1) 虽然本文针对LLM，但其"任务导向剪枝"思路可借鉴：在遥感目标检测中，可针对特定检测任务（如舰船/飞机检测）剪枝，保留对检测精度关键的通道/层，剪掉对遥感场景不重要的部分；(2) 本文指出困惑度不可靠，提示在遥感检测中不应仅依赖单一指标（如mAP），需结合推理速度、参数量、FLOPs等多维度评估；(3) 剪枝+微调的组合策略可参考：先激进剪枝再轻量微调恢复精度；(4) 剪枝与量化的对比结论（剪枝仅在量化低于3 bits时胜出）对遥感模型压缩策略选择有参考价值，但需在CNN/Transformer检测器上重新验证。

Abstract: The strongest open-weight coding models are mixture-of-experts (MoE) networks: most of their size comes from large pools of "expert" subnetworks, of which only a few act on any token. That pool is why these models do not fit on the machines most developers own, yet for a user who only wants coding help, most experts encode abilities that will never be invoked. We ask how many experts can be removed, and which, by pruning two recent open-weight MoE models from different families (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B and Gemma-4-26B-A4B) under five selection strategies, judged the way a user would: by whether the model still writes correct code. Half the experts can be removed from either model with no statistically detectable loss on the primary code benchmark, and the damage lands almost entirely on abilities outside coding, the intended trade. But the winning strategy flips between the two models, so a recipe validated on one family cannot be assumed to work on another. We further show that perplexity, the metric much of the pruning literature leans on, can rate a broken model above an intact one; that a lightweight fine-tune recovers about half of what aggressive pruning loses; and that against quantizing the full model to the same memory, pruning wins only where quantization would have to drop below 3 bits per weight. Five attempts to overturn that crossover, with failure criteria fixed in advance (better calibration, guarded selection, causal expert importance, failure attribution, and an agentic evaluation letting each model repair its failures from execution feedback), all leave it standing; the last shows single-shot benchmarks overstate compression penalties broadly, as one repair turn erases the 2-bit quantization penalty entirely. Expert pruning works, but it demands per-model validation on the task the model will actually serve.

</details>


### [258] [Graph-Embedded Intuitionistic Fuzzy Broad Learning System: A Multi-view Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16728)
*Yogesh Kumar,Manju,Mudasir Ganaie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种多视图图嵌入直觉模糊宽度学习系统(MVGIFBLS)，通过融合多视图学习、图嵌入和直觉模糊理论增强BLS在噪声和多源数据下的分类鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于通用数据分类的BLS改进，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：通用数据分类；应用场景：UCI、KEEL、AwA等通用基准数据集；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于Broad Learning System (BLS)的改进，集成多视图学习、图嵌入(graph embedding)、直觉模糊理论(intuitionistic fuzzy theory)，引入局部Fisher判别分析的固有子空间和惩罚子空间，以及基于核的邻域分析。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。BLS本身结构较简单，但本文未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。无法判断星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：UCI、KEEL、AwA benchmark数据集；评价指标：Area Under the Curve (AUC)分数；实验设置：对比评估、高斯特征噪声分析、消融研究、统计分析。

Experiments: 各组件均对整体框架有正向贡献；MVGIFBLS在AUC指标上持续取得更高分数，在高斯特征噪声下保持鲁棒性能。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景，未在遥感数据集上验证；2. 不涉及目标检测任务，仅做分类；3. 无任何轻量化或部署相关讨论；4. 仅在小型通用基准数据集上评估，未见大规模或高维数据验证。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。BLS的图嵌入和模糊理论思路理论上可迁移到遥感特征增强，但本文未涉及检测任务和轻量化，与课题核心方向差距较大。

Abstract: The Broad Learning System (BLS) has been widely used for data classification and is based on a layer-by-layer feed-forward structure. However, it gives the same importance to all data points, which reduces its effectiveness on real-world datasets with noise and outliers. In addition, it does not consider the geometric structure of the data and has limitations in handling data from multiple sources. To address these challenges, we propose a Multi-View Graph-Embedded Intuitionistic Fuzzy Broad Learning System (MVGIFBLS) that integrates multi-view learning, graph embedding, and intuitionistic fuzzy theory into the BLS framework. This design enables the model to combine information from multiple sources and learn more discriminative representations. Graph embedding captures the geometric relationships among samples and improves class separation through intrinsic and penalty subspaces based on local Fisher discriminant analysis. Intuitionistic fuzzy theory enhances robustness to noise, while kernel-based neighborhood analysis captures local data structures. We evaluate the proposed framework on several UCI, KEEL, and AwA benchmark datasets using comparative evaluation, Gaussian feature noise analysis, ablation studies, and statistical analysis. The results demonstrate that each component contributes positively to the overall framework and that the proposed MVGIFBLS consistently achieves higher Area Under the Curve (AUC) scores and maintains robust performance under Gaussian feature noise.

</details>


### [259] [BG4Sea: Biogeochemical Seasonal Forecastability via Progressive Information Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16731)
*Gabriela Martinez Balbontin,Anastase Charantonis,Dominique Bereziat,Stefano Ciavatta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BG4Sea，首个全球数据驱动的海洋生物地球化学季节预报系统，采用列自编码器+潜在预报器+FiLM条件注入+交叉注意力水平耦合的模块化架构，在BIORYS4再分析数据上实现6个月多变量预报。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦海洋生物地球化学季节预报（biogeochemical forecasting），不涉及遥感图像、卫星目标检测、在轨智能或模型轻量化。虽然可能间接使用卫星遥感数据（如海表温度、叶绿素），但核心任务是时序预报而非遥感图像目标检测，且未讨论任何轻量化或部署问题。

Reading priority: 1分，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：海洋生物地球化学季节预报（multivariate seasonal forecasting）；应用场景：全球海洋碳循环与生态系统管理；传感器/数据类型：海洋再分析数据BIORYS4（NEMO/PISCES模型输出），非遥感图像；目标类别：溶解化学、生物学、碳库变量（dissolved chemistry, biology, carbon-pool variables）。

Model architecture: 模块化架构：1）列自编码器（column autoencoder）将垂直剖面压缩为低维潜空间；2）潜在预报器（latent forecaster）在潜空间进行时序传播；3）表面强迫条件模块（surface-forcing conditioner）通过FiLM（Feature-wise Linear Modulation）注入物理边界信息；4）水平耦合模块（horizontal-coupling module）通过交叉注意力（cross-attention）融合相邻列上下文。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。列自编码器压缩垂直剖面到低维潜空间可视为一种降维/压缩策略，但目的不是模型轻量化，而是处理高维垂直剖面数据。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。模型运行在1/4度、月分辨率全球网格上，计算开销可能较大，但未讨论星上或边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：全球海洋再分析BIORYS4（NEMO/PISCES）；评价指标：超越persistence和climatology基线，按变量和预报提前时间（lead time）分别评估；分辨率：1/4度，月分辨率；预报时长：6个月。

Experiments: 在BIORYS4上训练和评估，与persistence和climatology基线对比，在大多数变量和提前时间上表现更优。讨论了各模块的可预测性归因（predictability attribution）及模型结构局限性。

Limitations: 1）与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2）未涉及任何轻量化或边缘部署技术；3）依赖再分析数据而非真实遥感观测；4）模型结构局限性在摘要中提及但未具体展开；5）未讨论实时推理或资源受限场景。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴价值极低。唯一可能间接参考的点：FiLM（Feature-wise Linear Modulation）作为一种条件注入机制，在遥感目标检测中可用于融合多模态信息（如光学+SAR），但与本课题核心方向关联微弱。列自编码器的潜空间压缩思路在遥感特征压缩中有一定理论相似性，但非轻量化方法。

Abstract: Marine biogeochemical forecasting is increasingly important for managing marine ecosystems and the carbon cycle, yet global, seasonal forecast products lag far behind physical oceanography, held back by the complexity of the processes involved and by data scarcity. We introduce BG4Sea, which to our knowledge is the first global, data-driven system to produce multivariate seasonal forecasts of the marine biogeochemical state. BG4Sea is a modular architecture with a column autoencoder that compresses the vertical column into a low-dimensional latent space, a latent forecaster propagates this representation forward in time, a surface-forcing conditioner that injects physical boundary information via Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), and a horizontal-coupling module that incorporates neighboring-column context through cross-attention. The model is trained and evaluated on the global ocean reanalysis BIORYS4 (NEMO/PISCES), and produces six-month forecasts at 1/4 degree, monthly resolution for dissolved chemistry, biology, and carbon-pool variables, outperforming persistence and climatology across most variables and lead times. We position BG4Sea as an interpretable baseline for future, more expressive approaches, and discuss predictability attribution to each component, alongside the model's structural limitations.

</details>


### [260] [The Anatomy of a Truth Direction: Knowledge-Dependent Dimensionality, a Relational Law, and a Convergent Category Geometry in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16741)
*Francesco Karim Vicidomini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究大语言模型中真值表示的子空间维度、架构组件来源及其混合性质，属于LLM可解释性方向，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化均无任何关联。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题为LLM内部真值表示的可解释性分析（truth representation in LLMs），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM可解释性分析（truth direction probing）。应用场景：大语言模型内部表征理解。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 研究对象为LLM（文中涉及四个基础模型、两个模型家族），关注注意力机制（attention）、前馈网络（feed-forward network）、SwiGLU值流（SwiGLU value stream）等组件。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等内容。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用LLM隐藏状态（hidden-state minimal pairs）进行SVD分析，评价指标为AUC（held-out AUC 0.938）、余弦相似度（cosine 0.959）、Mantel检验p值（p=0.0009）等，均为LLM可解释性评估指标，与遥感目标检测无关。

Experiments: 实验分为两部分：Part I对单个模型进行无训练方向探针（training-free directional probe），通过SVD分解隐藏状态最小对；Part II在四个基础模型上验证注意力传播真值框架、前馈网络对抗当前块框架等规律。包含33个类别、每关系888对、6个采样种子的压力测试。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但该论文完全聚焦LLM内部机制，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无任何交集，无法直接借鉴。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM可解释性领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题的研究目标、技术路线、评估体系均无关联。

Abstract: Bürger et al. (2024) demonstrated that truth representations in large language models are universal across statement polarity but reside within a multidimensional subspace. We extend that framework along three questions: how the dimensionality of the subspace depends on the model's knowledge, which architectural component builds the truth direction, and what the direction is a mixture of. In Part I (one model), a training-free directional probe derived from the SVD of hidden-state minimal pairs shows that the dimensionality of truth is knowledge-dependent: the signal is concentrated on a single axis for behaviorally known facts (held-out AUC 0.938) and becomes diffuse as knowledge decreases or material heterogeneity increases; seven falsification experiments sustain the one-dimensional reading, and the same decomposition recovers the supervised polarity direction of Bürger et al. at cosine 0.959 without polarity labels. In Part II (four base models, two families, pre-registered predictions), a relational law emerges: attention propagates truth frames it did not write, the feed-forward network opposes the frame of its current block, and the genuine post-peak decay is causally attributed on all four models to the SwiGLU value stream; per-category truth axes form a semantically signed arrangement that contradicts the lexical surface and converges across families (Mantel p = 0.0009). Stress tests at three scales (33 categories, 888 pairs per relation, six sampling seeds) expose a sign instability of the per-category orientation, repair it with a declared spectral consensus gauge, and sharpen the convergence into a knowledge-gated law, strengthening from +0.42 on dense mixed-knowledge material to +0.74 on knowledge-restricted categories: the geometry of the mixture belongs to the knowledge domain rather than to the architecture.

</details>


### [261] [Robust Losses from Univariate Base Functions for Noisy-Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16768)
*Peng Hu,Jianwei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种从单变量基函数构造鲁棒多分类损失函数的通用框架，通过Target Separation和Binary Reduction两种方案系统性地设计和分析损失函数的噪声鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于带噪标签学习中的鲁棒损失函数设计，属于通用机器学习/分类问题，不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型轻量化任何一方面。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：带噪标签下的鲁棒分类损失函数设计；应用场景：通用图像分类；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。论文核心贡献在损失函数层面，而非网络结构设计。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要提及在合成和真实带噪标签基准数据集上进行实验，但未给出具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 实验表明所提损失函数在各种噪声设置下达到有竞争力或更优的性能，但摘要未给出具体数值结果或对比方法细节。

Limitations: 摘要未说明局限性。但该工作仅关注损失函数的鲁棒性，未考虑遥感场景下的特殊噪声分布（如空间标注噪声、边界模糊等），也未涉及检测任务的定位损失鲁棒性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。如果课题涉及遥感目标检测中的标注噪声问题（如遥感图像中目标边界模糊导致的标注不准确），可参考其损失函数设计思路，但需要将分类损失扩展到检测任务的分类+回归联合损失设计。

Abstract: Learning with noisy labels is a fundamental problem in training reliable deep neural networks. Robust loss functions provide a direct and effective way to mitigate the adverse effects of label noise. However, most existing robust losses are designed directly at the level of the final multiclass objective, which makes it difficult to systematically characterize and extend their robustness properties. In this paper, we propose a general framework that constructs robust multiclass losses from univariate base functions. By defining mapping operators from base functions to multiclass losses, the robustness of the induced losses can be characterized through simple properties of the base functions. We develop two complementary construction schemes, Target Separation and Binary Reduction, corresponding to inter-class independent and inter-class dependent formulations, respectively. For both schemes, we analyze their symmetry and asymmetry properties and derive corresponding sufficient conditions, which provide theoretical criteria for noise-robust loss design. The proposed framework also provides a new route to constructing symmetric losses, serving as a complement to normalization-based symmetric loss designs. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world noisy-label benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed losses achieve competitive or superior performance under various noise settings.

</details>


### [262] [On the Potential of Graph Neural Networks as Metamodels for Supply Chain Optimization: Dataset, Architectures, and Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16769)
*Tushar Lone,Neha Karanjkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索使用图神经网络（GNN）作为供应链图结构系统的替代模型，用于结构优化和参数优化，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测、舰船/飞机/车辆检测等检测任务；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等任何轻量化或部署技术。论文聚焦于供应链系统的GNN替代建模，属于运筹学/图机器学习方向，与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：供应链图结构系统的替代建模（surrogate modeling）；应用场景：供应链优化；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。摘要未说明任何遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: GNN（Graph Neural Networks）架构，具体子类型摘要未说明。用于节点级和网络级预测。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是GNN替代模型与仿真之间的accuracy-compute trade-off，但并非面向模型轻量化或部署优化。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：程序化生成的供应链图数据集（使用SupplyNetPy仿真库）；评价指标：节点级和网络级预测精度、accuracy-compute trade-off。与遥感数据集完全无关。

Experiments: 探索了适用于供应链替代建模的GNN架构，分析了精度-计算权衡。具体数值结果摘要未说明。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。局限性包括：仅针对供应链场景，未涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等核心问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于供应链优化和图机器学习领域，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题无交叉。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a powerful, differentiable class of learning models for graph-structured systems. Their ability to generalize across topologies opens the prospect of a surrogate for combined structural and parametric optimization, which classical metamodels cannot offer. Supply chains are a natural target, yet the use of GNN surrogates for supply chain problems is largely unexplored. This paper lays the foundation, presents initial steps, and discusses key research directions. As a foundation, we formulate the problem and create a large public training dataset of programmatically generated supply chain graphs with input parameters and steady-state performance metrics obtained using our SupplyNetPy simulation library. As initial steps, we explore GNN architectures that work well as surrogates for node- and network-level predictions, and analyze their accuracy-compute trade-off against simulation. Most importantly, we outline the exciting directions this opens, namely gradient-based optimization over topology, fast design-space exploration, and sensitivity analysis.

</details>


### [263] [MultiLoReFT: Decoupling Shared and Modality-Specific Subspaces in Multimodal Learning via Low-Rank Representation Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16789)
*Sana Tonekaboni,Viktoria Schuster,Caroline Uhler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MultiLoReFT，一种用于多模态学习的低秩表示微调框架，通过解耦共享与模态特有信息提升多模态表征的可解释性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦多模态表示学习与微调方法，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多模态表示学习与微调；应用场景：通用多模态感知与决策；传感器类型：未说明；目标类别：未说明。与遥感场景无关。

Model architecture: MultiLoReFT框架，基于低秩适应（LoRA）扩展到多模态场景，学习可解释的投影子空间以解耦共享与模态特有信息。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。仅提及"efficient and scalable low-rank representation fine-tuning"，但这是微调效率而非模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用模拟和真实世界基准（simulated and real-world benchmarks），但未给出具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 在模拟和真实基准上验证了多模态预测能力，并展示了共享与模态特有信息的分布情况。未提供与SOTA方法的定量对比。

Limitations: 摘要未讨论局限性；未涉及遥感场景验证；未提供轻量化/部署相关指标；未说明在边缘或星上平台的可行性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果未来研究方向涉及遥感多模态数据（如光学+SAR融合）的轻量化表示学习，其低秩解耦思路可能有参考价值，但当前论文未涉及遥感场景和轻量化部署。

Abstract: Real-world perception and decision making are inherently multimodal, integrating complementary signals across modalities. However, training multimodal models faces two main obstacles. First, collecting large-scale, well-aligned paired multimodal datasets is often impractical, making end-to-end multimodal training difficult. Second, existing multimodal representations frequently entangle information shared across modalities with modality-specific information, hindering interpretability and control. We introduce MultiLoReFT, an efficient and scalable low-rank representation fine-tuning framework for multimodal learning with pretrained unimodal models. MultiLoReFT extends low-rank adaptation to the multimodal setting and learns interpretable projection subspaces that decouple shared and modality-specific information. Across simulated and real-world benchmarks, it produces representations that support multimodal prediction while explicitly revealing how shared and modality-specific information is distributed across modalities.

</details>


### [264] [Interpretable Anomaly and Drift Detection with Gaussian Mixture Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16811)
*Behnam Asadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视GMM作为轻量级可解释异常检测与数据流分布漂移检测工具，通过BIC自动选择分量数、极值理论设定阈值、以及"未解释质量"作为漂移信号，在7个公开基准上验证了其竞争性表现与可解释性优势。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。本文聚焦于通用异常检测与分布漂移检测，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星数据、Earth observation、SAR/高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更未讨论星上部署、轻量化推理等边缘部署问题。虽然文中提到"lightweight"一词，但指的是GMM模型本身的轻量特性，而非面向遥感在轨场景的模型压缩或加速方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：异常检测（anomaly detection）与分布漂移检测（distributional drift detection）；应用场景：通用数据流（data streams），非遥感场景；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs)，通过BIC自动选择分量数，k-means初始化，使用负对数似然（negative log-likelihood）作为异常分数，极值理论（Extreme Value Theory）设定阈值。漂移检测使用"未解释质量"（unexplained mass）统计量。
