Experiments: 在多个真实室内环境中验证，仅用16个距离样本即可达到与全量LiDAR SLAM相当的定位效果。方法在静态环境下可证明收敛到真实位姿，在动态环境下若变化模式被正确学习也可保证收敛。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 仅适用于室内平面环境，不适用于遥感场景；3. 依赖对动态环境变化模式的先验学习，泛化性受限；4. 未涉及任何深度学习模型或轻量化技术。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可参考的是"稀疏采样"的思想——在遥感在轨场景中，是否可以用稀疏的传感器采样或稀疏的特征图计算来降低计算负载？但论文本身并未涉及模型层面的轻量化，且场景差异巨大。

Abstract: Localization is a key task in robot navigation, and many techniques exist for it. In many plausible scenarios, a robot might face unforeseen, dynamic obstacles, rendering any pre-determined map inaccurate for localization. In this work, we propose a robust lifelong localization framework in dynamic planar indoor environments, using the robot's odometry and sparse distance sampling. We demonstrate how distance samples can be used to provide a robust prior on the robot's location. This technique can solve the kidnapped robot problem in real time, up to symmetries. Based on insights from real-world recorded data, we also account for dynamic obstacles. We then fuse this prior, over time, with the odometry to converge to the robot's location. A central property of our method is that it provably converges to the robot's ground truth pose even in large indoor environments when the environment is static. We further show that this guarantee also holds in dynamic environments, as long as the nature of those changes has been correctly learned. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in different real-world indoor environments. In particular, we achieve a localization comparable to SLAM with merely a few (sixteen) distance samples, as opposed to the full LiDAR range. Sufficing with only sparse distance sampling is advantageous in terms of sensor cost, privacy, storage space, and transmission bandwidth.

</details>


### [321] [ConceptTree: Bringing Semantic Transparency to Black-Box Decision Making for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17861)
*Yongyan Wen,Feifan Liu,Jinyi Chen,Bo An,Peng Liu,Siyuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ConceptTree框架，将机器人长时域操作中的高层技能选择建模为基于可解释概念（concept-level predicates）的决策树推理，提升决策透明度和可干预性。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文聚焦于**机器人操作（robotic manipulation）**中的可解释决策，属于具身智能/机器人学领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、轻量化部署均无直接关联。论文未涉及任何遥感图像、卫星数据、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：长时域机器人操作中的高层技能选择（high-level skill selection）；应用场景：真实世界机器人操作任务；传感器类型：视觉观测（visual observations）；目标类别：未涉及目标检测，关注的是操作技能（skills）而非目标类别。

Model architecture: ConceptTree框架：将视觉输入映射到归一化概念空间（normalized concept space），在该空间上训练决策树（decision tree）来预测高层技能。决策过程透明、可追溯、可干预。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：真实世界机器人操作任务（具体数据集名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：与现有基于概念的方法（concept-based baselines）对比，具体指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 在多个复杂度递增的真实机器人操作任务上评估，ConceptTree持续优于现有基于概念的基线方法，尤其在复杂长时域场景下。提供定性案例研究，展示通过修改单个概念实现细粒度干预。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 未涉及任何轻量化或部署优化；3. 仅在机器人操作场景验证，泛化性未知。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果未来在遥感在轨场景中需要构建可解释的决策流程（如检测结果的可信度推理），ConceptTree中的概念空间+决策树思路可参考，但与本课题核心（轻量化目标检测）方向差异过大。

Abstract: Establishing interpretable decision-making processes in long-horizon robotic manipulation is critical for enabling reliable human oversight and intervention. However, existing approaches to robotic manipulation largely treat skill selection as opaque mappings from observations to actions, offering limited transparency into how decisions are formed. In this work, we propose ConceptTree, a framework that reframes high-level manipulation skill selection as reasoning over human-interpretable concepts, representing high-level policies as a sequence of concept-level predicates over visual observations. Rather than relying on implicit latent representations, our method learns a normalized concept space grounded in visual inputs, over which a decision tree is trained to predict high-level skills. This formulation yields a transparent decision process that is both traceable and intervenable, enabling direct inspection and modification of policy behavior. We evaluate our approach on a set of real-world robotic manipulation tasks with increasing complexity. Experimental results show that ConceptTree consistently outperforms existing concept-based baselines, particularly in complex, long-horizon scenarios. Furthermore, we provide qualitative case studies showing that our model supports fine-grained intervention by modifying individual concepts, enabling targeted correction of decision errors without retraining.

</details>


### [322] [Leveraging Two Robotic Arms for Tight Assembly Performance Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17876)
*Dror Livnat,Yuval Lavi,Michael M. Bilevich,Dan Halperin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于双机械臂协同的装配操作端到端框架，利用零件CAD模型实现装配，相比单臂系统执行时间减少50%以上。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、在轨目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关，属于机器人装配操作领域，不涉及任何遥感图像处理或深度学习模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：双机械臂协同装配操作；应用场景：工业/机器人装配；传感器类型：未涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：机械零件装配。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构，仅提及端到端框架，涉及机器人运动规划与轨迹生成。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署信息。讨论的是机器人装配执行时间，非星上推理延迟。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：未使用遥感或视觉数据集，使用CAD模型和物理机器人实验；评价指标：执行时间、轨迹质量、机器人放置搜索效率。

Experiments: 仿真与实物实验均进行；主要结果：双机械臂相比单臂平均执行时间减少50%以上，轨迹质量更高，有效放置搜索加速。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无可借鉴的局限性信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人装配规划领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或星上部署等任何相关技术。

Abstract: We provide a novel end-to-end framework for the execution of an assembly operation by two robotic arms, given the digital CAD models of the parts and their desired relative placement in their assembled state. We analyze and demonstrate the advantages of using two robotic arms simultaneously in tight assembly operations, compared to single-arm systems. Our method is implemented in both simulation and using physical robots. It provides theoretical guarantees on execution time and trajectory accuracy, supported by empirical evidence. In particular, we show that coordinated movement of two arms reduces average execution time by more than 50% compared to using a single arm only, produces higher-quality trajectories, and accelerates the search for valid robot placements. Furthermore, we establish bounds on the required dimensions of the robotic cell. Our open source software together with real-life video demonstrations are available in our project page.

</details>


### [323] [Manifold-Guided Motion Planning for Tight Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17898)
*Dror Livnat,Michael M. Bilevich,Michal Kleinbort,Dan Halperin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CMG-RRT采样规划器，用于刚体装配中的紧约束运动规划问题，通过关键流形引导采样实现高成功率。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向基本无关（1分）。该论文研究的是机器人刚体装配运动规划，属于robotics领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：刚体装配运动规划；应用场景：机器人装配、拼图解谜（Elk disentanglement puzzle）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: CMG-RRT（Critical-Manifold Guided RRT），基于采样的运动规划器，利用关键流形（critical manifold）引导采样，采用配置空间层次细分（hierarchical subdivision）策略。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：旋转装配基准测试（rotational assembly benchmarks）、Elk disentanglement puzzle；评价指标：成功率（success rate）。

Experiments: 在所有测试实例上达到100%成功率，首次全自动求解Elk disentanglement puzzle。摘要未说明对比方法和消融实验细节。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型压缩、目标检测或遥感相关技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人运动规划领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Motion planning for rigid-body assembly poses a fundamental challenge in robotics due to tight geometric constraints. In such scenarios, feasible motions often require passing through (near-)zero clearance configurations in which the parts are tightly constrained by contact. In this work, we introduce Critical-Manifold Guided RRT (CMG-RRT), a sampling-based planner designed specifically for tight assembly problems. Our key observation is that in tight assemblies, valid solution paths lie on or near a critical manifold: the subset of configuration space consisting of poses with at least one contact point between parts. CMG-RRT guides exploration by adaptively biasing sampling toward neighborhoods of the critical manifold using a hierarchical subdivision of the configuration space. We prove that CMG-RRT is probabilistically complete under standard clearance assumptions. Empirical evaluation on challenging rotational assembly benchmarks demonstrates a 100% success rate across all tested instances, including, to the best of our knowledge, the first fully automatic solution of the Elk disentanglement puzzle. Our open source software is available through our project page: https://www.cgl.cs.tau.ac.il/projects/tight-assembly-planning.

</details>


### [324] [UMCP: A Unified Multi-Task Collaborative Perception Network for Luggage Trolley Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17950)
*Zhirui Sun,Zhihao Jiang,Yao Wang,Jianwei Peng,Jiankun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个基于YOLOv12的多任务协同感知网络UMCP，用于机器人自动行李车收集场景中的检测、关键点检测和朝向估计，在保持精度的同时降低模型复杂度和计算成本。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文面向机器人自动行李车收集场景，属于室内/地面机器人视觉感知任务，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、航空图像、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及在轨部署或星上智能。虽然涉及目标检测和模型轻量化，但场景差异巨大，方法迁移性有限。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，阅读优先级最低。除非对YOLOv12的多任务扩展或朝向估计损失函数设计有特殊兴趣，否则不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多任务协同感知（目标检测+关键点检测+朝向估计）；应用场景：机器人自动行李车收集（室内/地面机器人）；传感器类型：摘要未说明（推测为RGB相机）；目标类别：行李车（luggage trolley）。

Model architecture: 基于YOLOv12架构，设计统一的多任务协同感知网络UMCP。关键模块包括：关键点特征与朝向特征融合模块、朝向特征增强模块（OFEM, Orientation Feature Enhancement Module）。采用圆形概率分布建模和KL散度损失来提升朝向估计精度。

Lightweight method: 轻量化主要体现在模型结构设计层面：通过统一的多任务网络替代级联多模型推理，减少推理延迟和部署成本。摘要未提及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等具体轻量化技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等具体部署指标。仅提到"substantially reducing model complexity and computational cost"，但无量化数据。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 实验结果表明该方法在保持竞争性精度的同时显著降低了模型复杂度和计算成本。摘要未提供与基线方法的详细对比数据、消融实验结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨部署完全无关；2. 缺乏具体的轻量化量化指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟等）；3. 未说明数据集和评价指标，实验可复现性差；4. 多任务设计针对行李车场景，泛化到遥感目标检测（如舰船、飞机、车辆）需大量改造。

Ideas for my research: 1. 多任务统一网络的设计思路（检测+关键点+朝向）可借鉴到遥感旋转框检测任务中，例如同时检测舰船并预测其朝向；2. YOLOv12作为基线的选择思路可参考，但需替换为遥感专用骨干；3. 圆形概率分布+KL散度损失用于朝向估计的思路，对遥感旋转框检测中的角度回归可能有启发；4. 整体借鉴价值较低，因为场景差异大且缺乏轻量化量化指标。

Abstract: In robotic autonomous luggage trolley collection, robots must continuously localize scattered luggage trolleys in cluttered and dynamic environments. This requires the vision system to achieve both high accuracy and real-time performance. However, existing visual perception approaches for luggage trolleys often rely on cascaded multi-model inference, leading to increased inference latency and high deployment costs. To address these limitations, this article presents a unified multi-task collaborative perception network (UMCP) that simultaneously performs luggage trolley detection, keypoint detection and orientation estimation. Based on the YOLOv12 architecture, keypoint features are fused with orientation features and then fed into an orientation feature enhancement module (OFEM), thereby improving orientation estimation accuracy. In addition, circular probability distribution modeling with a Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence loss is adopted to enhance orientation estimation accuracy further. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves competitive overall accuracy while substantially reducing model complexity and computational cost compared with existing methods. A website about this work is available at https://sites.google.com/view/robot-umcp.

</details>


### [325] [Does Robust VIO Need More Learning? Geometry-Verified Visual Measurements under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17956)
*Yangyang Ning,Shu Liang,Quanbo Ge,Tianchen Deng,Yuhua Qi,Shenghai Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种最小化学习的立体视觉-惯性里程计框架，仅在视觉测量生成阶段使用学习（SEA-RAFT进行密集立体匹配和不确定性预测），而时序跟踪、几何验证和状态估计保持显式，以提升分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是视觉-惯性里程计（VIO）的鲁棒性问题，属于机器人/自动驾驶领域的SLAM/定位任务，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化模型部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关，属于VIO/SLAM领域，不建议投入阅读时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：立体视觉-惯性里程计（Stereo VIO）；场景：室内外移动机器人/无人机导航；传感器：立体相机+IMU；目标类别：不涉及目标检测，无具体目标类别。

Model architecture: SEA-RAFT用于密集立体匹配和不确定性预测；时序跟踪、几何验证和状态估计为显式非学习模块；滑动窗口立体-惯性估计器，不确定性加权重投影因子。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等任何模型压缩或加速方法。相反，其核心思路是"最小化学习"以减少对学习模型的依赖，但并非从轻量化角度出发。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文关注的是分布偏移下的鲁棒性，而非计算效率或星上部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：EuRoC、VIODE、4Seasons；评价指标：准确性和稳定性（具体指标摘要未详细列出，如ATE/RPE等常见VIO指标未明确说明）。

Experiments: 在EuRoC、VIODE、4Seasons上验证了运动模糊、动态场景、光照变化、室内外分布偏移下的准确性和稳定性。消融实验表明：仅靠学习的光流不够，增益来自学习匹配+几何验证+不确定性加权的组合。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星场景，任务差异巨大；2. 不涉及目标检测；3. 不涉及模型轻量化或部署优化；4. 未报告计算开销指标。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可参考的点：1）不确定性预测机制可用于遥感目标检测中的预测置信度校准；2）"最小化学习+显式几何约束"的思路在星上资源受限场景下有一定启发，但论文本身未从轻量化角度设计。总体而言，不建议作为核心参考论文。

Abstract: Learning is increasingly introduced into visual-inertial odometry (VIO), ranging from learned feature front-ends to learning-dominant motion and geometry estimation. However, learning more of the pipeline does not necessarily improve robustness when deployment conditions differ from the training distribution. This work asks whether robust VIO under distribution shift truly requires deeper learned estimation, or whether learning can be confined to visual measurement generation. We propose a minimal-learning stereo VIO framework in which SEA-RAFT is used only to propose dense stereo correspondences and predict their uncertainty, while temporal tracking, geometric verification, and state estimation remain explicit. Dense flow is sampled at sparse feature locations, filtered using predicted uncertainty and stereo epipolar consistency, and incorporated into a sliding-window stereo-inertial estimator through uncertainty-weighted reprojection factors. The same uncertainty is further propagated through stereo triangulation for downstream anisotropic 3D Gaussian mapping. Experiments on EuRoC, VIODE, and 4Seasons demonstrate accurate and stable estimation under motion blur, dynamic scenes, illumination changes, and large indoor-to-outdoor distribution shifts. Ablations show that learned flow alone is insufficient: the gains arise from combining learned correspondence proposals with geometric verification and uncertainty-aware weighting. These results suggest that, for OOD-robust VIO, carefully integrated learned visual measurements can be more effective than learning a larger fraction of the estimation pipeline. Code and configs for the benchmark will be open-source upon acceptance. A supplementary video is available at https://drive.google.com/file/d/1EVRhOkhanmNXHbQS1Vr80FoEIAYOYOV2/view

</details>


### [326] [MEVION: Low-Cost Open-Source Data Collection System for Powerful and High-Speed Dual-Arm Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17970)
*Kento Kawaharazuka,Yoshiki Obinata,Hirokazu Ishida,Jihoon Oh,Temma Suzuki,Shintaro Inoue,Keita Yoneda,Ayumu Iwata,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MEVION，一种低成本、开源的双臂机器人数据采集系统，旨在解决ALOHA系统在力和速度方面的不足，支持重物操作和快速操作任务的模仿学习。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文聚焦于机器人学中的双臂机器人数据采集系统设计，属于机器人操作与模仿学习领域，与本研究方向无任何交集。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：双臂机器人数据采集与模仿学习；应用场景：机器人操作（重物搬运、快速操作）；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：机器人夹爪操作对象。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的检测或视觉模型架构。系统采用4个6自由度（6-DoF）机械臂，配备平行夹爪，肘关节采用闭链机构（类似四足机器人设计）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。本文讨论的是机器人硬件系统，与星上/边缘部署无关。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。系统成本约14,000美元，单臂重量7.0kg，最大扭矩60Nm。

Experiments: 摘要未说明定量实验结果。仅提及系统支持之前无法实现的对象操作任务数据采集，并支持基于模仿学习的运动生成。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供有意义的局限性分析。从机器人学角度看，摘要未说明数据采集质量、模仿学习成功率等关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。本文属于机器人硬件系统设计，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何关联。

Abstract: The global competition for developing robotic foundation models is intensifying. Among the data collection systems used for dual-arm robots, ALOHA is representative of being low-cost and open-source, and is widely adopted by researchers as a de facto standard. However, due to its limited ability to generate high forces and speeds, it is difficult to handle heavy objects or perform fast manipulations. To address this, we developed MEVION, a low-cost and open-source dual-arm robot data collection system capable of generating greater force and speed. All parts of this robot can be sourced through e-commerce, and by extensively utilizing sheet metal welding, its large body structure is constructed with a small number of components at low cost, while also simplifying assembly. MEVION is equipped with four 6-DoF arms with parallel grippers. Each arm weighs 7.0 kg and has a maximum torque of 60 Nm, and the entire system can be constructed for about USD 14,000. The elbow joint adopts a closed-link mechanism similar to those used in quadruped robots, which reduces the distal mass and enables higher force and speed output at the end-effector. We demonstrate that MEVION enables data collection for object manipulation tasks not previously possible and supports imitation learning-based motion generation. All hardware and software of this work are included in the Supplementary Materials or https://github.com/haraduka/mevion.

</details>


### [327] [RynnBrain 1.1: Towards More Capable and Generalizable Embodied Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17977)
*Kehan Li,Bohan Hou,Minghao Zhu,Tianyi Zhang,Zesen Cheng,Zhikai Wang,Sicong Leng,Xin Li,Xiao Lin,Biying Yao,Minghua Zeng,Jiangpin Liu,Ronghao Dang,Jiayan Guo,Siteng Huang,Haoyu Zhao,Heng Ping,Yaxi Zhao,Kexiang Wang,Tong Lu,Shengke Xue,Jiahao Tang,Yulei Wang,Zejing Wang,Jianwei Gao,Shijian Lu,Chengju Liu,Jianfei Yang,Mingxiu Chen,Deli Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RynnBrain 1.1 是一个面向机器人操作（embodied manipulation）的多尺度基础模型家族（2B/9B/122B-A10B），支持具身感知、空间推理、定位和规划，并在真实机器人平台上部署验证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于具身智能（embodied AI）和机器人操作（robot manipulation），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：具身感知、空间推理、3D grounding、接触点预测、机器人操作规划。应用场景：机器人操作（Unitree G1, Astribot-S1, Tianji-Wuji等机器人平台）。传感器类型：摘要未说明。目标类别：摘要未说明。

Model architecture: RynnBrain 1.1 家族包含三个规模：2B、9B、122B-A10B（MoE架构）。采用统一的时空与物理基础框架训练。2B和9B模型引入原生3D grounding。VLA版本采用统一跨具身动作空间和具身特定掩码。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然模型有2B的小规模版本，但论文未讨论模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅在真实机器人上部署验证，但未给出量化指标。

Datasets and metrics: 评测基准：VSI-Bench、MMSI、RefSpatial-Bench。真实机器人实验评估过程分数（process scores）和成功率（success rates）。

Experiments: 122B-A10B模型在VSI-Bench、MMSI、RefSpatial-Bench上超越所有评估的闭源和开源模型。真实机器人实验中，RynnBrain初始化的策略优于Qwen-based和代表性通用VLA。联合多任务和多具身训练相比单任务训练提升了过程分数和成功率。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。摘要未讨论模型在资源受限设备上的部署优化。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。仅有的潜在启发：（1）多尺度模型家族（2B/9B/122B）的设计思路可类比遥感检测中不同精度-速度权衡的模型变体；（2）MoE（122B-A10B）的稀疏激活思想在理论上可探索用于遥感模型的推理加速，但论文未给出具体效率数据。

Abstract: We present RynnBrain 1.1, a family of embodied foundation models spanning 2B, 9B, and 122B-A10B scales. Trained with a unified spatio-temporal and physically grounded framework, RynnBrain 1.1 supports embodied perception, spatial reasoning, localization, and planning. Compared with RynnBrain 1.0, it further introduces contact-point prediction across the model family and native 3D grounding for the 2B and 9B models, yielding representations and outputs that are more directly aligned with robot manipulation. We also develop RynnBrain-VLA with a unified cross-embodiment action space and embodiment-specific masking, and deploy it on Unitree G1, Astribot-S1, and Tianji-Wuji. RynnBrain 1.1 achieves strong results on embodied cognition, localization, and 3D grounding, with the 122B-A10B model outperforming all evaluated proprietary and open-source models on VSI-Bench, MMSI, and RefSpatial-Bench. Real-robot experiments show that RynnBrain-initialized policies outperform Qwen-based and representative generalist VLAs, while joint multi-task and multi-embodiment training improves process scores and success rates over per-task training.

</details>


### [328] [Distilling Global Traversability Priors for Image-based Affordance Prediction in Off-road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17984)
*Matthew Sivaprakasam,Samuel Triest,Micah Nye,Deegan Atha,Shehryar Khattak,David Fan,Wenshan Wang,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用卫星图像提取远距离可穿越性前沿，辅助无人车在非结构化地形中的长距离自主导航。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文虽然使用了卫星图像（satellite imagery），但其核心任务是地面无人车的自主导航（autonomous navigation），而非遥感图像中的目标检测。不涉及遥感在轨目标检测，也不涉及模型轻量化或星上部署。卫星图像仅作为辅助监督信号使用，并非分析对象。与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：地面无人车长距离自主导航（long-range off-road navigation）；场景：非结构化地形（unstructured terrain）；传感器：FPV图像、LiDAR或相机、卫星图像；目标：提取可穿越性前沿（traversability-aware frontiers）。不涉及遥感目标检测。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：图像/位姿对数据集（image/pose pairs），利用卫星图像计算可行导航路径作为监督；评价指标：离线基准测试（offline benchmarks）中性能提升>10%，以及真实实验中人为干预次数（number of human interventions）。

Experiments: 离线基准测试中比现有方法提升>10%；真实实验中减少了人为干预次数。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。未涉及任何目标检测任务，未涉及模型压缩或边缘部署。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值低。唯一可参考的点是：卫星图像被用作外部先验知识来辅助地面任务，但这与在轨遥感目标检测的轻量化方向无直接关联。

Abstract: Standard methods for autonomous navigation in unstructured terrain are prone to myopic behaviors in long-horizon scenarios. The use of metric maps built from LiDAR or cameras provides necessary local geometry and semantic information but is strictly limited by depth sensing range. By discarding data beyond the mapping horizon robots suffer from suboptimal, short-sighted decisions. To recover this lost information, we focus on extracting long-range traversability-aware frontiers directly from first-person-view (FPV) images. By leveraging satellite imagery, we compute the set of feasible navigation paths for a dataset of image/pose pairs and use them to supervise our network, reducing the need for extensive human demonstration data. We demonstrate that this approach improves performance in long-range off-road navigation over existing methods by more than 10% in various offline benchmarks and reduces the number of human interventions incurred in a set of real-world experiments. More details can be found at https://theairlab.org/ss_frontiers_iros .

</details>


### [329] [Closing the Loop in Humanoid VLA: Persistent 3D Object Tokens for Verifiable Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18016)
*Peng Ren,Haoyang Ge,Jiang Zhao,Cong Huang,Yukun Shi,Pei Chi,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Persistent Object Tokenization (POT)，通过维护角色索引的3D物体记录来改进人形机器人长时程操作任务中的物体状态一致性问题，在Unitree G1上取得显著提升。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究人形机器人视觉-语言-动作策略，属于机器人操作领域，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。不涉及任何遥感传感器、卫星图像、在轨部署或轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：人形机器人长时程操作（loco-manipulation）；应用场景：机器人抓取、操作、服务任务；传感器：RGB-D相机；目标类别：任务物体（task objects）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: POT-VLA：基于Vision-Language-Action策略，包含角色索引的3D物体记录（role-indexed 3D object records）、物体token化（object tokens）、全身动作专家（whole-body action expert）、几何谓词检查（geometric predicate checks）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。运行平台为Unitree G1人形机器人，但未给出计算资源或实时性指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：8个真实世界任务族（eight real-world task families）以及外部Being-0对齐的服务任务；评价指标：成功率（successes），如39/80提升至71/80，以及44/50 vs 37/50。

Experiments: 在Unitree G1上，POT-VLA将匹配的GR00T-N1.7基线从39/80提升至71/80；在Being-0对齐任务上达到44/50 vs 37/50。最大提升出现在需要维持3D关系的任务上。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化信息。摘要未讨论计算效率、模型大小、推理速度等部署关键指标。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于机器人操作领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型轻量化技术。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies are a promising foundation for general robot control, but long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation requires the robot to treat task objects as persistent physical entities across movement, contact, occlusion, and recovery. We study this problem as object-state divergence: the object state used to condition a whole-body action can differ from the state used to decide whether the action achieved the intended physical relation. We propose \emph{Persistent Object Tokenization} (POT), which maintains role-indexed 3D object records from RGB-D observations and converts them into object tokens for a whole-body action expert. Instantiated as \emph{POT-VLA}, the same object records condition action generation and support geometric predicate checks, yielding a closed-loop execution system in which object state is both actionable and verifiable. On a Unitree G1, POT-VLA improves a matched direct GR00T-N1.7 baseline from 39/80 to 71/80 successes over eight real-world task families. In an external Being-0-aligned reference, POT-VLA achieves 44/50 successes on aligned service tasks, compared with the 37/50 success reported by the Being-0 paper. The largest gains occur on tasks requiring maintained 3D relations, suggesting that persistent object-centered state is a useful abstraction for verifiable humanoid VLA execution.

</details>


### [330] [RoboHarness: Memory-Driven Orchestration of Heterogeneous Robot Policies for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18060)
*Jinbang Huang,Yuanzhao Hu,Zhiyuan Li,Ran Qi,Yixin Xiao,Zhanguang Zhang,Mark Coates,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboHarness框架，用于异构机器人策略（VLA、RL、TAMP）的统一编排与零样本长时程规划，解决策略能力边界不确定性和分布不匹配问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人长时程任务中的异构策略编排（orchestration），涉及VLA、RL、TAMP等机器人控制策略，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人长时程任务规划与策略编排；应用场景：机器人操作（manipulation）、导航等；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：无。

Model architecture: RoboHarness统一框架，包含多模态执行记忆（multi-modal execution memory）、在线证据（online evidence）用于能力边界刻画、Memory Bridge用于策略间稳定切换。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 三个公开benchmark、500个定制化任务、135个真实机器人实验。具体数据集名称和评价指标摘要未说明。

Experiments: 实验验证了capability-aware routing和stable policy orchestration的有效性，在零样本长时程规划和分布外鲁棒性上有显著提升。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与本人课题无直接关联，不提供可借鉴点。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic tasks require diverse capabilities that no single policy can reliably provide. Heterogeneous policies offer complementary strengths, but orchestrating them requires reasoning over uncertain capability boundaries and cross-policy distribution mismatch, which are largely overlooked by existing planning methods built on homogeneous, predefined skills with fixed applicability. We propose RoboHarness, a unified framework that encapsulates independently developed robot control systems as reusable agentic skills. Although instantiated in this work with VLAs, RL policies, and task-and-motion planning (TAMP) systems, RoboHarness is designed as a general framework compatible with a broader range of robot policies, such as navigation policies, model predictive controllers, and world-action models. RoboHarness uses multi-modal execution memory and online evidence to characterize policy capability boundaries for capability-aware decomposition and routing. To stabilize policy handoffs, its Memory Bridge retrieves execution trajectories associated with the next policy, estimates its in-distribution state region, and guides the robot toward that region without joint policy retraining. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks, 500 customized tasks, and 135 real-robot experiments demonstrate effective capability-aware routing and stable policy orchestration, yielding substantial improvements in zero-shot long-horizon planning and out-of-distribution robustness.

</details>


### [331] [UniETP: Unifying Environments for Generalizable Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18062)
*Peiran Xu,Jiaqi Zheng,Ziyou Wang,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个统一接口UniETP，整合四个具身任务规划模拟器（AI2-THOR, VirtualHome, Habitat, BEHAVIOR），标准化观测与动作空间并构建新评测基准。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦具身任务规划（Embodied Task Planning），属于机器人/智能体在交互环境中执行原子动作序列的问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Experiments: 在提出的benchmark上对近期具身规划模型进行了实验，分析了性能瓶颈。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的遥感、检测或轻量化信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于具身AI/机器人规划领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题的研究范畴无交集。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the problem of Embodied Task Planning, where an agent is required to execute a sequence of atomic actions within an interactive environment to complete a user-specified task. Though a variety of simulators and datasets have previously been built for this task, these efforts are largely isolated, with each using its own observation format, action type, and task domain. This fragmentation complicates comprehensive model evaluation and hinders the scalability of training data. As an effort towards generalizable embodied planning, we propose UniETP, a unified interface integrating four commonly-used simulators (AI2-THOR, VirtualHome, Habitat, BEHAVIOR). UniETP is characterized by both standardization and diversity. On one hand, it formalizes all the simulators into a consistent observation and action space, and builds an evaluation system to support complicated task goal. On the other hand, it enhances task diversity and complexity across dimensions like task logic, instance grounding, and instruction understanding, constructing a new dataset with varied levels of difficulty in an automatic manner. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmark are conducted to evaluate the embodied planning capabilities of recent models and analyze the performance bottlenecks. Codes and data will be available at https://github.com/woyut/UniETP .

</details>


### [332] [Technical Design Review of Duke Robotics Club's Oogway & Crush: AUVs for RoboSub 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18075)
*Patrick Zheng,Saagar Arya,Hung Le,Mathew Chu,Nathanael Ren,Niko Weaver,Isabella Chen,Jill Wang,Raine Cheng,Siddharth Kini,Avrick Altmann,Srinath Iyer,Ivan Chen,Ian Suh,Parker Jones,Pierson Jones,Sebastian deSouza,Suhaani Sriram,Suvas Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文介绍杜克机器人俱乐部为RoboSub 2026设计的两款自主水下航行器（AUV），重点在机械、电气和软件子系统上的可靠性改进，涉及声纳目标检测和视觉任务规划。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是水下自主航行器（AUV）参加RoboSub竞赛，不涉及遥感、卫星、航空图像、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素，也不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、星上部署等轻量化技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：自主水下航行器竞赛（RoboSub 2026）；应用场景：水下环境；传感器类型：声纳、视觉相机；目标类别：竞赛任务中的水下目标（pinger等）。与遥感卫星场景无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体的模型架构。仅提及软件方面改进了state estimation、sonar-based object detection、vision-driven task planning和IVC，但未给出网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。虽然涉及嵌入式系统（AUV上的计算硬件），但未给出具体指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的数据集和评价指标。竞赛场景下的评估方式未提及。

Experiments: 摘要未提供定量实验结果、对比方法或消融实验。仅描述了系统层面的改进方向。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关。该论文面向水下机器人竞赛，传感器模态（声纳+水下视觉）、任务场景（水下自主导航与操作）、部署平台（AUV）均与遥感卫星场景差异巨大。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能相关的点是"sonar-based object detection"中的声纳目标检测方法，但声纳与遥感图像（光学/SAR）的物理机理和数据处理方式完全不同，且论文未给出任何方法细节。不建议作为参考。

Abstract: The Duke Robotics Club presents Oogway and Crush, our AUVs for RoboSub 2026. This year's strategy expands on our previously narrowed scope, targeting all four of RoboSub's design goals for the first time: movement, vision, manipulation, and acoustic tracking. This expansion is based on sustained reliability investment across all three subsystems. Mechanically, Crush gained two additional thrusters and a CFD-optimized case, providing pitch stability. Electrically, we addressed accumulated failure points by repairing unreliable connections and upgraded thruster control hardware. We also redesigned our acoustics system, adding a new custom PCB with higher-order filters, significantly improving pinger detection reliability. On the software side, improvements to state estimation, sonar-based object detection, vision-driven task planning, and IVC enable more capable and coordinated autonomous runs. Paired with investments in our testing infrastructure to maximize our limited pool time, we can now attempt a broader set of tasks while maintaining the reliability our competition strategy demands.

</details>


### [333] [Importance Sampling and PCA for Finding Failures in Commercial Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18106)
*Hailey Warner,Duncan Eddy,Shreya Parjan,Caroline Cahilly,Harrison Delecki,Matthias Kleinstauber,Chaitanya Shinde,Jerry Lopez,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究自动驾驶卡车系统中罕见故障的发现方法，使用强化学习（AST）和扩散模型（DiFS）生成碰撞场景，并通过PCA进行故障分类与诊断。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于自动驾驶（autonomous trucking）系统的故障发现与安全性验证，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测模型或轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：自动驾驶系统罕见故障发现（rare failure discovery）；应用场景：商用自动驾驶卡车（commercial autonomous trucking stack）；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：摘要未说明。

Model architecture: AST（Adaptive Stress Testing）：基于强化学习的故障搜索方法；DiFS（Diffusion-based Failure Sampling）：基于去噪扩散模型的故障采样方法；PCA（Principal Component Analysis）：主成分分析用于故障分类。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文未讨论任何模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等内容。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。本文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。实验基于模拟环境（simulations）中的自动驾驶卡车场景，对比方法为传统Monte Carlo模拟。

Experiments: AST和DiFS两种算法在merge和cut-in maneuvers场景中成功发现了传统Monte Carlo模拟无法发现的碰撞；通过PCA聚类和逆变换可复现故障轨迹。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无直接借鉴意义。本文方法针对自动驾驶安全验证，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测模型或边缘部署。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。如果强行关联，PCA用于故障模式分类的思路或许可迁移到遥感模型在轨推理的异常检测场景，但关联度极低。

Abstract: Methods for discovering rare failures in autonomous systems have so far been demonstrated almost exclusively in simulations with simple, academic driving stacks, leaving open whether they generalize to the more robust planners used in commercial systems. We address this gap by applying two rare-event discovery algorithms to a commercial autonomous trucking stack. Adaptive stress testing (AST) uses reinforcement learning to search for the most likely noise trajectories leading to a simulated collision, while diffusion-based failure sampling (DiFS) trains a denoising diffusion model to sample a diverse set of failures. We show that both algorithms find simulated collisions during merge and cut-in maneuvers where traditional Monte Carlo simulation does not. To make these failures actionable, we introduce a statistical analysis based on principal component analysis (PCA) that classifies failures into common modes and identifies the timesteps that most influence the outcome. We cluster the principal components and invert the PCA transform to recover generalized noise trajectories, and show that these trajectories reproduce failures in identical and similar scenarios. This provides a path from failure discovery to systematic diagnosis of perception-level flaws.

</details>


### [334] [Isaac Sim-to-Real: Reinforcement Learning based Locomotion for Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18135)
*Jordan Dowdy,Jean Chagas Vaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于Isaac Sim/Isaac Lab的强化学习四足机器人全身控制框架，实现零样本sim-to-real迁移，在Unitree Go1上验证。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦四足机器人全身控制与sim-to-real迁移，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：四足机器人全身控制与运动策略；应用场景：机器人locomotion；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：无目标检测任务。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构，仅提及基于RL的locomotion框架，使用Isaac Sim和Isaac Lab进行训练。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。仅在Unitree Go1四足机器人硬件上验证，关注sim-to-real迁移能力，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：无标准数据集；评价指标：velocity tracking performance（速度跟踪性能）、抗扰动恢复能力、线速度2.0 m/s、角速度1.8 rad/s。

Experiments: 在Unitree Go1实物机器人上验证，与四足机器人内置控制器对比，结果显示速度跟踪性能相当，但抗大扰动恢复能力更强。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人控制领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Learning-based approaches to locomotion have risen in popularity in recent years, showing the capability for complex legged locomotion and whole-body control. Reinforcement learning (RL), the primary learning-based approach for locomotion, often utilizes a high-performance simulation tool, providing a controlled and efficient training and development environment. However, policies that perform well in simulation frequently encounter unexpected challenges when deployed on a physical system, known as the sim-to-real gap. This work presents a robust RL locomotion framework capable of whole-body control. The proposed RL framework utilizes Nvidia's new set of simulation tools, Isaac Sim, and its companion RL framework, Isaac Lab, for training, achieving a zero-shot sim-to-real policy. The performance of our policy is validated on physical hardware using the Unitree Go1, with experimental results showing similar velocity tracking performance to the quadruped's integrated controller, with a greater ability to recover from large disturbances, and achieve linear velocities of 2.0 m/s and angular velocities of 1.8 rad/s.

</details>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [335] [When Do Multimodal and Graph-Augmented RAG Help? A Controlled Evaluation for Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16604)
*Sokipriala Jonah*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种可解释的多模态图RAG架构，将文本、知识图谱三元组和图像/表格检索融合用于文档问答，但在PubLayNet数据集上的实验表明知识图谱增强无可靠提升，纯文本系统在纯图像问题上准确率为零，多模态系统受限于检索召回率和生成器对密集科学图像的理解能力。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于文档级检索增强生成(RAG)和多模态问答，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等检测任务，更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、星上部署等模型压缩与边缘部署技术。虽然论文提到了"deployment cost"和"tokenisation can dominate deployment cost"，但这是针对大语言模型推理的token开销，与遥感在轨目标检测的轻量化部署完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。除非对多模态RAG或文档问答有额外兴趣，否则可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：文档级检索增强生成(RAG)与多模态问答。应用场景：文档理解与问答，非遥感场景。传感器类型：不涉及。目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 多模态Graph-RAG架构，包含三个独立证据源：(1) 文本基线；(2) LLM提取的主语-关系-宾语三元组构成的知识图谱；(3) CLIP-based的图表检索。三个证据源在生成时融合。使用了两种闭源和两种开源多模态生成器。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提到"image tokenisation can dominate deployment cost"和"elevenfold variation in input tokens across generators"，但这是针对LLM推理的token开销分析，与遥感在轨部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：PubLayNet（1000页文档数据），包含单段落、多跳和图表三类问题。评价指标：准确率(accuracy)、图像检索Recall@3=0.371。

Experiments: 四路消融实验，对比两个闭源和两个开源多模态生成器。主要发现：(1) 知识图谱增强在本语料库上无可靠准确率提升；(2) 纯文本系统在纯图像问题上准确率为零；(3) 多模态系统在纯图像问题上准确率仅0.057-0.114；(4) 图像检索Recall@3=0.371是瓶颈；(5) 基于标题构建的问题会高估纯文本的视觉问答能力；(6) 同一图像在不同生成器上的输入token数相差11倍。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；(2) 实验仅在文档理解数据集PubLayNet上进行，不涉及遥感图像；(3) 不涉及任何目标检测方法；(4) 不涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术；(5) 知识图谱增强效果在该场景下无提升，结论不具有遥感领域的可迁移性。

Ideas for my research: (1) 弱相关借鉴点：论文分析了"image tokenisation can dominate deployment cost"，这一思路可类比到遥感在轨场景——遥感大图的token化方式（如分块策略、patch大小）对在轨推理的计算开销和延迟有显著影响，值得在轻量化部署中关注；(2) 论文的多模态融合思路（文本+图像+知识图谱）在遥感场景中可类比为"遥感图像+地理元数据+遥感知识图谱"的融合，但与本课题的"目标检测+轻量化"核心方向关联较弱；(3) 整体而言，该论文对本课题的可借鉴点非常有限。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems commonly operate on text extracted from documents, potentially losing information contained in figures, tables, layout, and relationships distributed across passages. We present an explainable multimodal graph-RAG architecture that augments a text-only baseline with LLM-extracted subject--relation--object triples and CLIP-based retrieval of figures and tables. The three evidence sources are retrieved independently and fused only at generation time, allowing the effects of graph evidence, visual evidence, and generator choice to be evaluated separately. We conduct a controlled four-way ablation on 1,000 PubLayNet pages across single-passage, multi-hop, and figure questions using two closed-weight and two open-weight multimodal generators. We also compare matched caption-answerable and pixel-only figure question sets to distinguish caption recovery from genuine visual question answering. Knowledge-graph augmentation provides no reliable accuracy improvement across generators or question types in this corpus. On pixel-only questions, text-only systems achieve zero accuracy, while multimodal systems reach 0.057--0.114 and are constrained by both image retrieval (Recall@3 = 0.371) and the generators' ability to interpret dense scientific figures and tables. Caption-derived questions substantially overestimate text-only visual question-answering capability. Processing the same image also produces an elevenfold variation in input tokens across generators, showing that image tokenisation can dominate deployment cost. Source code, experiment configurations, and evaluation resources are publicly available at https://github.com/sokistar24/multimodal-graph-rag. These findings show that the value of multimodal and graph augmentation depends on retrieval design, corpus structure, benchmark construction, and generator efficiency.

</details>


### [336] [A Unified Multisensor Machine-Learning Framework for Live Fuel Moisture Content Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16714)
*Valerio Pampanoni,Emilio Chuvieco,Alvise Ferrari,Giovanni Laneve,Simone Saquella*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一机器学习框架，利用卫星植被指数、气象、地形和季节预测因子来估算活燃料含水量（LFMC），用于火灾管理，不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。虽然使用了卫星遥感数据（MODIS、VIIRS、Landsat、Sentinel-2/3），但任务是回归（估算燃料含水量），而非目标检测；完全不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、星上部署等任何模型压缩或边缘部署技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：回归（估算live fuel moisture content）；应用场景：火灾管理、植被可燃性评估；传感器类型：Terra/Aqua MODIS、VIIRS、Landsat 8/9、Sentinel-2、Sentinel-3；目标类别：无（非目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: Random Forest和XGBoost回归器，分别训练Grass、Shrub、Tree三个模型。摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未使用剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、星上/边缘部署等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：GlobeLFMC 2.0测量数据，匹配MODIS、VIIRS、Landsat 8/9、Sentinel-2、Sentinel-3地表反射率产品；评价指标：R²（决定系数），最佳模型在Grass、Shrub、Tree上分别达到0.715、0.693、0.700。

Experiments: 主要实验：单产品实验表明天气、地形和一年中的日循环提供了植被指数之外的主要预测增益；采用留出观测日期的验证设计；对比了Random Forest和XGBoost两种回归器。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，因此主要局限在于：1）非目标检测任务；2）非深度学习模型（传统机器学习）；3）无任何轻量化或部署考虑；4）无实时推理需求。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能参考的是多传感器数据融合与光谱响应函数校准的思路，但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心方向（检测+轻量+星上部署）无直接关联。

Abstract: Live fuel moisture content controls vegetation flammability and is a high-importance variable in fire management. Nevertheless, it remains difficult to estimate and map over large areas due to the concentration of field observations in specific regions. We develop a unified machine-learning framework that estimates live fuel moisture content from satellite vegetation indices, meteorological variables, topography and seasonal predictors. GlobeLFMC 2.0 measurements are matched to Terra and Aqua MODIS, VIIRS, Landsat 8/9, Sentinel-2 and Sentinel-3 surface-reflectance products, combining the long MODIS record with finer-resolution recent observations. To account for differences among sensors, optical predictors are restricted to a common red, near-infrared and shortwave-infrared feature space; site--product combinations and field time series are screened for remote-sensing suitability; and spectral response function diagnostics are combined with target-independent empirical reflectance calibration toward a Sentinel-2 reference domain. Preliminary single-product experiments show that weather, topography and cyclic day-of-year provide most of the predictive gain beyond vegetation indices, whereas optional product-specific predictors do not justify their additional dependencies. Separate Grass, Shrub and Tree models are trained with Random Forest and XGBoost regressors. Under the primary validation design, which withholds observation dates from sites represented in training, the best models achieve pooled R2 values of 0.715, 0.693 and 0.700 for Grass, Shrub and Tree, respectively. The framework can incorporate additional optical sensors when compatible reflectance bands, documented spectral responses and sufficient overlap observations are available for calibration and validation.

</details>


### [337] [Luminosity-Adaptive Contrast Enhancement Using CLAHE for Retinal Fundus Images with Quantitative Validation and Comparative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17691)
*K. Mithra,Prem Kumar Santhanam*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合HSV色彩空间亮度校正与CLAHE的视网膜眼底图像增强两阶段流水线，在DRIVE数据集上相比HE和AHE取得更优的PSNR/SSIM/CNR指标。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于医学视网膜眼底图像增强，属于医学影像处理领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像增强（luminosity correction + CLAHE）；应用场景：眼科临床筛查（diabetic retinopathy, glaucoma, retinal vein occlusion）；传感器类型：眼底相机（Canon CR5）；目标类别：无目标检测任务，仅涉及血管病理区域二值掩膜提取。

Model architecture: 两阶段流水线：Stage 1 - HSV色彩空间分解后对V通道做CLAHE；Stage 2 - 二值掩膜提取高反射区域。无深度学习模型，为传统图像处理方法。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未使用剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。处理时间0.14秒/图像（CPU上传统方法），但未报告参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DRIVE（40张眼底图像，584×565像素，Canon CR5相机，眼科医生标注）；评价指标：PSNR（29.3 dB）、SSIM（0.91）、CNR（3.12）；对比方法：HE（PSNR=21.4, SSIM=0.74）、AHE（PSNR=23.1, SSIM=0.79）。

Experiments: 在DRIVE数据集上，所提方法在PSNR、SSIM、CNR三项指标上均优于HE和AHE基线。消融实验未说明。讨论了局限性并指出未来可与深度学习方法对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 仅使用40张图像的小规模数据集，泛化性存疑；3. 传统图像处理方法，未涉及深度学习；4. 无目标检测任务，无轻量化设计；5. 摘要未说明在真实临床环境中的鲁棒性测试。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能间接参考的点：CLAHE作为一种经典图像增强方法，在遥感图像预处理中也有应用（如遥感图像对比度增强），但本文未涉及遥感场景，且方法过于基础，对在轨目标检测轻量化课题无直接帮助。

Abstract: Background: Retinal fundus imaging is central to the early diagnosis of sight-threatening conditions including diabetic retinopathy, glaucoma, and retinal vein occlusion. Clinical utility of fundus images is routinely compromised by non-uniform illumination, motion blur, and low contrast - artefacts that increase the risk of diagnostic error. Effective image enhancement is therefore a prerequisite for reliable computer-aided ophthalmic diagnosis. Methods: This study proposes a two-stage image enhancement pipeline combining luminosity correction via HSV colour space decomposition with Contrast Limited Adaptive Histogram Equalization (CLAHE) applied exclusively to the Value (V) channel. Experiments are conducted on the publicly available DRIVE dataset (40 retinal fundus images, 584 x 565 pixels, Canon CR5 camera, ophthalmologist-annotated ground truth). Quantitative evaluation employs Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR), Structural Similarity Index (SSIM), and Contrast-to-Noise Ratio (CNR). Baseline comparisons include standard Histogram Equalization (HE) and Adaptive Histogram Equalization (AHE). A binary masking step is subsequently applied to isolate hyper-reflective regions consistent with vascular pathology. Results: The proposed method achieves PSNR = 29.3 dB, SSIM = 0.91, and CNR = 3.12 - outperforming HE (PSNR = 21.4 dB, SSIM = 0.74) and AHE (PSNR = 23.1 dB, SSIM = 0.79) across all metrics, with an average processing time of 0.14 seconds per image. Conclusions: The combined luminosity-CLAHE pipeline yields measurably superior contrast and structural fidelity compared to established baseline methods, with processing speed compatible with clinical screening workflows. Limitations and directions for deep-learning-based comparison are discussed.

</details>
